## LY.ILL.EL.35 看鬼临何卦何爻发动，知犯何神何煞：八宫方位（坎北水口、离南灶前、艮山林东北、坤坟墓西南、乾西北兑西天曹寺观、巽东南、震东伐树起造）；爻位动工（六造墙作墓、五砌路修街、四门栏檐下、三房内桥梁、二修厨作灶、初穿井开沟）；世为本宅兴修、应为对门垦掘

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看鬼落在哪一卦、哪一爻发动，就知道在那里冒犯了什么神、什么煞，因而牵连上身：官鬼在坎位，是北方来侵，或是兴动水口；鬼到离宫，是南向的妨碍，或是在灶前动工；艮主山林，或是遭遇东北；坤成坟墓，或是值西南；乾是西北，兑是西方，或是冒犯天曹、修寺观；巽是东南；震是东方，或是砍伐树林并起造。六爻的鬼，必是造墙作墓；五爻的官，必是砌路修街；四爻，断门栏，或是在檐下动工；三爻，推房内，或是动犯桥梁；二爻，是修厨房、作灶；初爻，是穿井、开沟；鬼在世爻上，是本宅动了工；在应爻上，是对门在挖掘；临门的爻，是开窗不便；变出兄弟，是挖坑、建造不通。注说：以上说的，都是把上面的鬼逐一说明白，不必再注。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Look at which trigram and which line the ghost moves in, to know where a god or a killing was offended and thereby brought on the body: the Pressure line in the Kan place means encroachment from the north, or works at a water mouth; a ghost at the Li palace means obstruction facing south, or works in front of the stove; Gen governs mountain and forest, or meets the northeast; Kun makes graves, or falls southwest; Qian is northwest and Dui is west, or an offence against the Heavenly Bureau in repairing a temple or abbey; Xun is southeast; Zhen is east, or felling woods and building. A ghost on the sixth line surely means building a wall or making a grave; the Pressure line on the fifth surely means paving a road or repairing a street; the fourth, judge gates and railings, or works under the eaves; the third, work out the inside of the house, or works offending bridges and beams; the second is repairing the kitchen or making a stove; the first is digging a well or opening a ditch; on the Self line, works on one's own house; on the Other line, digging opposite the door; on the door line, opening a window is inconvenient; changing into Peer, a pit or structure is blocked. The commentary says: all of the above sets the ghosts out one by one, and there is no need for further notes.

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1382 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1382*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 看鬼临何卦何爻而发动，知那处犯神犯煞以干连。 | 看鬼落在哪一卦、哪一爻发动，就知道在那里冒犯了什么神、什么煞，因而牵连上身。 | Look at which trigram and which line the ghost moves in, to know where a god or a killing was offended and thereby brought on the body. |
| 官居坎位北方侵，或兴水口。 | 官鬼在坎位，是北方来侵，或是兴动水口。 | The Pressure line in the Kan place means encroachment from the north, or works at a water mouth. |
| 鬼到离宫南向碍，或动灶前。 | 鬼到离宫，是南向的妨碍，或是在灶前动工。 | A ghost at the Li palace means obstruction facing south, or works in front of the stove. |
| 艮主山林或遭东北。 | 艮主山林，或是遭遇东北。 | Gen governs mountain and forest, or meets the northeast. |
| 坤成坟墓或值西南。 | 坤成坟墓，或是值西南。 | Kun makes graves, or falls southwest. |
| 乾为西北，兑为西、或犯天曹修寺观。 | 乾是西北，兑是西方，或是冒犯天曹、修寺观。 | Qian is northwest and Dui is west, or an offence against the Heavenly Bureau in repairing a temple or abbey. |
| 巽作东南。 | 巽是东南。 | Xun is southeast. |
| 震作东，或伐树林并起造。 | 震是东方，或是砍伐树林并起造。 | Zhen is east, or felling woods and building. |
| 六鬼必造墙作墓。 | 六爻的鬼，必是造墙作墓。 | A ghost on the sixth line surely means building a wall or making a grave. |
| 五官必砌路修街。 | 五爻的官，必是砌路修街。 | The Pressure line on the fifth surely means paving a road or repairing a street. |
| 四象断门栏、或兴工于檐下，三爻推房内、或动犯于桥梁。 | 四爻，断门栏，或是在檐下动工；三爻，推房内，或是动犯桥梁。 | The fourth, judge gates and railings, or works under the eaves; the third, work out the inside of the house, or works offending bridges and beams. |
| 二乃修厨作灶。 | 二爻，是修厨房、作灶。 | The second is repairing the kitchen or making a stove. |
| 初为穿井开沟。 | 初爻，是穿井、开沟。 | The first is digging a well or opening a ditch. |
| 在世则本宅兴修，在应则对门垦掘。 | 鬼在世爻上，是本宅动了工；在应爻上，是对门在挖掘。 | On the Self line, works on one's own house; on the Other line, digging opposite the door. |
| 临门爻，窗开不便。 | 临门的爻，是开窗不便。 | On the door line, opening a window is inconvenient. |
| 化兄弟，坑造不通。 | 变出兄弟，是挖坑、建造不通。 | Changing into Peer, a pit or structure is blocked. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1383 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1383*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以上所言，皆论上鬼逐一开明，不必再注。 | 以上说的，都是把上面的鬼逐一说明白，不必再注。 | All of the above sets the ghosts out one by one, and there is no need for further notes. |

> **按**:这一节和黄金策「修造动土必是煞遇勾陈」（LY.ILL.HJC.100）是同一件事：病由动土修造、犯了土神引起，用鬼所在的卦位定方向，用鬼所在的爻位定动的是什么工（初爻穿井开沟、二爻修厨作灶、三爻房内桥梁、四爻门栏檐下、五爻砌路修街、六爻造墙作墓）。「官」在这里是官鬼的省写。「在世则本宅兴修，在应则对门垦掘」是说鬼落在世爻是自己家动工，落在应爻是对门人家动工。「临门爻」的「门」原文没有说是哪一爻，这里照字面译。「化兄弟，坑造不通」的「不通」读法没有把握。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This section is the same matter as the Huangjin Ce's 'works and breaking ground surely mean a killing meeting Hook Earth' (LY.ILL.HJC.100): the illness comes from breaking ground or building and offending an earth spirit, the direction is fixed by the palace the ghost is in, and what work was done is fixed by the line the ghost is on (the first line digging a well or ditch, the second repairing the kitchen or making a stove, the third the inside of the house or bridges, the fourth gates and eaves, the fifth paving a road or repairing a street, the sixth building a wall or making a grave). 'Guan' (official) is here short for the Pressure line. 'On the Self, works on one's own house; on the Other, digging opposite the door' means that with the ghost on the Self line the works are in one's own house, and on the Other line in the house across the door. The 'door' of 'the door line' is not said in the original to be any particular line, and is translated literally. The reading of 'is blocked' in 'changing into Peer, a pit or structure is blocked' is uncertain. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.EL.36、LY.ILL.HJC.100、LY.ILL.HJC.101、LY.ILL.YM.57、LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.EL.04

## LY.ILL.EL.36 按十二支断神鬼：亥天门张壬（祠山大帝）、子北斗河伯、丑牛触之魂土神、寅东岳虎伤鬼、卯东岳、辰土神龙王、巳火德星君蚕室蛇伤鬼、午金枪教主（五显灵官）马伤鬼、未伽蓝土地、申元帅将军伤司、酉佛象丧煞少女、戌土神犬伤鬼；外鬼带青龙三官大帝、内鬼带青龙水仙五圣；子鬼带玄武外为驿帝内为水伤

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:亥是天门，和张壬作扰害；带青龙的象，宜叩拜三元；子是北斗，加上河伯作为灾殃；加了玄武的爻，应当酬谢圣帝；丑，是被牛顶撞的魂；寅，是被虎伤的鬼；卯，要禳解东岳；辰，要酬谢龙王；巳，推为火德尊星、被蛇所伤的鬼；午，断为金枪教主、被马踏的魂；未，叫伽蓝；申，说是元帅；酉，叫雌雄二煞；戌，遇上恶犬伤人。注说：亥鬼是张壬，就是祠山大帝；外鬼带青龙，是三官大帝；内鬼带青龙，是水仙五圣；子鬼是北斗、又是河伯水官，在外带玄武，是驿帝，在内带玄武，是水伤；丑鬼是土神，又是土伤之鬼；寅鬼是东岳，又是虎伤之鬼；卯鬼也是东岳；辰鬼是土神，又是龙王；巳鬼是火德星君和蚕室，又是蛇伤之鬼；午鬼是金枪教主，就是五显灵官，以及马伤之鬼；未鬼是土神，又是伽蓝土地；申鬼是元帅将军的职司和伤司，又有寺观中所冒犯的神；酉鬼是佛象和丧煞，又是少女；戌鬼是土神，又是犬伤之鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Hai is the Heavenly Gate and the harm done by Zhang Ren; with the image of the Azure Dragon, it is proper to appeal to the Three Primes; Zi is the Northern Dipper together with the River Count, as calamity; a line with the Dark Tortoise added should make offering to the Holy Emperor; Chou is the soul gored by an ox; Yin is the ghost wounded by a tiger; Mao: exorcise the Eastern Peak; Chen: thank the Dragon King; Si: work out the honoured Fire Virtue star and the ghost wounded by a snake; Wu: judge the Golden Spear Master and the soul trampled by a horse; Wei is called the Sangharama; Shen is said to be the Marshal; You is named the two killings, male and female; Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person. The commentary says: the Hai ghost is Zhang Ren, that is, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain; an outer ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors; an inner ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Water Immortal and the Five Sages; a Zi ghost is the Northern Dipper and also the River Count and Water Official, and with the Dark Tortoise outside it is the Post Emperor, and inside it is the water injury; a Chou ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by earth; a Yin ghost is the Eastern Peak and also a ghost wounded by a tiger; a Mao ghost is also the Eastern Peak; a Chen ghost is an earth spirit and also the Dragon King; a Si ghost is the Fire Virtue Star Lord and the silkworm house, and also a ghost wounded by a snake; a Wu ghost is the Golden Spear Master, that is, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, and a ghost wounded by a horse; a Wei ghost is an earth spirit and also the Sangharama Earth God; a Shen ghost is the office of marshals and generals and the Injuring Office, and also a god offended in a temple or abbey; a You ghost is a Buddha image and the Mourning Killing, and also a young woman; a Xu ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by a dog.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1384 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1384*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 亥作天门及张壬之扰害。 | 亥是天门，和张壬作扰害； | Hai is the Heavenly Gate and the harm done by Zhang Ren; |
| 带青龙之象，宜叩三元。 | 带青龙的象，宜叩拜三元； | with the image of the Azure Dragon, it is proper to appeal to the Three Primes; |
| 子为北斗兼河伯以为殃。 | 子是北斗，加上河伯作为灾殃； | Zi is the Northern Dipper together with the River Count, as calamity; |
| 加玄武之爻当酬圣帝。 | 加了玄武的爻，应当酬谢圣帝； | a line with the Dark Tortoise added should make offering to the Holy Emperor; |
| 丑言牛触之魂， | 丑，是被牛顶撞的魂； | Chou is the soul gored by an ox; |
| 寅是虎伤之鬼， | 寅，是被虎伤的鬼； | Yin is the ghost wounded by a tiger; |
| 卯禳东岳， | 卯，要禳解东岳； | Mao: exorcise the Eastern Peak; |
| 辰谢龙王， | 辰，要酬谢龙王； | Chen: thank the Dragon King; |
| 巳推火德尊星、蛇伤之鬼， | 巳，推为火德尊星、被蛇所伤的鬼； | Si: work out the honoured Fire Virtue star and the ghost wounded by a snake; |
| 午断金枪教主、马踏之魂， | 午，断为金枪教主、被马踏的魂； | Wu: judge the Golden Spear Master and the soul trampled by a horse; |
| 未曰伽蓝， | 未，叫伽蓝； | Wei is called the Sangharama; |
| 申云元帅， | 申，说是元帅； | Shen is said to be the Marshal; |
| 酉命雌雄二煞， | 酉，叫雌雄二煞； | You is named the two killings, male and female; |
| 戌逢恶犬伤人 | 戌，遇上恶犬伤人。 | Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1384 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1384*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 亥鬼为张壬，即祠山大帝。 | 亥鬼是张壬，就是祠山大帝。 | The Hai ghost is Zhang Ren, that is, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain. |
| 外鬼带青龙，为三官大帝； | 外鬼带青龙，是三官大帝； | An outer ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors; |
| 内鬼带青龙，为水仙五圣。 | 内鬼带青龙，是水仙五圣。 | an inner ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Water Immortal and the Five Sages. |
| 子鬼为北斗、又为河伯水官，在外带玄武，为驿帝，在内带玄武，为水伤。 | 子鬼是北斗、又是河伯水官，在外带玄武，是驿帝，在内带玄武，是水伤。 | A Zi ghost is the Northern Dipper and also the River Count and Water Official, and with the Dark Tortoise outside it is the Post Emperor, and inside it is the water injury. |
| 丑鬼为土神，又为土伤之鬼。 | 丑鬼是土神，又是土伤之鬼。 | A Chou ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by earth. |
| 寅鬼为东岳、又为虎伤之鬼。 | 寅鬼是东岳，又是虎伤之鬼。 | A Yin ghost is the Eastern Peak and also a ghost wounded by a tiger. |
| 卯鬼亦为东岳，辰鬼土神、又为龙王。 | 卯鬼也是东岳；辰鬼是土神，又是龙王。 | A Mao ghost is also the Eastern Peak; a Chen ghost is an earth spirit and also the Dragon King. |
| 巳鬼为火德星君并蚕室、又为蛇伤之鬼。 | 巳鬼是火德星君和蚕室，又是蛇伤之鬼。 | A Si ghost is the Fire Virtue Star Lord and the silkworm house, and also a ghost wounded by a snake. |
| 午鬼为金枪教主，即五显灵官、并马伤之鬼。 | 午鬼是金枪教主，就是五显灵官，以及马伤之鬼。 | A Wu ghost is the Golden Spear Master, that is, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, and a ghost wounded by a horse. |
| 未鬼为土神，又为伽蓝土地，申鬼为元帅将军之职并伤司，又有寺观中所犯之神。 | 未鬼是土神，又是伽蓝土地；申鬼是元帅将军的职司和伤司，又有寺观中所冒犯的神。 | A Wei ghost is an earth spirit and also the Sangharama Earth God; a Shen ghost is the office of marshals and generals and the Injuring Office, and also a god offended in a temple or abbey. |
| 酉鬼为佛象并丧煞、又为少女。 | 酉鬼是佛象和丧煞，又是少女。 | A You ghost is a Buddha image and the Mourning Killing, and also a young woman. |
| 戌鬼为土神，又为犬伤之鬼。 | 戌鬼是土神，又是犬伤之鬼。 | A Xu ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by a dog. |

> **按**:这一节前半是歌诀（到「戌逢恶犬伤人」），后半是解说（从「亥鬼为张壬」起）；原文两半接在同一行里没有断开，这里按内容分成两段引文。「亥鬼为张壬」一句在原文里紧接「戌逢恶犬伤人」，是解说的第一句。十二支各配一位或几位神鬼，大体的路数和易冒「按十二支断神鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.61）一致：亥子是水神（天门、北斗、河伯），寅卯是东岳，巳午是火德、金枪教主（五显），辰戌丑未是土神，申酉是元帅、佛象；又和黄金策旧注的「刑不善终」按地支分（LY.ILL.HJC.103：辰压死、午汤火烫、酉刀箭、亥落水、寅虎噬、巳蛇伤）相通：寅虎伤、巳蛇伤、午马伤、戌犬伤都是各支所属的动物。「驿帝」「水伤」「元帅将军之职并伤司」按字面译。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** The first half of this section is a verse (down to 'Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person') and the second half explanation (from 'the Hai ghost is Zhang Ren'); in the original the two halves run on in the same line without a break, and they are made into two quotations here by content. The sentence 'the Hai ghost is Zhang Ren' follows 'Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person' directly in the original and is the first sentence of the explanation. Each of the twelve branches is paired with one or several gods and ghosts, in the same general way as the Yi Mao's 'reading ghosts and spirits by the twelve branches' (LY.ILL.YM.61): Hai and Zi are water gods (the Heavenly Gate, the Northern Dipper, the River Count), Yin and Mao are the Eastern Peak, Si and Wu are the Fire Virtue and the Golden Spear Master (the Five Manifest), Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei are earth spirits, and Shen and You are the Marshal and the Buddha image; it also links with the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce on 'not dying a good death' by branch (LY.ILL.HJC.103: Chen crushed to death, Wu scalded or burned, You blade and arrow, Hai drowned, Yin bitten by a tiger, Si bitten by a snake): Yin the tiger, Si the snake, Wu the horse and Xu the dog are all the animals belonging to each branch. 'The Post Emperor', 'the water injury' and 'the office of marshals and generals and the Injuring Office' are translated literally. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.35、LY.ILL.EL.37、LY.ILL.YM.61、LY.ILL.HJC.103、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.EL.37 按爻位断神鬼：六爻上仓至圣坟墓之神（素原、坟墓土神、远方之鬼）、五爻中界至尊东岳（栏路五圣五路大神倒路之鬼）、四爻大小伤神右仪门将军（门前鬼）、三爻家堂郡王府城隍（床婆弟兄鬼房内鬼桥上鬼）、二爻县城隍灶神（夫妻鬼厨下鬼）、初爻土地井泉童子（井前之鬼、无井即土鬼）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:六爻，是上仓的至圣、坟墓的神；五爻，是中界的至尊、路途的鬼；四爻，推为檐外伤、朝门前的魍魉；三爻断，鬼在六爻，是上苍的素原、以及坟墓土神、或远方的鬼；鬼在五爻，是中界至尊，就是东岳，又是栏路五圣、五路大神、或倒在路上的鬼；鬼在四爻，是大小伤神、以及右仪门的将军、又是门前的鬼；鬼在三爻，是家堂和郡王，就是府城隍，又是床婆、弟兄的鬼，以及房内的鬼、桥上的鬼；鬼在二爻，是县城隍和灶神，又是夫妻等的鬼、厨下的鬼；鬼在初爻，是土地和井泉童子、以及井前的鬼，如果没有井，就是土鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The sixth line is the supreme sage of the upper granary and the god of graves; the fifth is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm and the ghost of the road; the fourth is worked out as an injury beyond the eaves and the goblins before the court gate; the third judge: a ghost on the sixth line is the plain vow of the upper heaven, the earth god of graves, or a ghost from a far place; a ghost on the fifth line is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm, that is, the Eastern Peak, and also the Five Sages of the barrier road, the Great God of the Five Roads, or a ghost fallen on the road; a ghost on the fourth line is the greater and lesser Injuring gods, and the general at the right ceremonial gate, and also a ghost before the door; a ghost on the third line is the household shrine and the Prince of the Commandery, that is, the City God of the prefecture, and also the Bed Mother and a ghost of brothers, and a ghost within the room and a ghost on the bridge; a ghost on the second line is the City God of the county and the Kitchen God, and also a ghost of husband and wife and a ghost beneath the kitchen; a ghost on the first line is the Earth God and the Well-and-Spring Boy, and a ghost before the well, and if there is no well it is an earth ghost.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1385 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1385*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六乃上仓至圣、坟墓之神。 | 六爻，是上仓的至圣、坟墓的神。 | The sixth line is the supreme sage of the upper granary and the god of graves. |
| 五为中界至尊、路途之鬼。 | 五爻，是中界的至尊、路途的鬼。 | The fifth is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm and the ghost of the road. |
| 四推檐外伤朝门前魍魉。 | 四爻，推为檐外伤、朝门前的魍魉。 | The fourth is worked out as an injury beyond the eaves and the goblins before the court gate. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1385 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1385*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 三断鬼在六爻，为上苍素原、并坟墓土神、或远方之鬼。 | 三爻断：鬼在六爻，是上苍的素原、以及坟墓土神、或远方的鬼。 | The third judge: a ghost on the sixth line is the plain vow of the upper heaven, the earth god of graves, or a ghost from a far place. |
| 鬼在五爻为中界至尊、即东岳也，又为栏路五圣、并五路大神、或倒路之鬼。 | 鬼在五爻，是中界至尊，就是东岳，又是栏路五圣、五路大神、或倒在路上的鬼。 | A ghost on the fifth line is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm, that is, the Eastern Peak, and also the Five Sages of the barrier road, the Great God of the Five Roads, or a ghost fallen on the road. |
| 鬼在四爻为大小伤神、并在右仪门将军、又为门前之鬼。 | 鬼在四爻，是大小伤神、以及右仪门的将军、又是门前的鬼。 | A ghost on the fourth line is the greater and lesser Injuring gods, and the general at the right ceremonial gate, and also a ghost before the door. |
| 鬼在三爻，为家堂并郡王，即府城隍，又为床婆弟兄鬼，及房内鬼、桥上鬼。 | 鬼在三爻，是家堂和郡王，就是府城隍，又是床婆、弟兄的鬼，以及房内的鬼、桥上的鬼。 | A ghost on the third line is the household shrine and the Prince of the Commandery, that is, the City God of the prefecture, and also the Bed Mother and a ghost of brothers, and a ghost within the room and a ghost on the bridge. |
| 鬼在二爻为县城隍并灶神，又为夫妻等鬼、厨下鬼。 | 鬼在二爻，是县城隍和灶神，又是夫妻等的鬼、厨下的鬼。 | A ghost on the second line is the City God of the county and the Kitchen God, and also a ghost of husband and wife and a ghost beneath the kitchen. |
| 鬼在初爻，为土地并井泉童子，并井前之鬼，如无井即土鬼。 | 鬼在初爻，是土地和井泉童子、以及井前的鬼，如果没有井，就是土鬼。 | A ghost on the first line is the Earth God and the Well-and-Spring Boy, and a ghost before the well, and if there is no well it is an earth ghost. |

> **按**:这一段歌诀只剩三句（六、五、四爻），第四句「三断」之后原文没有接歌诀，而是直接接解说「鬼在六爻」，所以「三断」这两个字看起来是歌诀残留，这里和解说的第一句连在一起引。第一位「上仓至圣」「素原」的「素原」是素食所许的愿（见 LY.ILL.HJC.93 后各条的「素愿」）。这一节的爻位配法和易冒「按爻位断神鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.58）、黄金策「六爻推鬼神」（LY.ILL.HJC.101）大体同路：初爻井、二爻灶、三爻家堂、五爻路、六爻坟墓；差别是易林补遗每爻多列了具体的神名和几种鬼。「床婆」是保护婴孩的床神。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** The verse of this passage is left with only three lines (the sixth, fifth and fourth lines), and after 'the third judge' the original does not go on with verse but goes straight into the explanation 'a ghost on the sixth line', so that 'the third judge' looks like a remnant of the verse and is quoted here joined to the first sentence of the explanation. 'Plain vow' is a vow made with vegetarian offering. The assignment by line position agrees in outline with the Yi Mao's 'reading ghosts and spirits by line position' (LY.ILL.YM.58) and the Huangjin Ce's 'reading ghosts and spirits by the six lines' (LY.ILL.HJC.101): the first line the well, the second the stove, the third the household shrine, the fifth the road, the sixth the graves; the difference is that the Yilin Buyi lists specific gods and several kinds of ghost for each line. The 'Bed Mother' is the bed goddess who protects infants. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.36、LY.ILL.EL.38、LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.HJC.101、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.EL.38 官临世上按爻位断神：鬼持世于初爻家堂作祸、二爻社坛作祟、三爻犯桥道之鬼或园中花木之精、四爻犯五道及师王佛实之灾、五爻无鬼不言、六爻山神为害及星宿降灾；鬼值空中莫断片殃之祟

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼临在世爻上，要注明六象的神；鬼落在空亡中，不要断作哪一份殃祟。注说：鬼在初爻持世，是家堂作祸；鬼在二爻持世，上气为实，并且是社坛作祟；鬼在三爻持世，是冒犯了桥道中的鬼，如果不是桥道，就是园中花木的精怪；鬼在四爻持世，是冒犯了五道，也有师王佛实的灾；鬼在五爻持世，原文说没有（可说的）不言；鬼在六爻持世，是山神为害，以及星宿降灾；六爻里鬼虽然持世，落空了就不说。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line on the Self line: note the spirits of the six images; a ghost in Void: do not judge a haunting of any part of calamity. The commentary says: a ghost on the first line holding the Self is the household shrine making trouble; a ghost on the second holding the Self, the upper qi is real, and the altar of the soil haunts; a ghost on the third holding the Self offends the ghost of a bridge or road, and if not a bridge or road, it is the spirit of garden flowers and trees; a ghost on the fourth holding the Self offends the Five Roads, and there is also calamity from teachers, kings and Buddhas (the text says 'shi'); with the fifth, if there is no ghost, nothing is said; a ghost on the sixth holding the Self is the Mountain God doing harm and stars descending calamity; though the ghost in the six lines holds the Self, if it is Void nothing is said.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1386 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1386*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官临世上，注开六象之神。 | 官鬼临在世爻上，要注明六象的神。 | The Pressure line on the Self line: note the spirits of the six images. |
| 鬼值空中，莫断片殃之祟。 | 鬼落在空亡中，不要断作哪一份殃祟。 | A ghost in Void: do not judge a haunting of any part of calamity. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1387–1389 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 1387–1389*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼值初爻持世，家堂作祸，鬼值二世，上气为实、并社坛作祟。 | 鬼在初爻持世，是家堂作祸；鬼在二爻持世，上气为实，并且是社坛作祟。 | A ghost on the first line holding the Self is the household shrine making trouble; a ghost on the second holding the Self, the upper qi is real, and the altar of the soil haunts. |
| 鬼值三世，犯桥道中之鬼，若非桥道，即是园中花木之精。 | 鬼在三爻持世，是冒犯了桥道中的鬼，如果不是桥道，就是园中花木的精怪。 | A ghost on the third line holding the Self offends the ghost of a bridge or road, and if not a bridge or road, it is the spirit of garden flowers and trees. |
| 鬼值四世，犯五道亦有师王佛实之灾。 | 鬼在四爻持世，是冒犯了五道，也有师王佛实的灾。 | A ghost on the fourth line holding the Self offends the Five Roads, and there is also calamity from teachers, kings and Buddhas (the text says 'shi'). |
| 五世无鬼不言。 | 五爻（持世），没有鬼就不说。 | With the fifth, if there is no ghost, nothing is said. |
| 鬼值六世，山神为害，及星宿降灾。 | 鬼在六爻持世，是山神为害，以及星宿降灾。 | A ghost on the sixth line holding the Self is the Mountain God doing harm and stars descending calamity. |
| 六爻内鬼虽持世，空则不言。 | 六爻里鬼虽然持世，落空了就不说。 | Though the ghost in the six lines holds the Self, if it is Void nothing is said. |

> **按**:这一节和易冒「世于初者，家神也，世于二者，土神也……」（LY.ILL.YM.58）是同一件事：鬼持世时，看世在哪一爻。易林补遗的配法：初家堂、二社坛、三桥道或园中花木之精、四五道及师王佛、五爻不言、六山神及星宿。「上气为实」「师王佛实」两处原文没有解释，读法没有把握，这里按字面译。「六象之神」指上一节（LY.ILL.EL.37）里六个爻位各配的神。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This section is the same matter as the Yi Mao's 'the Self on the first line is a household spirit, on the second an earth spirit...' (LY.ILL.YM.58): when the ghost holds the Self, look at which line the Self is on. The Yilin Buyi's assignments: the first the household shrine, the second the altar of the soil, the third a bridge or road or the spirit of garden flowers and trees, the fourth the Five Roads and teachers, kings and Buddhas, the fifth left unsaid, the sixth the Mountain God and the stars. 'The upper qi is real' and 'teachers, kings and Buddhas (shi)' are not explained in the original, the reading is uncertain, and they are translated literally. 'The spirits of the six images' means the gods paired with the six line positions in the previous section (LY.ILL.EL.37). Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.37、LY.ILL.EL.39、LY.ILL.YM.58、LY.ILL.HJC.101

## LY.ILL.EL.39 青龙临财、玄武临福化出官爻必是福神见咎（大则五福茶延、小则五圣五路）；朱雀临父母变出鬼爻必是先年许下之愿；居兑卦又犯天曹；判鬼神须审病源轻重，重则大神轻则小祟

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:青龙、财爻、子孙变出官爻，是福神来照应；兑宫、朱雀、文书变成鬼象，是先前许的愿在催。注说：青龙如果临财、玄武临福（子孙），变出官爻，必是福神见咎；福神，大的是五福和茶延，小的是五圣和五路；凡是判断鬼神，必须审察病源的轻重，重的就是大神，轻的就是小祟；再看朱雀临父母变出鬼爻，必是先年许下的愿；如果落在兑卦，又是冒犯了天曹。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output changing into a Pressure line: the fortune spirit shines on it; Dui, the Vermilion Bird and the writing image becoming a ghost image: the earlier vow presses. The commentary says: if the Azure Dragon sits on Wealth and the Dark Tortoise on Output (the fortune spirit) and it changes into a Pressure line, it is surely Output (the fortune spirit) showing fault; the fortune spirit, at the great end, is the Five Blessings and the Tea Feast, and at the small end the Five Sages and the Five Roads; in judging ghosts and spirits one must examine the lightness or weight of the source of the illness, the heavy being a great god and the light a small haunting; look further at the Vermilion Bird on the Resource line changing into a Pressure line, which is surely a vow made in a former year; if it sits in the Dui hexagram, it is again an offence against the Heavenly Bureau.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1390 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1390*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙财子化官爻，福神相照。 | 青龙、财爻、子孙变出官爻，是福神来照应； | The Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output changing into a Pressure line: Output (the fortune spirit) shines on it; |
| 兑雀文书之鬼象，前愿相催。 | 兑宫、朱雀、文书变成鬼象，是先前许的愿在催。 | Dui, the Vermilion Bird and the writing image becoming a ghost image: the earlier vow presses. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1391 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1391*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 青龙若临财武临福化出官爻，必是福神见咎。 | 青龙如果临财、玄武临福（子孙），变出官爻，必是福神见咎。 | If the Azure Dragon sits on Wealth and the Dark Tortoise on Output (the fortune spirit) and it changes into a Pressure line, it is surely Output (the fortune spirit) showing fault. |
| 福神者，大则五福及茶延，小则五圣及五路。 | 福神，大的是五福和茶延，小的是五圣和五路。 | The fortune spirit, at the great end, is the Five Blessings and the Tea Feast, and at the small end the Five Sages and the Five Roads (as Output). |
| 凡判鬼神，须审病源轻重，重则大神，轻则小祟。 | 凡是判断鬼神，必须审察病源的轻重，重的就是大神，轻的就是小祟。 | In judging ghosts and spirits one must examine the lightness or weight of the source of the illness, the heavy being a great god and the light a small haunting. |
| 复看朱雀临父母变出鬼爻，必是先年许下之愿。 | 再看朱雀临父母变出鬼爻，必是先年许下的愿。 | Look further at the Vermilion Bird on the Resource line changing into a Pressure line, which is surely a vow made in a former year. |
| 若居兑卦，又犯天曹。 | 如果落在兑卦，又是冒犯了天曹。 | If it sits in the Dui hexagram, it is again an offence against the Heavenly Bureau. |

> **按**:「福神」在这里是子孙（福神就是子孙）。「福神见咎」是本来带福的六亲（青龙财、玄武福）变出官爻，成了作祟的鬼——所以是福神要求还愿，而不是凶鬼。「大则五福及茶延，小则五圣及五路」是说福神的大小：大神（五福、茶延）和小神（五圣、五路）；判断时按病源轻重取大神或小祟。「先年许下之愿」是以前许过没还的愿，朱雀是口舌，父母是文书誓约，所以是口头许过的愿；与 LY.ILL.EL.35 前一节不同，与易隐鬼崇·旧愿（LY.ILL.YY.32）、黄金策「鬼持世是旧愿未酬」（LY.ILL.HJC.110）是同一件事。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** 'Fortune spirit' here is Output (the fortune spirit is Output). 'The fortune spirit showing fault' is when a relation that would bring fortune (Wealth on the Azure Dragon, Output on the Dark Tortoise) changes into a Pressure line and becomes a haunting ghost — so it is the fortune spirit demanding the redemption of a vow, and not an evil ghost. 'At the great end the Five Blessings and the Tea Feast, at the small end the Five Sages and the Five Roads' speaks of the size of the fortune spirit: great gods (the Five Blessings, the Tea Feast) and small gods (the Five Sages, the Five Roads); in judging one takes a great god or a small haunting by the weight of the source of the illness. 'A vow made in a former year' is an earlier vow made and not redeemed: the Vermilion Bird is talk, and the Resource line is documents and oaths, so it is a vow spoken aloud; it is the same matter as the Yi Yin's 'ghosts and hauntings: old vows' (LY.ILL.YY.32) and the Huangjin Ce's 'a ghost holding the Self is an old vow unpaid' (LY.ILL.HJC.110). Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.38、LY.ILL.EL.40、LY.ILL.YY.32、LY.ILL.HJC.110、LY.ILL.HJC.109

## LY.ILL.EL.40 六亲内外动断是谁的鬼：父母内动本宗祠堂宗相、外动外族高亲；财内动先亡妻妾（妻魂妾魄）、外动奴仆或情人；兄动手足相知之辈（昆弟姨妹朋友）；福动儿孙之鬼、僧道之灵（儿女侄婿）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻在内发动，才断祠堂里的宗相；父母爻在外发动，可以说是外族的高亲；财爻在内宫动，必是妻子的魂、妾的魄；妻财在外卦摇动，不是奴仆就是情人；兄弟发动，是手足之亲、相知的人；子孙发动，是儿孙的鬼，僧道的灵。注说：父爻在内卦以及在亲宫动的，就说是本宗的尊长；父爻居外卦以及在他宫动的，就说是外姓的尊亲；财爻临内卦或在亲宫动的，必是先亡的妻妾；财爻在他宫以及外卦动的，如果不是奴仆，必是恩爱的情人；兄弟动的，或是兄弟、或是姨妹、或是朋友的魂；子孙动的，是儿女、是侄、是女婿，又是僧道的魂。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Resource line moving inside is judged the ancestral image of the ancestral hall; the Resource line moving outside may be called a high relative of another clan; Wealth moving in the inner palace is surely a wife's spirit and a concubine's soul; Wealth stirring in the outer trigram is either a servant or a lover; Peer thriving is a kin of hand and foot, an intimate; Output moving is the ghost of sons and grandsons, the spirit of a monk or Daoist. The commentary says: the Resource line moving in the inner trigram or in a kin palace is called an elder of one's own line; the Resource line in the outer trigram or moving in another palace is a revered kinsman of another surname; Wealth in the inner trigram or moving in a kin palace is surely a wife or concubine who died earlier; Wealth in another palace or moving in the outer trigram, if not a servant, is surely a loving sweetheart; when Peer moves, it is the spirit of a brother, or an aunt or sister, or a friend; when Output moves, it is a son or daughter, a nephew, a son-in-law, and also the spirit of a monk or Daoist.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1392 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1392*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母内兴，方断祠堂之宗相； | 父母爻在内发动，才断祠堂里的宗相； | The Resource line moving inside is judged the ancestral image of the ancestral hall; |
| 椿萱外动，可言外族之高亲。 | 父母爻在外发动，可以说是外族的高亲； | the Resource line moving outside may be called a high relative of another clan; |
| 财动内宫，必妻魂而妾魄； | 财爻在内宫动，必是妻子的魂、妾的魄； | Wealth moving in the inner palace is surely a wife's spirit and a concubine's soul; |
| 妻摇外卦，非奴仆即情人。 | 妻财在外卦摇动，不是奴仆就是情人； | Wealth stirring in the outer trigram is either a servant or a lover; |
| 兄兴为手足之亲、相知之辈； | 兄弟发动，是手足之亲、相知的人； | Peer thriving is a kin of hand and foot, an intimate; |
| 福动乃儿孙之鬼，僧道之灵。 | 子孙发动，是儿孙的鬼，僧道的灵。 | Output moving is the ghost of sons and grandsons, the spirit of a monk or Daoist. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1393 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1393*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父在内卦及亲宫动者，便曰本宗之尊长。 | 父爻在内卦以及在亲宫动的，就说是本宗的尊长。 | The Resource line moving in the inner trigram or in a kin palace is called an elder of one's own line. |
| 父居外卦及他宫动者，乃言外姓之尊亲。 | 父爻居外卦以及在他宫动的，就说是外姓的尊亲。 | The Resource line in the outer trigram or moving in another palace is a revered kinsman of another surname. |
| 财临内卦或亲宫动者，必先亡之妻妾。 | 财爻临内卦或在亲宫动的，必是先亡的妻妾。 | Wealth in the inner trigram or moving in a kin palace is surely a wife or concubine who died earlier. |
| 财值他宫及外卦动者，若非奴仆，必恩爱之情人。 | 财爻在他宫以及外卦动的，如果不是奴仆，必是恩爱的情人。 | Wealth in another palace or moving in the outer trigram, if not a servant, is surely a loving sweetheart. |
| 兄弟动者，或昆弟或姨妹或朋友之魂。 | 兄弟动的，或是兄弟、或是姨妹、或是朋友的魂。 | When Peer moves, it is the spirit of a brother, or an aunt or sister, or a friend. |
| 子孙动者，为儿女为侄为婿，又为僧道之魂。 | 子孙动的，是儿女、是侄、是女婿，又是僧道的魂。 | When Output moves, it is a son or daughter, a nephew, a son-in-law, and also the spirit of a monk or Daoist. |

> **按**:这一节和易冒「按六亲断神鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.60）、黄金策「以卦身推是什么人的鬼」和「鬼动化出六亲即以化出者断」（LY.ILL.HJC.102、HJC.108）是同一件事。易林补遗比易冒多了一个内外的分法：父母爻在内卦、亲宫是本宗的，在外卦、他宫是外姓的；财爻在内是先亡的妻妾，在外是奴仆或情人。「亲宫」是本宫（与卦同宫的爻所在的宫）。「椿萱」是父母的雅称，原文这里用在歌诀里。
>
> **Note:** This section is the same matter as the Yi Mao's 'reading ghosts and spirits by the six relations' (LY.ILL.YM.60) and the Huangjin Ce's 'reading whose ghost it is from the Body' and 'a ghost moving into a relation is judged by what it turns into' (LY.ILL.HJC.102, HJC.108). The Yilin Buyi adds a division into inner and outer beyond the Yi Mao: the Resource line in the inner trigram or a kin palace is of one's own line, and in the outer trigram or another palace of another surname; Wealth inside is a wife or concubine who died earlier, and outside a servant or a lover. A 'kin palace' is the home palace (the palace to which the hexagram belongs). 'Chun-xuan' is a polite name for parents and is used here in the verse.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.39、LY.ILL.EL.41、LY.ILL.YM.60、LY.ILL.HJC.102、LY.ILL.HJC.108、LY.ILL.YY.30

## LY.ILL.EL.41 卦有动爻病有鬼魅：一爻动一魂、两爻动两鬼、三爻动三魂、四爻动四鬼；定阴阳（重单为男、交折为女）；鬼食荤素（子孙及青龙爻动宜素祭、其余爻动荤素皆合）；定方向（子爻动正北方鬼、丑爻动东北方鬼，卯东酉西）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:卦里有动爻，病里就有鬼魅：比如三爻动，就是三个魂，四个象兴动就是四个鬼。要定阴阳，重、单是男，交、折是女；要分方向，卯动是东方，酉动是西方。注说：凡是推儿官，专看动爻，一爻动就是一个魂，两爻动就是两个鬼；想分男女，要看阴阳，重的是男，交的是女；想知道鬼吃荤还是吃素，只有子孙和青龙爻动的，都宜用素祭，其余的爻动，一概荤素都合；想知道鬼在哪个方位，就看动在哪一象：比如子爻动，是正北方的鬼；丑爻动，是东北方的鬼；其余照这样推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Where the hexagram has moving lines, the illness has ghosts and sprites: for example, if the third line moves, there are three souls, and if four images stir, four ghosts. To fix yin and yang, the double and the single are male, the crossed and the broken are female; to divide the direction, a moving Mao is east and a moving You is west. The commentary says: in working out a child-official, look only at the moving lines: one line moving is one soul, two lines moving are two ghosts; to divide male and female, look at yin and yang, the double being male and the crossed female; to know whether the ghost eats meat or vegetables, only when Output or the Azure Dragon line moves is a vegetarian offering suitable, and when any other line moves, both meat and vegetarian suit; to know in which direction the ghost is, look at which image it moves in: for example, a moving Zi line is a ghost of the due north, and a moving Chou line a ghost of the northeast; the rest follow this.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1394 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74: Searching out ghosts and spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1394*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦有动爻，病有鬼魅。 | 卦里有动爻，病里就有鬼魅。 | Where the hexagram has moving lines, the illness has ghosts and sprites. |
| 且如三爻动，则三魂，四象兴而四鬼。 | 比如三爻动，就是三个魂，四个象兴动就是四个鬼。 | For example, if the third line moves, there are three souls, and if four images stir, four ghosts. |
| 若定阴阳，重单是男交折女。 | 要定阴阳，重、单是男，交、折是女； | To fix yin and yang, the double and the single are male, the crossed and the broken are female; |
| 如分方向，卯动东方酉动西。 | 要分方向，卯动是东方，酉动是西方。 | to divide the direction, a moving Mao is east and a moving You is west. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1395 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1395*

| 两爻动，则两鬼。 | 两爻动，就是两个鬼。 | Two lines moving are two ghosts. |
| 欲分男女，须看阴阳。 | 想分男女，要看阴阳。 | To divide male and female, look at yin and yang. |
| 重则为男交则为女。 | 重的是男，交的是女。 | The double is male and the crossed is female. |
| 要知鬼食荤素，惟有子孙及青龙爻动者，皆宜素祭，其余爻动，概合荤素。 | 想知道鬼吃荤还是吃素，只有子孙和青龙爻动的，都宜用素祭，其余的爻动，一概荤素都合。 | To know whether the ghost eats meat or vegetables, only when Output or the Azure Dragon line moves is a vegetarian offering suitable, and when any other line moves, both meat and vegetarian suit. |
| 要知鬼在何方，却看动临何象。 | 想知道鬼在哪个方位，就看动在哪一象。 | To know in which direction the ghost is, look at which image it moves in. |
| 且如子爻动，正北方之鬼，丑爻动，东北方之鬼，其余仿此。 | 比如子爻动，是正北方的鬼；丑爻动，是东北方的鬼；其余照这样推。 | For example, a moving Zi line is a ghost of the due north, and a moving Chou line a ghost of the northeast; the rest follow this. |

> **按**:「重、单、交、折」是摇卦时四种爻的名称：重（老阳）、单（少阳）是阳，交（老阴）、折（少阴）是阴，所以歌诀说重、单是男，交、折是女；解说只说「重则为男，交则为女」，是说动的爻（重是动的阳、交是动的阴）。「儿官」原文没有解释，疑是「鬼」的别称（「官」是官鬼），这里按字面译作「child-official」并保留；「一爻动则一魂」是数动爻的条数当作魂数，这和易冒「以动爻当鬼、一动一鬼、三动三鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.54）是同一件事。「子孙及青龙爻动者宜素祭」是说这类鬼（子孙、青龙）偏于善神，用素祭。
>
> **Note:** 'Double, single, crossed, broken' are the names of the four kinds of line in casting: the double (old yang) and the single (young yang) are yang, and the crossed (old yin) and the broken (young yin) are yin, so the verse says the double and the single are male and the crossed and the broken female; the explanation says only 'the double is male, the crossed female', speaking of the moving lines (the double being a moving yang and the crossed a moving yin). 'Child-official' (er guan) is not explained in the original and looks like another name for the ghost ('guan' being the Pressure line); it is translated literally and kept. 'One line moving is one soul' counts the number of moving lines as the number of souls, the same matter as the Yi Mao's 'take the moving lines as the ghosts, one moving one ghost, three moving three ghosts' (LY.ILL.YM.54). 'Output or the Azure Dragon line moving suits a vegetarian offering' means that these kinds of ghost (Output and the Azure Dragon) lean toward benevolent spirits, and take a vegetarian offering.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.40、LY.ILL.EL.42、LY.ILL.YM.54、LY.ILL.YM.55

---

## LY.ILL.EL.43 搜决神鬼章结语：细查何象临官便议何神作祟；请祷则福无不至、祈禳则祸无不消；作此篇为却灾度命，恐人妄断而好杀费财，万不可轻言，须将六兽配五行细论方判无私

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:仔细查看是哪个象临在官鬼上，才议论是哪位神在作祟，这样请祷就没有福不到的，祈禳就没有祸不消的。现在作这一篇，恳切是为了却灾、度人性命，又恐怕人乱判断，常常担心人好杀生而白费钱财，万万不可轻易说，一样一样都要细细论，把六兽端正地配上五行来决断诸神，终究没有一个错误，详审它的确实，才判得没有私心。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Examine carefully which image sits on the Pressure line, and only then discuss which god is haunting; so prayer brings blessing everywhere and exorcism removes calamity everywhere. This chapter is written earnestly to ward off calamity and save lives, and for fear that people judge at random, always worrying that they are fond of killing and waste money, one must by no means speak lightly; each point must be discussed in detail, matching the six beasts correctly with the five phases to decide the spirits, so that in the end there is not one error, and its certainty is examined carefully, and only then is the judgment without partiality.

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1398 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1398*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 细查何象临官，便议何神作祟，请祷则福无不至，祈禳则祸无不消，今作此篇切为却灾而度命，恐人妄断，恒忧好杀而费财，万不可轻言，一一还须细论，将六兽端配五行决诸神，终无一误详其的确，方判无私。 | 仔细查看是哪个象临在官鬼上，才议论是哪位神在作祟，这样请祷就没有福不到的，祈禳就没有祸不消的；现在作这一篇，恳切是为了却灾、度人性命，又恐怕人乱判断，常常担心人好杀生而白费钱财，万万不可轻易说，一样一样都要细细论，把六兽端正地配上五行来决断诸神，终究没有一个错误，详审它的确实，才判得没有私心。 | Examine carefully which image sits on the Pressure line, and only then discuss which god is haunting; so prayer brings blessing everywhere and exorcism removes calamity everywhere; this chapter is written earnestly to ward off calamity and save lives, and for fear that people judge at random, always worrying that they are fond of killing and waste money, one must by no means speak lightly; each point must be discussed in detail, matching the six beasts correctly with the five phases to decide the spirits, so that in the end there is not one error, and its certainty is examined carefully, and only then is the judgment without partiality. |

> **打架**:要不要指到某一位神、祭祀要不要杀牲：易林补遗在本章前面列了大量神名，这里的收束是「恐人妄断，恒忧好杀而费财，万不可轻言」，一样一样细论、把六兽配五行才可判，而它随后的「解禳通用法」（LY.ILL.EL.54）更是劝人不要杀牲、多用斋戒念经；易冒也在结尾说「必实指鬼神而泥之则诬矣」，并且「祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身」（LY.ILL.YM.63、YM.64）；增删卜易的李我平批评指定神名（LY.ILL.ZS.25），野鹤反对杀牲救命（LY.ILL.ZS.25）。这几家在收束上意见接近。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to pin it to a particular god, and whether sacrifice should involve slaughter: the Yilin Buyi lists a great many names of gods earlier in the chapter, but its closing here is 'fearing people will judge at random, always worrying that they love killing and waste money, one must by no means speak lightly', so that each point must be discussed in detail and the six beasts matched with the five phases before one may judge, and its following 'general methods of exorcism' (LY.ILL.EL.54) go further and urge people not to slaughter but to use fasting and the recitation of scriptures; the Yi Mao also closes with 'to point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim' and 'of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself' (LY.ILL.YM.63, YM.64); Li Woping of the Zengshan Buyi criticises pinning names (LY.ILL.ZS.25), and the Old Man of Yehe opposes slaughter to save a life (LY.ILL.ZS.25). These books are close in their closing views.

> **按**:这一句的文字有些含混：「今作此篇切为却灾而度命」的「切」是恳切；「恒忧好杀而费财」是担心世人因为鬼神之说而宰杀牲畜、花费家财；「将六兽端配五行决诸神」是说要把六神和五行按次序配起来去断哪位神，不可随意。「终无一误详其的确，方判无私」的「无私」是不偏私、不受情面左右。
>
> **Note:** The wording of this sentence is somewhat loose: the 'qie' in 'written earnestly (qie) to ward off calamity and save lives' is earnestness; 'always worrying that they love killing and waste money' is the worry that, because of talk of ghosts and spirits, people will slaughter animals and spend their household wealth; 'matching the six beasts correctly with the five phases to decide the spirits' means matching the six spirits with the five phases in order to decide which god, not at will. 'Without partiality' in 'only then is the judgment without partiality' means impartial and not swayed by favour.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.42、LY.ILL.EL.44、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.YM.64、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.EL.44 总论八宫值鬼诀(上)：鬼发乾宫是西北寺观庙内神司、旧许天灯香愿经卷斗斋、已故父辈老魂、头风鬼；坎宫是北方水路神司、家内中男、耳聋溺死之魂，坎在外三爻又为北斗、加玄武作玄天上帝；艮宫是东北土神五圣山神土地、少男、臂疮手折指烂鼻鬼；震宫是东方九天（雷祀大帝）、三茅东岳树神、长男、杖伤折足驾舟鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼发动，看它落在哪一宫，就知道是哪一路的神鬼。乾宫：西北方来，是误犯了寺观或庙里的神司，或者旧时许下的天灯、香愿、经卷、斗斋这一类愿，是已经去世的父辈和年老过世的人的魂，是头风的鬼。坎宫：祸在北方，是水路的神司，还有家里的中男在作耗，也有耳聋的鬼、溺死的魂；坎在外三爻时又是北斗，再加玄武也当作玄天上帝。艮宫：东北方来，是冒犯了土神、五圣、山神、土地，也有家里的少男，以及臂上生疮、手折、指烂、烂鼻的鬼。震宫：祸在东方，宜向九天（雷祀大帝）叩祷，又犯三茅真君、东岳尊神和树头的神灵，也有家里的长男作祟，或是天嗔，或是被打死兼折足、驾舟的鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Pressure line moves, look at which palace it falls in, to know which kind of god or ghost it is. Qian: it comes from the northwest, and one has wrongly offended a temple or abbey or the gods and offices within a temple, or an old vow of a heaven lamp, incense, scriptures or a Dipper fast; it is the soul of a departed father or of an aged person who has died, the ghost of head-wind (chronic headache). Kan: the misfortune lies in the north; it is the gods and offices of the waterways, with the middle son of the household making trouble, and also ghosts of the deaf and souls of the drowned; when Kan is in the outer three lines it is also the Northern Dipper, and with the Dark Tortoise added it is also counted as the Supreme Emperor of the Mysterious Heaven. Gen: it comes from the northeast, offending the Earth spirit, the Five Sages, the Mountain God and the Earth God, and there is also the youngest son of the household and ghosts of arm sores, broken hands, rotten fingers and rotten noses. Zhen: the misfortune lies in the east, and it is fitting to pray to the Nine Heavens (the Great Emperor of the Thunder Rites); it also offends the True Lord of the Three Mao, the honoured god of the Eastern Peak and the sacred spirits of the treetops, and there is the eldest son as haunting, or heaven's wrath, or ghosts beaten to death, with broken legs, or who piloted boats.

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 震宫鬼动， | 震宫鬼动， | When the ghost moves in the Zhen palace, |
| 祸在东方， | 祸在东方， | the misfortune lies in the east; |
| 宜叩九天。 | 宜向九天叩祷。 | it is fitting to pray to the Nine Heavens. |
| 九天者， | 所谓九天， | The Nine Heavens are |
| 雷祀大帝， | 就是雷祀大帝， | the Great Emperor of the Thunder Rites, |
| 即王枢经也。 | 也就是王枢经了。 | that is, the Wangshu Scripture (the text has 'Wangshu', probably for 'Yushu'). |
| 又犯三茅真君及东岳尊神、及树头神圣， | 又冒犯三茅真君和东岳尊神，以及树头的神灵， | It also offends the True Lord of the Three Mao, the honoured god of the Eastern Peak and the sacred spirits of the treetops, |
| 亦有长男为祟、或天嗔、或责毙兼折足、驾舟等鬼。 | 也有家里的长男作祟，或是天嗔，或是被打死兼折了脚、驾船这类的鬼。 | and there is also the eldest son as haunting, or heaven's wrath, or ghosts beaten to death, with broken legs, who piloted boats and the like. |

> **按**:这一段是书里「总论」的头一段（编号「之一」），接在搜决神鬼章的结语（LY.ILL.EL.43）之后：按官鬼所在的宫来认神鬼，和易冒的八宫（LY.ILL.YM.57）、黄金策的八宫（LY.ILL.HJC.95）、本章前面「鬼临何卦何爻」（LY.ILL.EL.35）是同一类办法，只是这里每宫先给一首歌诀、再给一段解说，并且直接列出民间的具体神名。乾宫这里只有解说、没有歌诀。细目上，易林补遗大体把黄金策和易冒两套并列写了进去：乾用黄金策的天灯斗愿，震既有易冒的九天、折足，又有黄金策的树神、杖死，艮和易冒一样是山神、少男。「椿庭」指父亲（「萱堂」指母亲，见下一条）；「头风」是久治不愈的头痛；「斗齐」按上下文读作「斗斋」，「素原」读作「素愿」——这本书里「愿」常写作「原」（「香原」「香愿」并见）；「三茅」是道教茅山的三位茅君；「王枢经」疑是「玉枢经」（本章后面「解禳通用法」里写的就是「玉枢经」）；「瀹死」的「瀹」本义是煮，这里按上下句（「溺死」）读作溺死；「地氏」没有解释，按字面译；「手指皆为疮烂鼻亡」文字含混，对着下面解说「臂疮手折指烂鼻等鬼」译。「坎在外三爻」按字面译，指坎在外面三爻（外卦）。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the first section of the book's appended 'general discourse' (numbered 'one'), coming after the closing passage of the 'searching out ghosts and spirits' chapter (LY.ILL.EL.43): it recognises the god or ghost by the palace the Pressure line is in. It is the same kind of method as the Yi Mao's eight palaces (LY.ILL.YM.57), the Huangjin Ce's eight palaces (LY.ILL.HJC.95) and this chapter's earlier 'which trigram and which line the ghost is in' (LY.ILL.EL.35), except that here each palace gets a verse first and then a commentary, and popular names of particular gods are listed outright. For Qian there is only the commentary and no verse. In detail, the Yilin Buyi in the main sets the two systems, the Huangjin Ce's and the Yi Mao's, side by side: Qian uses the Huangjin Ce's heaven lamp and Dipper vow, Zhen has both the Yi Mao's Nine Heavens and broken leg and the Huangjin Ce's tree spirit and death by the rod, and Gen agrees with the Yi Mao in the Mountain God and the youngest son. 'Chun ting' (the toon-tree courtyard) means the father (the 'Xuan hall' means the mother, see the next entry); 'head-wind' is a headache that does not go away; 'dou qi' is read as 'dou zhai' (a fast of the Dipper) from the context, and the 'yuan' of 'su yuan' (a vegetarian vow) is the character for 'source', which this book often writes for the character for 'vow' (both forms of 'incense vow' occur); the 'Three Mao' are the three Mao lords of the Daoist Mount Mao; 'Wangshu Scripture' is probably the 'Yushu Scripture' (the Jade Pivot Scripture), which is the name written in this chapter's later 'general methods of exorcism'; the 'yue' in 'yue si' properly means to boil, and is read here as drowned from the neighbouring line ('drowned'); 'Earth clans' has no explanation and is rendered literally; the verse 'hands and fingers all turn to sores, and the dead with rotten noses' is loose in wording and is translated against the commentary below, 'ghosts of arm sores, broken hands, rotten fingers and rotten noses'. 'Kan in the outer three lines' is rendered literally and means Kan in the outer three lines (the outer trigram). Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.43、LY.ILL.EL.45、LY.ILL.EL.35、LY.ILL.YM.57、LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.YY.30、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.EL.45 总论八宫值鬼诀(下)：鬼发巽宫是东南镇海施相公、花园树木神道、长女、缢死疯疾腿折腰跎鬼；离宫是南方火部诸神（南斗六司、火德星君、五显灵官、灶神）、中女、目疾焚烧鬼；坤宫是西南土皇、坟墓、已故母亲老妇、虚黄鼓胀鬼；兑宫是正西佛天口许、伤神、少女、缺唇师巫鬼，加朱雀动是自己罚誓或他人咒咀天曹

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:巽宫：病患在东南，是冒犯了镇海的施相公、花园树木的神道，以及长女和上吊死的、疯疾的、腿折的、驼背的鬼。离宫：祸从南方起，是火部的诸神——南斗六司、火德星君、五显灵官、香头灶神这一类，还有中女以及眼盲、火烧这类的鬼，要赶紧酬愿、焚香。坤宫：西南方来，是冒犯了土皇使人不安，或者是坟墓上的缘故，和已故的母亲、年老的妇人，以及面色虚黄、腹胀如鼓的鬼。兑宫：祸落在正西，是许过佛、天的愿，也犯了伤神和少女，以及缺唇的、师巫这类的鬼；再加朱雀发动，不是自己发过誓、赌过咒，就是别人在天曹那里咒了他。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Xun: the illness lies in the southeast, and it is an offence against Lord Shi who pacifies the sea, the spirits of garden trees, and the eldest daughter and ghosts of the hanged, of madness, of broken legs and of bent backs. Li: the misfortune rises in the south, and it is the gods of the Fire Department, the likes of the Six Departments of the Southern Dipper, the Fire Virtue Star Lord, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and the incense head and Kitchen God, and also the middle daughter and ghosts of blindness and burning, and the vow should be redeemed quickly and incense burned. Kun: it comes from the southwest, and it is an offence against the Earth Emperor that brings unrest, or a matter of graves, together with the late mother and old women and ghosts of pale-yellow emptiness and drum-like swelling. Dui: the misfortune stains the due west, and it is a vow made to Buddha or Heaven, and it also offends the injuring spirit and the youngest daughter and ghosts of the harelipped, teachers and shamans; and if the Vermilion Bird moves too, then if it is not one's own sworn oath it is someone else cursing at the Heavenly Bureau.

| 加朱雀动， | 再加朱雀发动， | With the Vermilion Bird moving as well, |
| 若非自己罚誓心， | 如果不是自己罚咒发誓的心， | if it is not one's own heart of swearing an oath, |
| 是他人咒咀天曹。 | 就是别人在天曹面前咒诅。 | it is someone else cursing before the Heavenly Bureau. |

> **按**:这是「总论八宫值鬼诀」的下半（巽、离、坤、兑），体例同上一条（LY.ILL.EL.44）。「路冲」是路上冲撞；「腰跎」按字面译作弯腰，疑是「腰驼」；「麻疯」即麻风。离宫的歌诀「离家鬼发」的「家」按上下文是「宫」；它只有两个分句，一半歌诀一半解说，这里都当歌诀译。解说里「神撤南方有犯」的「撤」字费解，按「南方的神有所冒犯」的大意译；「香头灶神」按字面译作「香头、灶神」，「香头」可能是管香火的人，也可能是香火神，书里没有解释。「萱堂」是母亲（与上一条的「椿庭」相对）；「虚黄鼓胀」是面色萎黄、腹胀如鼓一类的病，和本章「土鬼」（LY.ILL.EL.34）的「膨胀虚黄」相同。兑宫最后「加朱雀动」是说朱雀也动：朱雀主口舌，所以是罚咒发誓一类的口舌事，不是自己发过誓，就是别人在天曹面前咒过他；和黄金策「朱雀咒诅口舌」（LY.ILL.HJC.96）、易冒「朱雀离神词讼囹圄冤鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.59）同一路。巽宫的「缢死、疯疾」是黄金策的巽（LY.ILL.HJC.95），「镇海、园林、长女」是易冒的巽（LY.ILL.YM.57），这里两边都收。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the second half of the 'general discourse on the eight palaces' (Xun, Li, Kun, Dui), in the same form as the previous entry (LY.ILL.EL.44). 'Lu chong' is a clash on the road; 'yao tuo' is translated literally as a bent waist and is probably 'yao tuo' meaning hunchback; 'ma feng' is leprosy. In the Li verse 'Li jia gui fa' the 'jia' (house) is 'palace' from the context; it has only two clauses, half verse and half commentary, and both are translated as verse here. In the commentary the 'che' in 'shen che nan fang you fan' is hard to understand, and is translated in the general sense 'there is an offence against a god in the south'; 'xiang tou zao shen' is translated literally as 'the incense head and the Kitchen God', and the 'incense head' may be a keeper of the incense fire or an incense spirit, which the book does not explain. The 'Xuan hall' is the mother (as opposed to the 'Chun ting' of the previous entry); 'pale-yellow emptiness and drum-like swelling' is an illness of a sallow face and a belly swollen like a drum, the same as the 'swelling and pale-yellow emptiness' of this chapter's 'Earth ghost' (LY.ILL.EL.34). The last 'with the Vermilion Bird moving' in Dui means the Vermilion Bird also moves: the Vermilion Bird governs the mouth and tongue, so it is a matter of oaths and curses, and if it is not one's own oath it is another's curse before the Heavenly Bureau; it is on the same path as the Huangjin Ce's 'the Vermilion Bird is curses and quarrels' (LY.ILL.HJC.96) and the Yi Mao's 'the Vermilion Bird is the spirit of Li, lawsuits and the grievous ghost of prison' (LY.ILL.YM.59). The Xun's 'hanged and madness' is the Huangjin Ce's Xun (LY.ILL.HJC.95) and 'pacifying the sea, gardens and groves and the eldest daughter' is the Yi Mao's Xun (LY.ILL.YM.57); here both are taken in. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.44、LY.ILL.EL.46、LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.YM.57、LY.ILL.YM.59、LY.ILL.HJC.95、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.YY.30、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.EL.46 总论五行值鬼诀：鬼值金爻是西伤（五道）总管丧神七煞、张相（六五相公）金神金元七总管寺内金刚自刎鬼锁条之愿、叶神（九卓）钟师（钟将军）武安王（关公）；木爻是茶延枷锁船神草野家堂萧公（五圣）东岳树神山神九良星煞悬梁（缢死）鬼；水爻是观音三官北斗龙王祠山大帝金龙四大王水仙杜氏河太曹三施宋二相晏公井泉童子徐大将军河伯水官溺死魂；火爻是赵玄坛五福五显灵官火德星君三煞天灯香愿南堂五圣灶神焚烧鬼；土爻是城隍社庙神五方贤圣土皇土地太岁螣蛇皮肠大王瘟疫鬼；此论五行所属，仍须把六兽入官细看、轻重同看，不可胡判

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼落在哪一行的爻上，就是哪一路的神鬼。金爻：是煞，还有西伤（就是五道）；总管、丧神、七煞；张相（就是六五相公）和金神七煞、金元七总管、寺里的金刚、自刎的鬼、锁条的愿；叶神（就是九卓）、钟师（就是钟将军）、武安王（就是关公）。木爻：茶延、枷锁、船神、草野、家堂、萧公（就是五圣）、东岳、树神、山神、九良星煞，和悬梁的鬼（就是上吊死的鬼）。水爻：观音、三官、北斗、龙王、祠山大帝、金龙四大王、水仙、杜氏夫人、河太、曹三、施相公、宋相公、晏公、坑厕的主、井泉童子、徐大将军、河伯水官，和溺死的魂。火爻：赵玄坛、五福大神、五显灵官、火德星君、三煞、天灯香愿、南堂五圣、灶神，和被火烧死的鬼。土爻：城隍、社庙里的神、五方贤圣、土皇、土地、太岁、螣蛇、皮肠大王，和瘟疫鬼。这里只是论五行各属哪些神，还要再把六兽放进官鬼里去寻，轻重也照这样比较，千万不要随口乱判是哪一位鬼神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line on a line of a given phase gives that kind of god or ghost. A Metal line: a killing star, and further the Western Injury (the Five Roads); the Chief Official, the mourning spirits, the Seven Killings; Lord Zhang (Lord Liuwu) with the Golden Spirit and the Seven Killings, the Chief Official Jin Yuanqi, the vajra guardians in temples, ghosts who cut their own throats, and the vow of the chain; the Leaf God (Jiuzhuo), Master Zhong (General Zhong) and the King of Martial Peace (Guan Gong). A Wood line: the Tea Feast, the cangue and fetters, the boat god, the wilds, the household shrine, Lord Xiao (the Five Sages), the Eastern Peak, the tree god, the Mountain God and the Nine Good Stars, and the ghost of hanging from a beam (that is, one who hanged himself). A Water line: Guanyin, the Three Officials, the Northern Dipper, the Dragon King, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain, the Golden Dragon Fourth Great King, the Water Immortal, Lady Du, He Tai, Cao San, Minister Shi, Minister Song, Lord Yan, the master of pit and privy, the youth of the well and spring, General Xu, the River Count and the water officials, and the souls of the drowned. A Fire line: Zhao Xuantan, the Great God of the Five Blessings, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, the Fire Virtue Star Lord, the Three Killings, the heaven lamp and incense vow, the Five Sages of the Southern Hall, the Kitchen God, and ghosts burned to death. An Earth line: the City God, the gods of shrines and temples, the sages of the five quarters, the Earth Emperor, the Earth God, Tai Sui, the Coiling Snake, the Great King Pichang, and ghosts of epidemic. This only discusses which gods belong to which phase; one must further bring the six beasts into the Pressure line to search, weigh the light and the heavy in the same way, and on no account judge which ghost or god it is by idle talk.

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1425 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1425*

| 鬼值土爻， | 鬼值土爻， | A ghost on an Earth line |
| 为城隍土壳神祠庙中神道、五方贤圣、土皇土地太岁、螣蛇、皮肠大王并瘟疫鬼。 | 是城隍、土壳神、祠庙中的神道、五方贤圣、土皇土地太岁、螣蛇、皮肠大王，和瘟疫鬼。 | is the City God, the earth-husk spirits, the gods within shrines and temples, the sages of the five quarters, the Earth Emperor, the Earth God and Tai Sui, the Coiling Snake, the Great King Pichang, and ghosts of epidemic. |
| 此论五行神所属， | 这里论的是五行各属哪些神， | This discusses which gods belong to each of the five phases; |
| 还将六兽入官寻， | 还要再把六兽放进官鬼里去寻， | one must further bring the six beasts into the Pressure line to search; |
| 轻重较量同此看， | 轻重也照这样比较来看， | weigh the light and the heavy in the same way; |
| 切莫胡言判鬼神。 | 千万不要随口乱判鬼神。 | on no account judge ghosts and gods by idle talk. |

> **按**:这是「总论」的第二段（编号「之二」）：把官鬼所在的五行各配一串神名。本章前面（LY.ILL.EL.30 到 EL.34）已经按五行配六兽逐个写过一遍，这里是把神名压成一张速查表；末了一句「还将六兽入官寻」就是指回那里——先看五行，再看六兽，两样合起来才断，不能只凭一样。名字上的几处存疑：「金罡」按下面解说的「寺内金刚」读作金刚（护法），这里音译带出；「茶廷」和解说的「茶延」是同一个，按「茶延」（Tea Feast，LY.ILL.EL.39 译法）处理；「兴武」疑是「真武」（易隐的水鬼名单是「观音真武三官」，LY.ILL.YY.28，顺序和这里一致），这里按原字音译；「河太曹堂」的「堂」和解说的「曹三」不一致，两处都按原文译；「三官驿帝」的「驿帝」费解，可能是「三官大帝」之误，这里按字面译；「土壳神」疑是「土谷神」（易隐的土鬼名单写「城隍土谷太岁皮场大王」，LY.ILL.YY.28），「皮肠大王」易隐写作「皮场大王」，这里都按原字译；「鬼值金爻为煞」的「煞」是杀气、凶星的统称。「瀹死」同上一条，读作溺死。「五道」「九卓」「钟将军」「关公」「六五相公」是解说自己给的对应，这里照抄，不另作认定。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the second section of the 'general discourse' (numbered 'two'): each phase the Pressure line falls in is matched with a string of divine names. Earlier in this chapter (LY.ILL.EL.30 to EL.34) the phases were matched with the six beasts one by one, and here the names are compressed into a quick-reference table; the last sentence, 'then bring the six beasts into the Pressure line to search', points back there: first look at the phase, then at the beast, and judge only with the two together, never from one alone. Doubts about a few names: 'Jingang' (with an unusual second character) is read as the vajra guardian from the 'vajra guardians in temples' in the commentary below and is transliterated; 'cha ting' and the commentary's 'cha yan' are the same, and are handled as 'Tea Feast' (as translated in LY.ILL.EL.39); 'Xingwu' is probably 'Zhenwu' (the Yi Yin's Water ghost list is 'Guanyin, Zhenwu, the Three Officials', LY.ILL.YY.28, in the same order as here), and is transliterated as written; the 'tang' in 'He Tai Cao Tang' does not agree with the commentary's 'Cao San' and both are translated as written; the 'yi di' in 'the Three Officials and the Emperor of the Post' is hard to understand and may be a slip for 'the Three Officials the Great Emperors', and is translated literally; 'earth-husk spirits' is probably 'earth-and-grain spirits' (the Yi Yin's Earth ghost list reads 'the City God, the Earth-and-Grain, Tai Sui, the Great King Pichang', LY.ILL.YY.28), and the Yi Yin writes 'Pichang' with a different character for 'chang'; all are translated as written; the 'sha' in 'a ghost on a Metal line is a killing star' is the general term for a killing air or ill-omened star. 'Yue si' is as in the previous entries, read as drowned. The correspondences 'Five Roads', 'Jiuzhuo', 'General Zhong', 'Guan Gong' and 'Lord Liuwu' are given by the commentary itself and are copied here, without further identification. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.45、LY.ILL.EL.47、LY.ILL.EL.30、LY.ILL.EL.31、LY.ILL.EL.32、LY.ILL.EL.33、LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.EL.39、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

## LY.ILL.EL.47 总论六神值诀：鬼值青龙是东岳家堂茶筵花煞五圣龙王三官五路及枷锁愿心产亡鬼；朱雀是城隍草野总管天曹华光（五显灵官）广灵侯（南堂陆太君）司令（灶神）及旧欠愿心牢中鬼；勾陈是土皇五方贤圣跌死鬼；螣蛇是妖怪土地里社之神产亡缢死二鬼；白虎是伤司五道丧煞赵玄坛金神（七煞）叶神（九卓）刀伤虎伤鬼；玄武是圣帝曹三河太伏尸坑厕井泉童子杜氏夫人采山（草野三郎）溺死窃盗二鬼；定六神诸位仍须八卦五行排、看清何爻临鬼，莫累人虚费财

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼值青龙：是东岳、家堂、茶筵、花煞、五圣、龙王、三官大帝、五路尊神，以及枷锁愿心、产亡的鬼。鬼值朱雀：是城隍、草野总管、天曹、华光（就是五显灵官）、广灵侯（就是南堂陆太君）、司令（就是灶神），以及旧欠愿心、牢里的鬼。鬼值勾陈：是土皇和五方贤圣，以及跌死的鬼。鬼值螣蛇：是妖怪，并且作犯，又是土、当方的土地、里社之神，以及产亡、缢死两种鬼。鬼值白虎：是伤司、五道、丧煞，赵玄坛和金神就是七煞，叶神就是九卓，以及刀伤、虎伤的鬼。鬼值玄武：是圣帝、曹三、河太、伏尸、坑厕、土井泉童子、杜氏夫人，采山就是草野三郎，以及溺死、偷盗两种鬼。要定下六神各是哪一位圣，还必须靠八卦和五行来排，哪一爻临在鬼上要看清楚，不要连累人白白花钱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Pressure line on the Azure Dragon: the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Tea Feast, the Flower Killing, the Five Sages, the Dragon King, the Three Officials the Great Emperors and the honoured god of the Five Roads, together with ghosts of the cangue-and-fetters vow and of death in childbirth. On the Vermilion Bird: the City God, the Chief Official of the wilds, the Heavenly Bureau, Huaguang (the Five Manifest Spirit Officials), the Marquis Guangling (Lady Lu of the Southern Hall), the Director (the Kitchen God), together with ghosts of old owed vows and of the prison. On Hook Earth: the Earth Emperor and the sages of the five quarters, and the ghost of one who died by a fall. On the Coiling Snake: monsters, and it makes an offence; also the earth, the Earth God of the local quarter, the god of the village altar, and the two ghosts of death in childbirth and of hanging. On the White Tiger: the Injuring Office, the Five Roads, the Mourning Killing; Zhao Xuantan and the Golden Spirit are the Seven Killings; the Leaf God is Jiuzhuo; together with ghosts of knife wounds and tiger wounds. On the Dark Tortoise: the Holy Emperor, Cao San, He Tai, the buried corpse, the pit and privy, the earth, the youth of the well and spring, Lady Du; the Mountain Gatherer is the Third Lord of the wilds; together with the two ghosts of drowning and of theft. To fix which sage each of the six spirits is, one must still arrange them by the eight trigrams and the five phases, look closely to see which line sits on the ghost, and not burden people with empty spending of money.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四·附总论之三:六神值诀》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1449 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74, appended general discourse 3: the six spirits", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1449*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 然定六神诸圣位， | 然而要定六神各是哪一位圣， | Yet to fix the seats of the sages of the six spirits, |
| 还须八卦五行排， | 还必须靠八卦和五行来排； | one must still arrange them by the eight trigrams and the five phases; |
| 谁爻临鬼详端的， | 哪一爻临在鬼上，要看得端正确实， | look closely to see which line sits on the ghost, |
| 莫累人间虚费财。 | 不要连累世人，白白花费钱财。 | and do not burden people with empty spending of money. |

> **按**:这是「总论」的第三段（编号「之三」）：官鬼所临的六神各配一串神名，体例是歌诀加解说。它和易冒「按六神断神鬼」（LY.ILL.YM.59）、黄金策「六神推鬼神」（LY.ILL.HJC.96）是同一类办法，只是名目更细；和本章前面按五行配六兽（LY.ILL.EL.30 到 EL.34）也可以对读，六神的神名与上一条（LY.ILL.EL.46）几乎全是同一批。最后一首歌诀说得最重：六神各是哪一位，还要靠八卦和五行来排、看清哪一爻临鬼，不能只凭六神一样就点名，也不要让人白白花钱——这和本章结语（LY.ILL.EL.43）、黄金策和易冒把神鬼只看作大类的做法一致。名字上的几处存疑：「花枷原」按「花枷愿」译；「止苍」疑是「上苍」（High Heaven），这里按原字音译；「承天后土皇」按字面译作「承天的后土皇」；「皇社神」按字面译作皇社（皇家的社坛）之神；「西台」没有解释，按字面译；「穿斋」按下面解说「窃盗」读作「穿窬」（挖墙洞偷东西）；「司令」解说里说就是灶神（通常写作「司命」，本书前文也写「司命」），按原字译作 Director；「螣蛇」一条「并作犯上」文字很简，「作犯」按「作出冒犯」译，「上」没有着落，这里不强译。这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the third section of the 'general discourse' (numbered 'three'): each of the six spirits the Pressure line sits on is matched with a string of divine names, in the form of a verse plus a commentary. It is the same kind of method as the Yi Mao's 'judging gods and ghosts by the six spirits' (LY.ILL.YM.59) and the Huangjin Ce's 'the six spirits and gods and ghosts' (LY.ILL.HJC.96), with finer names, and can also be read against this chapter's earlier matching of the phases with the six beasts (LY.ILL.EL.30 to EL.34); the names of gods here are almost all the same set as in the previous entry (LY.ILL.EL.46). The last verse is the weightiest: which sage each of the six spirits is must still be arranged by the eight trigrams and the five phases, looking closely at which line sits on the ghost; one must not name a god from the six spirits alone, and one must not make people spend money for nothing, which agrees with this chapter's closing passage (LY.ILL.EL.43) and with the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Mao, who treat gods and ghosts only in broad kinds. Doubts about a few names: 'hua jia yuan' is translated as the vow of flowers and the cangue, the character for 'source' standing for 'vow'; 'Zhicang' is probably 'Shangcang' (High Heaven), and is transliterated as written; 'cheng tian hou tu huang' is rendered literally as 'the Sovereign Earth Emperor who receives Heaven'; 'huang she shen' is rendered literally as the spirit of the imperial altar; 'Western Terrace' has no explanation and is rendered literally; 'chuan zhai' is read as 'chuan yu' (boring through a wall to steal) from the commentary 'theft'; 'Director' (si ling) is said by the commentary to be the Kitchen God (usually written 'si ming', and this book earlier also writes 'Director of Fate'), and is translated as written; the Coiling Snake's 'and it makes an offence' is very terse: 'zuo fan' is translated as 'makes an offence', and the 'shang' is left unforced. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.46、LY.ILL.EL.48、LY.ILL.EL.43、LY.ILL.EL.30、LY.ILL.YM.59、LY.ILL.HJC.96、LY.ILL.YY.17、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZSZ.07、LY.ILL.GLJ.13

---

## LY.ILL.EL.48 总论星煞值鬼诀(一)：看神司先推官鬼（属阳男伤、属阴女鬼），再看临何星何煞；青龙带天喜（正月起戌顺行十二位）是花煞之神；白虎带丧门（正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑循环）是丧煞；朱雀带天火（子卯午酉循环）天烛（正月起巳顺行至辰）是五显灵官、灶神；玄武官爻带天贼（按十二属相）或天盗（亥寅巳申循环）是草野三郎

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看神司，先推官鬼：官鬼属阳，是男人受伤；属阴，是女鬼。再看它临着哪一颗星、哪一个煞，就知道是什么在作祟。青龙上的鬼带天喜，可以说是花煞之神（天喜的排法：正月从戌上起，顺着数十二位）。鬼临白虎又带丧门，就说是丧煞为殃（丧门：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑、五月又到戌，只在这四个位上循环）。天火、天烛和朱雀同在，是五显灵官和灶皇（天火：正月子、二月卯、三月午、四月酉，循环；天烛：正月起在巳宫，顺着数到辰）；这个煞就是大朱雀，官鬼临上它，就是冒犯了五显灵官、灶神在作祟，而且天烛遇到发动时，做福、祈禳也大凶。玄武上的官爻临着天贼星或天盗煞，就是草野三郎（天贼：正月龙〔辰〕、二月鸡〔酉〕、三月虎〔寅〕、四月羊〔未〕、五月鼠〔子〕、六月蛇〔巳〕、七月犬〔戌〕、八月兔〔卯〕、九月猴〔申〕、十月牛〔丑〕、子月马〔午〕、丑月猪〔亥〕；天盗：正月亥、二月寅、三月巳、四月申，只在这四位上循环）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To look at a god or office, first work out the Pressure line: if it is yang, a man is harmed; if it is yin, it is a woman's ghost. Then look at which star and which killing it sits on, and one knows which haunting is the calamity. A ghost on the Azure Dragon with Heavenly Joy may be called the god of the Flower Killing (Heavenly Joy is set thus: the first month starts from Xu and runs forward through twelve places). A ghost on the White Tiger with Mourning Gate is said to be the Mourning Killing as calamity (Mourning Gate: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, the fifth again reaching Xu, cycling only through these four positions). Heavenly Fire and Heavenly Candle together with the Vermilion Bird are the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and the Kitchen Sovereign (Heavenly Fire: the first month Zi, the second Mao, the third Wu, the fourth You, cycling; Heavenly Candle: the first month starts at the snake palace and runs forward, counting to the dragon); this killing is the Great Vermilion Bird, and a Pressure line on it is an offence against the Five Manifest Spirit Officials or the Kitchen God as haunting, and when it meets movement, making blessings or praying for exorcism is also very ominous. A Pressure line on the Dark Tortoise that sits on the Heavenly Thief star or the Heavenly Robber killing is the Third Lord of the wilds (Heavenly Thief by the twelve animals: the first month the dragon [Chen], the second the rooster [You], the third the tiger [Yin], the fourth the sheep [Wei], the fifth the rat [Zi], the sixth the snake [Si], the seventh the dog [Xu], the eighth the hare [Mao], the ninth the monkey [Shen], the tenth the ox [Chou], the Zi month the horse [Wu], the Chou month the pig [Hai]; Heavenly Robber: the first month Hai, the second Yin, the third Si, the fourth Shen, cycling only through these four).

| 玄武官爻临天贼星或临天盗煞， | 玄武上的官爻临着天贼星或天盗煞， | A Pressure line on the Dark Tortoise that sits on the Heavenly Thief star or the Heavenly Robber killing |
| 便为草野三郎。 | 就是草野三郎。 | is the Third Lord of the wilds. |

> **打架**:丧门、天喜怎么排，各书不同。①丧门：易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.48）是正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，四个位置周而复始；断易天机的注释引鬼谷子的杀例，同样是正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，并说「只此四位，轮十二月」；火珠林（卜筮大全里抄了同一段）的丧门杀例是正月未、二月辰、三月丑、四月戌，也说「只此四位轮十二月」——四个位置一样，起点差一位。②天喜：易林补遗是正月从戌起、顺行十二位（戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰、巳、午、未、申、酉），周易尚占的逐月吉凶神杀表也是这样；卜筮大全的神煞篇是「春戌夏丑为天喜，秋辰冬未」，按季不按月。这几本书都没有说谁对谁错，按哪一家排，同一个月里的位置会不同。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How Mourning Gate and Heavenly Joy are set differs from book to book. (1) Mourning Gate: the Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.48) has the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, the four positions cycling; the commentary in the Duanyi Tianji quotes the rule of the killings of Guiguzi, which is likewise the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, and says 'only these four positions, rotating through twelve months'; the rule of the Mourning Gate killing in the Huozhulin (whose same passage is copied in the Buyi Daquan) is the first month Wei, the second Chen, the third Chou, the fourth Xu, and also says 'only these four positions rotating through twelve months': the same four positions, with the starting point one place off. (2) Heavenly Joy: the Yilin Buyi has the first month starting from Xu and running forward through twelve places (Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen, Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, You), and the Zhouyi Shangzhan's monthly table of lucky and unlucky stars agrees; the chapter on killings in the Buyi Daquan says 'spring Xu, summer Chou, autumn Chen, winter Wei is Heavenly Joy', by season and not by month. None of these books says which is right, and which one is followed changes the position in the same month.

> **按**:这是「总论」的第四段（编号「之四」），体例又变了：每条先是一句诗、再是解说，解说里还带着各星煞的「起例」——也就是这颗星每个月落在哪一支。这一节共五条（LY.ILL.EL.48 到 EL.52），讲的是「官鬼临了某颗星、某个煞，就是某位神鬼」，而且要同六兽合看（青龙对天喜，白虎对丧门，朱雀对天火天烛，玄武对天贼天盗……）。这一条的头两句是总则：看神司要先推官鬼，鬼属阳是男人受伤、属阴是女鬼（和黄金策的阳男阴女，LY.ILL.HJC.94 同一路）。「凡居卦下论阴阳」的「卦下」按字面译作「居在卦里」。「灶皇」按字面译作 Kitchen Sovereign，解说里已经说是灶神。「荡荡顺行数至龙」的「龙」是辰，天烛从巳起（蛇宫），数到辰正好十二个月；「采山」在下面 LY.ILL.EL.47 的解说里说就是草野三郎，「水三郎」按字面译。天贼那首口诀把十二属相对着地支读：龙辰、鸡酉、虎寅、羊未、鼠子、蛇巳、犬戌、兔卯、猴申、牛丑、马午、猪亥，每月往后推五位，是一个整齐的数列，易冒也有同一首口诀（易冒讲诸星一章的注），两处一致。 书里没有说这里的「正月、二月」是起卦的月还是别的月，这里照抄；「只此四位周而复始」是说五月回到正月的位置、九月也回到正月的位置，依此类推。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the fourth section of the 'general discourse' (numbered 'four'), and its form changes again: each item is a line of verse and then a commentary, and the commentary also carries the 'rules of setting' of each star or killing, that is, the branch on which the star falls in each month. This part of five entries (LY.ILL.EL.48 to EL.52) is about 'a Pressure line on such a star or such a killing is such a god or ghost', and it has to be read together with the six beasts (Azure Dragon with Heavenly Joy, White Tiger with Mourning Gate, Vermilion Bird with Heavenly Fire and Candle, Dark Tortoise with Heavenly Thief and Robber, and so on). The first two lines of this entry are the general rule: to look at a god or office one first works out the Pressure line; a yang ghost is a man harmed and a yin one a woman's ghost (on the same path as the Huangjin Ce's yang is male, yin is female, LY.ILL.HJC.94). 'Everything under the hexagram' in the first verse is translated as 'wherever it sits in the hexagram'. 'Kitchen Sovereign' is a literal rendering of 'zao huang', which the commentary already says is the Kitchen God. In 'counting until it reaches the dragon' the dragon is Chen; the Heavenly Candle starts from Si (the snake palace) and counting to Chen makes exactly twelve months; the 'Mountain Gatherer' is said in the commentary of LY.ILL.EL.47 to be the Third Lord of the wilds, and 'water Third Lord' is rendered literally. The Heavenly Thief verse reads the twelve animals against the branches: dragon Chen, rooster You, tiger Yin, sheep Wei, rat Zi, snake Si, dog Xu, hare Mao, monkey Shen, ox Chou, horse Wu, pig Hai, each month moving on five places, which is a regular series, and the Yi Mao has the same verse (in its note in the chapter on the stars), the two agreeing. The book does not say whether the 'first month' and so on are the months of the divination or of something else, and here it is copied as written; 'only these four positions, cycling round and round' means that the fifth month returns to the position of the first and the ninth to that of the first, and so on. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.47、LY.ILL.EL.49、LY.ILL.EL.29、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.BSZZ.37

---

## LY.ILL.EL.49 总论星煞值鬼诀(二)：勾陈官鬼或螣蛇官鬼临丧门或吊客（正月辰、二月丑、三月戌、四月未循环）皆主伏尸土；玄武鬼爻带咸池沐浴（正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午循环）是杜氏夫人；鬼带勾陈又临折煞（正月酉、二月午、三月卯、四月子循环）并卦内驿马（正月申、二月巳、三月寅、四月亥循环）发动必犯途中跌死伤亡；玄武官爻带天河煞（正月起辰顺行十二位）必是井中或江河溺死之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:勾陈上的官鬼或螣蛇上的官鬼，又临着丧门或遇上吊客，都主伏尸土（吊客的排法：正月辰、二月丑、三月戌、四月未，只在这四位上轮；丧门的排法见上一条）。玄武上的鬼爻如果带着咸池、沐浴，就是杜氏夫人（沐浴：正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午，只在这四位上轮；咸池煞的排法相同，也是正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午）。鬼带勾陈，又临折煞，并且卦里的驿马发动，必定犯了途中跌死、伤亡（折煞：正月酉、二月午、三月卯、四月子，周而复始；驿马：正月申、二月巳、三月寅、四月亥，也是这四位轮）。玄武上的官爻又带天河煞，必是井中溺死的鬼，不然就必是江河里淹死的人（天河煞：正月从辰上起，顺行十二位）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Hook Earth Pressure line or a Coiling Snake Pressure line that also sits on Mourning Gate or meets Guest of Condolence in every case governs the earth of a buried corpse (Guest of Condolence is set thus: the first month Chen, the second Chou, the third Xu, the fourth Wei, turning only through these four positions; Mourning Gate is set as in the previous entry). If a Dark Tortoise Pressure line carries Xianchi and Bath, it is Lady Du (Bath: the first month Mao, the second Zi, the third You, the fourth Wu, turning only through these four positions; the Xianchi killing is set in the same way, also the first month Mao, the second Zi, the third You, the fourth Wu). If the ghost carries Hook Earth and also sits on Breaking Killing, and the Post Horse in the hexagram moves, it surely offends a death by a fall or an injury on the road (Breaking Killing: the first month You, the second Wu, the third Mao, the fourth Zi, cycling round and round; Post Horse: the first month Shen, the second Si, the third Yin, the fourth Hai, also turning through these four positions). If a Dark Tortoise Pressure line also carries the Heavenly River killing, it is surely the ghost of one drowned in a well, or else of one drowned in a river (Heavenly River killing: the first month starts from Chen and runs forward through twelve places).

| 伏尸土禁不为良。 | 伏尸、土禁，都不是好事。 | the buried corpse and the earth taboos are not good. |

| --- | --- | --- |

| 正月从辰上起， | 正月从辰上起， | the first month starts from Chen, |
| 顺行十二位。 | 顺着行十二位。 | and runs forward through twelve places. |
| 玄武官爻又带天河煞者， | 玄武上的官爻又带着天河煞的， | A Dark Tortoise Pressure line that also carries the Heavenly River killing |
| 必是井中瀹死之鬼， | 必定是井中溺死的鬼， | is surely the ghost of one drowned in a well, |
| 不然必江河溺死者。 | 不然必定是江河里淹死的人。 | or else surely of one drowned in a river. |

> **按**:这一条讲勾陈、螣蛇、玄武三兽各自配的星煞。伏尸土：「陈蛇位」是勾陈、螣蛇这两个土兽的位置；「伏尸土禁」按字面译作「buried corpse and the earth taboos」——「土禁」可能指动土的禁忌，书里没有解释。「街妨」译作「obstruction in the street」。沐浴、咸池在书里是两个星，排法却一样（正月卯、二月子、三月酉、四月午），都是每次往后退三位的四位循环。「瀹死」的「瀹」本义是煮，这里按上下句（「溺死」）读作溺死。折煞、驿马也各有四位循环（折煞每次退三位，驿马每次退三位）。天河煞和官符、刀砧一样，是从正月起顺行十二个月，一个月一位。和易冒「天河为觉溺之屈」（LY.ILL.YM.62）、易隐「风波浴盆浮沉溺死」（LY.ILL.YY.29）是同一件事。 书里没有说这里的「正月、二月」是起卦的月还是别的月，这里照抄；「只此四位周而复始」是说五月回到正月的位置、九月也回到正月的位置，依此类推。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This entry is about the stars and killings matched with each of the three beasts Hook Earth, the Coiling Snake and the Dark Tortoise. The earth of a buried corpse: the 'seats of Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake' are the positions of these two Earth beasts; 'the buried corpse and the earth taboos' is translated literally, and the 'earth taboos' may mean the taboos on digging the earth, which the book does not explain. 'Jie fang' is translated as 'obstruction in the street'. Bath and Xianchi are two different stars in this book but are set the same way (the first month Mao, the second Zi, the third You, the fourth Wu), a cycle of four positions each moving back three places. The 'yue' in 'yue si' properly means to boil, and is read here as drowned from the neighbouring line ('drowned'). Breaking Killing and the Post Horse also each have a cycle of four positions (Breaking Killing moving back three places at each step, and the Post Horse likewise). The Heavenly River killing, like the Official Talisman and the Chopping Block, runs forward from the first month through the twelve months, one place a month. It is the same matter as the Yi Mao's 'Heavenly River is the wrong of those who wake to drown' (LY.ILL.YM.62) and the Yi Yin's 'wind and waves, the Bath Basin, floating and sinking, drowned' (LY.ILL.YY.29). The book does not say whether the 'first month' and so on are the months of the divination or of something else, and here it is copied as written; 'only these four positions, cycling round and round' means that the fifth month returns to the position of the first and the ninth to that of the first, and so on. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.48、LY.ILL.EL.50、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.YY.05、LY.ILL.BSZZ.37

## LY.ILL.EL.50 总论星煞值鬼诀(三)：朱雀官鬼值官符（正月从午顺行十二位）是总管作祟；鬼带刀砧（正月从午顺行十二位）或逢羊刃（甲卯乙辰丙戊午丁己未庚酉辛戌壬子癸丑）是伤司；鬼值太岁带黄旌（正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑循环）是后土（土皇）；官临贵人（甲戊庚牛羊、乙己鼠猴、丙丁猪鸡、壬癸蛇兔、六辛马虎）或天喜是萧堂五圣

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:朱雀上的官鬼又值着官符，就说是总管在作祟（官符：正月从午上起，顺行十二位）。鬼带刀砧，或遇上羊刃，就是伤司（刀砧煞：正月从午上起，顺行十二位；羊刃煞按日干：甲日在卯、乙日在辰、丙戊日在午、丁己日在未、庚日在酉、辛日在戌、壬日在子、癸日在丑）。太岁就是当年；鬼值太岁若带着黄旌，就是后土，后土就是土皇（黄旌星：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只在这四位上轮）。官鬼临着贵人或者天喜，就是萧堂五圣（天乙贵人：甲、戊、庚日在牛羊〔丑未〕，乙、己日在鼠猴〔子申〕，丙、丁日在猪鸡〔亥酉〕，壬、癸日在蛇兔〔巳卯〕，辛日遇马虎〔午寅〕；天喜的排法见前）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line on the Vermilion Bird that also meets the Official Talisman is said to be the Chief Official haunting (Official Talisman: the first month starts from Wu and runs forward through twelve places). A ghost carrying the Chopping Block, or meeting the Goat Blade, is the Injuring Office (Chopping Block killing: the first month starts from Wu and runs forward through twelve places; Goat Blade killing by day stem: a Jia day at Mao, a Yi day at Chen, a Bing or Wu day at Wu, a Ding or Ji day at Wei, a Geng day at You, a Xin day at Xu, a Ren day at Zi, a Gui day at Chou). Tai Sui is the year itself; a ghost on Tai Sui, if it carries the Yellow Banner, is then the Sovereign Earth, and the Sovereign Earth is the Earth Emperor (Yellow Banner star: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, turning only through these four). A Pressure line on the Noble or on Heavenly Joy is the Five Sages of the Xiao Hall (Tianyi Noble: Jia, Wu and Geng days at the ox and sheep [Chou, Wei], Yi and Ji days at the rat and monkey [Zi, Shen], Bing and Ding days at the pig and rooster [Hai, You], Ren and Gui days at the snake and hare [Si, Mao], and Xin days meeting the horse and tiger [Wu, Yin]; Heavenly Joy is set as before).
