| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 太岁者，即年如也。 | 太岁，就是当年。 | Tai Sui is the year itself. |
| 黄旌星起例：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只此四位轮。 | 黄旌星的排法：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只在这四位上轮。 | The rule for setting the Yellow Banner star: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, only these four positions in rotation. |
| 又天乙贵人云： | 又有天乙贵人的口诀说： | Again the Tianyi Noble says: |
| 甲戊庚牛羊，乙己鼠猴乡， | 甲、戊、庚日是牛羊（丑未），乙、己日是鼠猴的地方（子申）， | Jia, Wu and Geng days: the ox and sheep; Yi and Ji days: the land of the rat and monkey; |
| 丙丁猪鸡位，壬癸蛇兔藏， | 丙、丁日是猪鸡的位（亥酉），壬、癸日蛇兔藏着（巳卯）， | Bing and Ding days: the places of the pig and rooster; Ren and Gui days: the snake and hare lie hidden; |
| 六辛逢马虎，此是贵人方。 | 六个辛日遇马、虎（午寅），这些是贵人的方位。 | the six Xin days meet the horse and tiger; these are the directions of the Noble. |
| 天喜星已列篇前， | 天喜星已经列在篇前， | The Heavenly Joy star has already been set out earlier; |
| 鬼值太岁若带黄旌，即为后土。 | 鬼值太岁，如果带着黄旌，就是后土。 | a ghost on Tai Sui, if it carries the Yellow Banner, is then the Sovereign Earth. |
| 后土者，土皇也。 | 后土，就是土皇。 | The Sovereign Earth is the Earth Emperor. |
| 又论官临贵人或临天喜，便是萧堂五圣。 | 又论官鬼临着贵人，或者临着天喜，就是萧堂五圣。 | Again, a Pressure line sitting on the Noble or on Heavenly Joy is the Five Sages of the Xiao Hall. |

> **打架**:天乙贵人和羊刃怎么定，各书不同。①天乙贵人：易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.50）是「甲戊庚牛羊，乙己鼠猴乡，丙丁猪鸡位，壬癸蛇兔藏，六辛逢马虎」，庚日的贵人在牛羊（丑未）；卜筮大全的神煞篇是「甲戊兼牛羊，乙己鼠猴乡，丙丁猪鸡位，壬癸兔蛇藏，庚辛逢马虎」，庚日和辛日一样在马虎（午寅）；周易尚占的逐日表里庚日的贵人也是午。②羊刃：易林补遗是每个日干都取「禄前一位」（甲卯、乙辰、丙戊午、丁己未、庚酉、辛戌、壬子、癸丑）；易冒讲诸星一章的注明说阳干顺行、阴干逆行（甲禄寅取卯、乙禄卯取寅、丙戊禄巳取午、丁己禄午取巳，「乙逢寅动当为刃，辛见申摇岂不凶，丁己忌巳癸忧亥」），并批评「近世不分阴阳顺逆，概以禄前一位为刃，至有误认乙禄到卯、辰为羊刃之说」——易林补遗的表正是这种一律取禄前一位的排法（乙日在辰），易冒没有点名。两边差在乙、丁、己、辛、癸五个阴干上，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How the Tianyi Noble and the Goat Blade are fixed differs from book to book. (1) Tianyi Noble: the Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.50) has 'Jia, Wu and Geng the ox and sheep; Yi and Ji the land of the rat and monkey; Bing and Ding the places of the pig and rooster; Ren and Gui the snake and hare lie hidden; the six Xin meet the horse and tiger', so that the Noble of a Geng day is at the ox and sheep (Chou, Wei); the chapter on killings in the Buyi Daquan has 'Jia and Wu together the ox and sheep; Yi and Ji the land of the rat and monkey; Bing and Ding the places of the pig and rooster; Ren and Gui the hare and snake lie hidden; Geng and Xin meet the horse and tiger', so that a Geng day is, like a Xin day, at the horse and tiger (Wu, Yin); in the table by day in the Zhouyi Shangzhan the Noble of a Geng day is also Wu. (2) Goat Blade: the Yilin Buyi takes, for every day stem, 'the place one step ahead of the Salary' (Jia Mao, Yi Chen, Bing and Wu Wu, Ding and Ji Wei, Geng You, Xin Xu, Ren Zi, Gui Chou); the note of the Yi Mao in its chapter on the stars says plainly that yang stems go forward and yin stems go backward (Jia with its Salary at Yin takes Mao, Yi with its Salary at Mao takes Yin, Bing and Wu with the Salary at Si take Wu, Ding and Ji with the Salary at Wu take Si, 'Yi meeting Yin moving is surely the blade, Xin seeing Shen shake is not ominous, Ding and Ji fear Si and Gui worries about Hai'), and it criticises that 'in recent times people do not tell yang from yin, forward from backward, and take in every case the place one step ahead of the Salary as the blade, even to the mistaken saying that Yi with its Salary at Mao has Chen as the Goat Blade': the Yilin Buyi's table is exactly this way of taking the place one step ahead of the Salary in every case (Yi on Chen), though the Yi Mao does not name it. The two differ on the five yin stems Yi, Ding, Ji, Xin and Gui, and the books do not settle it.

> **按**:这一条讲朱雀、白虎（伤司）、太岁、贵人配的星煞。官符和刀砧煞的排法完全一样：正月从午起，顺行十二个月，一月一位；羊刃煞不按月、按日干排，一个日干一个位置。「太岁者，即年如也」的「如」文字费解，按「太岁就是当年」译。天乙贵人那首口诀里的牛羊是丑未、鼠猴是子申、猪鸡是亥酉、蛇兔是巳卯、马虎是午寅，译文里一并写出地支。「六辛」按字面译作「the six Xin days」（辛日）。「后土者，土皇也」——后土（Sovereign Earth）就是土皇（Earth Emperor），和前面的勾陈、土鬼里的「土皇」（LY.ILL.EL.34、EL.47）是同一个称呼。「萧堂五圣」按字面译作 the Five Sages of the Xiao Hall，和前面的「萧公即五圣」（LY.ILL.EL.46）呼应。这里「贵人」的「贵人」不是天将，是天乙贵人星。 书里没有说这里的「正月、二月」是起卦的月还是别的月，这里照抄；「只此四位周而复始」是说五月回到正月的位置、九月也回到正月的位置，依此类推。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This entry is about the stars and killings matched with the Vermilion Bird, the White Tiger (the Injuring Office), Tai Sui and the Noble. The Official Talisman and the Chopping Block killing are set in exactly the same way: from Wu in the first month, running forward through the twelve months, one place a month; the Goat Blade killing is set not by month but by day stem, one position for each day stem. The 'ru' in 'Tai Sui is the year itself' is hard to understand, and is translated as 'Tai Sui is the year itself'. In the verse of the Tianyi Noble the ox and sheep are Chou and Wei, the rat and monkey Zi and Shen, the pig and rooster Hai and You, the snake and hare Si and Mao, and the horse and tiger Wu and Yin, and the translation writes the branches out together. 'The six Xin' is translated literally as 'the six Xin days' (Xin days). 'The Sovereign Earth is the Earth Emperor': the Sovereign Earth is the Earth Emperor, the same title as 'Earth Emperor' in the earlier Hook Earth and Earth ghost entries (LY.ILL.EL.34, EL.47). 'The Five Sages of the Xiao Hall' is a literal rendering, and echoes the earlier 'Lord Xiao is the Five Sages' (LY.ILL.EL.46). The 'Noble' here is not a celestial general but the Tianyi Noble star. The book does not say whether the 'first month' and so on are the months of the divination or of something else, and here it is copied as written; 'only these four positions, cycling round and round' means that the fifth month returns to the position of the first and the ninth to that of the first, and so on. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.49、LY.ILL.EL.51、LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.EL.46、LY.ILL.EL.47、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.YY.29

---

## LY.ILL.EL.51 总论星煞值鬼诀(四)：鬼值三丘（春丑夏辰秋未冬戌）或临五墓（对宫取，春未夏戌秋丑冬辰）便为坟墓；土鬼带暗金煞（寅申巳亥在巳、子午卯酉在酉、辰戌丑未在丑）或值羊刃是产亡带血之鬼；冲对年庚为撞命（天对甲庚乙辛丙壬丁癸、地冲六冲；天对且地冲是真撞命，只地冲是傍撞命，只天对不冲不是）；鬼值年建当言太岁

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:鬼值三丘，或者临着五墓，就是坟墓（三丘：春丑、夏辰、秋未、三冬逢戌；五墓从三丘的对宫取，病人做福也难留）。土鬼带着暗金煞，或者值着羊刃星，是难产而亡、带血的鬼（暗金煞：寅、申、巳、亥的落在巳，子、午、卯、酉的落在酉，辰、戌、丑、未的落在丑；暗金对产妇最难当）。冲到病人的生年（年庚）就叫撞命：撞有天对和地冲的分别，天对是甲庚、乙辛、丙壬、丁癸，地冲是子午、丑未、寅申、卯酉、辰戌、巳亥；天对又地冲是真撞命（比如病人丙子年生，卦里壬午鬼动），只有地冲而天干大不对的（比如庚午鬼）是傍撞命，只有天对而地不冲的不算撞命；鬼值年建，就说是太岁。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost on the Three Mounds, or on the Five Tombs, is then a grave (Three Mounds: spring Chou, summer Chen, autumn Wei, the three winter months meeting Xu; the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace of the Three Mounds, so that even when the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept). An Earth ghost carrying the Hidden Metal killing, or sitting on the Goat Blade star, is the bloody ghost of one who died in childbirth (Hidden Metal killing: for Yin, Shen, Si and Hai it falls at Si; for Zi, Wu, Mao and You at You; for Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei at Chou; Hidden Metal is the hardest for a woman in childbirth to bear). A clash on the year of birth (nian geng) of the sick person is called a collision with life: collision has the distinction of heaven-opposition and earth-clash; heaven-opposition is Jia-Geng, Yi-Xin, Bing-Ren and Ding-Gui, and earth-clash is Zi-Wu, Chou-Wei, Yin-Shen, Mao-You, Chen-Xu and Si-Hai; heaven-opposition together with earth-clash is a true collision with life (for example, the sick person was born in the Bingzi year and a Renwu ghost moves in the hexagram); earth-clash with the stems greatly unmatched (for example a Gengwu ghost) is a side collision with life; heaven-opposition without earth-clash is not a collision with life; if the ghost sits on the year's branch, one says it is on Tai Sui.

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 撞对者，有天对地冲之辨。 | 撞对，有天对和地冲的分别。 | A collision-opposition has the distinction of heaven-opposition and earth-clash. |
| 天对者，甲庚、乙辛、丙壬、丁癸是也。 | 天对，是甲庚、乙辛、丙壬、丁癸。 | Heaven-opposition is Jia-Geng, Yi-Xin, Bing-Ren and Ding-Gui. |
| 地冲者，子午、丑未、寅申、卯酉、辰戌、巳亥是也。 | 地冲，是子午、丑未、寅申、卯酉、辰戌、巳亥。 | Earth-clash is Zi-Wu, Chou-Wei, Yin-Shen, Mao-You, Chen-Xu and Si-Hai. |
| 假如病人丙子生，卦中壬午鬼动，正所谓天对地冲，此乃真撞命上也。 | 假如病人是丙子年生，卦里壬午鬼发动，正是所说的天对地冲，这是真撞命。 | Suppose the sick person was born in the Bingzi year and in the hexagram the Renwu ghost moves: this is exactly what is called heaven-opposition with earth-clash, a true collision with life. |
| 又如庚午鬼地冲大不对，乃傍撞命上也，余偕仿此。 | 又如庚午鬼，地冲而天干大不对，是傍撞命，其余都照这样仿。 | Or again, a Gengwu ghost, an earth-clash but greatly unmatched in the stems, is a side collision with life; the rest all follow this. |
| 设若天对地不冲，非是撞命之沦。 | 假设天对而地不冲，不算是撞命。 | Suppose there is heaven-opposition but no earth-clash: it is not a case of falling into collision with life. |
| 如鬼值年建，当言太岁上也。 | 如果鬼值年建，就应当说在太岁上。 | If the ghost sits on the year's branch, one should say it is on Tai Sui. |

> **按**:三丘、五墓的口诀，卜筮大全的神煞篇里有一模一样的一首（「春丑夏辰秋即未，三冬逢戌是三丘；却与五墓对宫取，病人作福也难留」），断易天机的注释引鬼谷子的五墓杀例是「春未、夏戌、秋丑、冬辰」，正是三丘的对宫，三处一致；周易尚占的四时神杀表也是三丘春丑夏辰秋未冬戌、五墓春未夏戌秋丑冬辰。原文「三冬逢戍」的「戍」是「戌」的误刻，按戌译。暗金煞的口诀没有说前面的寅申巳亥是什么的支（日、月还是年），原文只写「寅申巳亥巳来防」，这里照抄、不补；「羊刃已其在前」按「羊刃已经写在前面」译；「层口是产亡带血之鬼也」的「层口」文字含混，这里译出后面的意思，并在译文里标明这里原文含混。撞命这一段：「年庚」是生年干支，「撞对」是冲或对着它；天对是天干相冲（甲庚、乙辛、丙壬、丁癸），地冲是地支相冲；例子里病人丙子年生、鬼是壬午——壬对丙是天对、午冲子是地冲，两样都有，所以是真撞命；庚午鬼和丙子只有午冲子（庚不对丙），所以是傍撞命；只有天干相对、地支不冲的，不算撞命。「鬼值年建当言太岁」是说鬼落在当年地支上，就当太岁论（不再按撞命论）。与黄金策「太岁鬼临是祖传旧例」（LY.ILL.HJC.109）是同一个落点。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** The verse of the Three Mounds and Five Tombs is found word for word in the chapter on killings of the Buyi Daquan ('spring Chou, summer Chen, autumn Wei, the three winter months meet Xu are the Three Mounds; then the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace, and even if the sick person performs blessing rites he can hardly be kept'); the commentary in the Duanyi Tianji quotes the rule of the Five Tombs killing from Guiguzi as 'spring Wei, summer Xu, autumn Chou, winter Chen', which is exactly the opposite palace of the Three Mounds, so the three agree; the Zhouyi Shangzhan's table of seasonal killings also has the Three Mounds at spring Chou, summer Chen, autumn Wei, winter Xu and the Five Tombs at spring Wei, summer Xu, autumn Chou, winter Chen. The 'xu' in the original 'the three winter months meet Xu' is a miscut of 'xu' (the branch Xu), and is translated as Xu. The verse of the Hidden Metal killing does not say what branch the preceding Yin, Shen, Si and Hai are (of the day, the month or the year), the original only writing 'for Yin, Shen, Si, Hai, guard against Si', and it is copied as written and not supplied; 'the Goat Blade has already been set out' translates a garbled 'yang ren yi qi zai qian'; the 'ceng kou' in 'ceng kou is the bloody ghost of one who died in childbirth' is garbled, and the meaning of what follows is given and the translation marks that the original is garbled here. The collision with life: 'nian geng' is the stem-branch of the birth year, and 'zhuang dui' is a clash with or opposition to it; heaven-opposition is a clash of stems (Jia-Geng, Yi-Xin, Bing-Ren, Ding-Gui) and earth-clash a clash of branches; in the example the sick person was born in the Bingzi year and the ghost is Renwu: Ren opposes Bing (heaven-opposition) and Wu clashes Zi (earth-clash), so both are present and it is a true collision; the Gengwu ghost and Bingzi have only Wu clashing Zi (Geng does not oppose Bing), so it is a side collision; where the stems are opposed but the branches do not clash, it does not count as a collision. 'A ghost on the year's branch is to be called Tai Sui' means that a ghost falling on the branch of the current year is judged as Tai Sui (and no longer as a collision with life). It comes to the same point as the Huangjin Ce's 'the ghost on Tai Sui is an old family custom' (LY.ILL.HJC.109). Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.50、LY.ILL.EL.52、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.YY.05、LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.ZSZ.01

---

## LY.ILL.EL.52 总论星煞值鬼诀(五)：官临华盖（正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑循环）是僧道之魂，鬼值咸池是邪淫之鬼；鬼值天刑（正月起辰逆行十二位）必犯责死之鬼，临天狱煞（正月亥、二月申、三月龙蛇、四月寅循环）是牢中之鬼，临劫煞（与天狱同）或刀砧必自刎而亡

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼临着华盖，就是僧道的魂（华盖：正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑，只在这四位上周而复始；咸池煞已写在前面）。鬼值咸池，就是邪淫的鬼。鬼值天刑煞，必定是犯了被责打死的鬼（天刑：正月从辰上起，逆行十二位）；如果临着天狱煞，就是牢里的鬼（天狱煞：正月遇亥，二月申，三月龙蛇〔即巳〕，四月寅，五月循环又到亥，周而复始定它是哪一路神）；鬼临劫煞，或者刀砧煞，必定是自刎而亡的（劫煞的起法和天狱煞相同，刀砧煞见前）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line sitting on Huagai is the soul of a monk or Daoist (Huagai: the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, cycling round and round through only these four positions; the Xianchi killing has been set out earlier). A ghost on Xianchi is a ghost of lewdness. A ghost on the Heavenly Punishment killing is surely one who offended by dying of flogging (Heavenly Punishment: the first month starts from Chen and runs backward through twelve places); if it sits on the Heavenly Prison killing, it is a ghost in prison (Heavenly Prison killing: the first month meets Hai, the second Shen, the third the dragon or snake [that is, Si], the fourth Yin, the fifth month circles round and reaches Hai again, cycling round and round it fixes which spirit it is); a ghost on the Robbery Killing or the Chopping Block killing is surely one who died by cutting his own throat (the Robbery Killing is set in the same way as the Heavenly Prison killing, the Chopping Block killing as before).

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四·附总论之四:星煞值鬼诀》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1482 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74, appended general discourse 4: ghosts by the stars and killings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1482*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 华盖僧魂并道者， | 华盖，是僧人的魂和道士； | Huagai: the souls of monks and Daoists; |
| 咸池妓女及邪娘。 | 咸池，是妓女和邪淫的女子。 | Xianchi: courtesans and depraved women. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1483 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1483*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 华盖起例： | 华盖的排法： | The rule for setting Huagai: |
| 正月戌、二月未、三月辰、四月丑， | 正月在戌、二月在未、三月在辰、四月在丑， | the first month Xu, the second Wei, the third Chen, the fourth Chou, |
| 只此四位， | 只在这四个位上， | only these four positions, |
| 周而复始。 | 周而复始。 | cycling round and round. |
| 咸池煞开在前， | 咸池煞已经写在前面， | The Xianchi killing was set out earlier; |
| 官临华盖乃为僧道之魂。 | 官鬼临着华盖，就是僧道的魂。 | the Pressure line on Huagai is the soul of a monk or Daoist. |
| 鬼值咸池， | 鬼值咸池， | A ghost on Xianchi |
| 即是邪淫之鬼。 | 就是邪淫的鬼。 | is a ghost of lewdness. |

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四·附总论之四:星煞值鬼诀》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1484 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74, appended general discourse 4: ghosts by the stars and killings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1484*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 天刑牢狱中鬼。 | 天刑，是牢狱中的鬼。 | Heavenly Punishment: the ghost in the prison. |
| 劫煞刀砧自刎伤。 | 劫煞、刀砧，是自刎受伤的。 | Robbery Killing and the Chopping Block: wounded by cutting one's own throat. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1485 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1485*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 天刑起例： | 天刑的排法： | The rule for setting Heavenly Punishment: |
| 正月从辰上起， | 正月从辰上起， | the first month starts from Chen, |
| 逆行十二位。 | 倒着行十二位。 | and runs backward through twelve places. |
| 天狱煞云： | 天狱煞的口诀说： | The Heavenly Prison killing says: |
| 正月逢亥二月申， | 正月遇到亥、二月遇到申， | the first month meets Hai, the second Shen, |
| 三月龙蛇四月寅， | 三月龙蛇、四月寅， | the third the dragon or snake, the fourth Yin, |
| 五月循环又到亥， | 五月循环又到亥， | the fifth month circles round and reaches Hai again, |
| 周而复始定其神。 | 周而复始，定它是哪一路神。 | cycling round and round it fixes which spirit it is. |
| 劫煞兴天狱煞同。 | 劫煞的起法和天狱煞相同。 | The Robbery Killing rises the same as the Heavenly Prison killing. |
| 刀砧煞亦列在前： | 刀砧煞也已经写在前面： | The Chopping Block killing has also been set out earlier: |
| 鬼值天刑煞， | 鬼值天刑煞， | a ghost on the Heavenly Punishment killing |
| 必犯责死之鬼， | 必定犯了被责打死的鬼； | is surely one who offended by dying of flogging; |
| 如临天狱杀， | 如果临着天狱煞， | if it sits on the Heavenly Prison killing, |
| 乃牢中之鬼， | 就是牢中的鬼； | it is a ghost in prison; |
| 鬼临劫杀或刀砧煞， | 鬼临着劫煞，或者刀砧煞， | a ghost on the Robbery Killing or the Chopping Block killing |
| 必定自刎而亡。 | 必定是自刎而亡的。 | is surely one who died by cutting his own throat. |

> **按**:华盖是每次退三位的四位循环（戌、未、辰、丑），咸池已在 LY.ILL.EL.49 写了。天刑是从正月的辰起、每月退一位，走十二个月（和官符、刀砧、天河相反的方向）；断易天机的注释引鬼谷子的刑煞例（天刑）也是「从正月起辰，逆行」，一致。天狱煞「三月龙蛇」：龙是辰、蛇是巳，原文没有定是哪一个；按前后是每次退三位（亥、申、巳、寅），这里应是巳，易冒讲诸星一章注里引的同一首口诀写的是「三月随蛇」，是巳，所以译文里写作「the dragon or snake」并在这里说明。「劫煞兴天狱煞同」按字面译，不另作展开；歌诀「天刑牢狱中鬼」少了一个字（应是「天刑天狱牢中鬼」），这里按残句直译，「自刎伤」译作「wounded by cutting one's own throat」。「责死」是被责打（杖责）而死。与易隐「鬼带天刑天狱牢狱鬼」「鬼加刀砧羊刃劫杀自刎鬼」「鬼带华盖孤神僧道绝嗣鬼」（LY.ILL.YY.29）、易冒「天狱笞杖徒流之鬼」「华盖僧道咸池娼妓」（LY.ILL.YM.62）同一路。 书里没有说这里的「正月、二月」是起卦的月还是别的月，这里照抄；「只此四位周而复始」是说五月回到正月的位置、九月也回到正月的位置，依此类推。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** Huagai is a cycle of four positions each moving back three places (Xu, Wei, Chen, Chou), and Xianchi was already written in LY.ILL.EL.49. Heavenly Punishment starts from Chen in the first month and moves back one place a month through the twelve months (the opposite direction to the Official Talisman, the Chopping Block and the Heavenly River); the commentary in the Duanyi Tianji quotes the rule of Guiguzi for the punishing killing (Heavenly Punishment) as 'starting from Chen in the first month and running backward', which agrees. In the Heavenly Prison killing's 'the third month the dragon or snake', the dragon is Chen and the snake is Si, and the original does not fix which; the series moves back three places each time (Hai, Shen, Si, Yin), so here it should be Si, and the same verse quoted in the note of the Yi Mao's chapter on the stars reads 'the third month follows the snake', which is Si, so the translation writes 'the dragon or snake' and this is explained here. 'The Robbery Killing rises the same as the Heavenly Prison killing' is translated literally and not expanded; the verse 'Heavenly Punishment, the ghost in prison' is short of a character (it should be 'Heavenly Punishment and Heavenly Prison, the ghost in prison'), and is translated as the broken line stands, 'wounded by cutting one's own throat' translating 'zi wen shang'. 'Ze si' is dying by being flogged (beaten with the rod). It is on the same path as the Yi Yin's 'a ghost carrying Heavenly Punishment, Heavenly Prison and Earthly Prison is a jail ghost', 'a ghost with the Chopping Block, Goat Blade and Robbery is a throat-cutting ghost' and 'a ghost carrying Huagai and the Lone Spirit is a ghost of monks and Daoists with no heir' (LY.ILL.YY.29), and the Yi Mao's 'Heavenly Prison is a ghost of flogging, beating, penal servitude and exile' and 'Huagai is monks and Daoists, Xianchi is courtesans' (LY.ILL.YM.62). The book does not say whether the 'first month' and so on are the months of the divination or of something else, and here it is copied as written; 'only these four positions, cycling round and round' means that the fifth month returns to the position of the first and the ninth to that of the first, and so on. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.51、LY.ILL.EL.53、LY.ILL.EL.49、LY.ILL.EL.25、LY.ILL.YM.62、LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.HJC.103

## LY.ILL.EL.53 星煞值鬼诀结语：此是玄机真妙诀、千金不换不可乱传；古时正统各处相同、今时邪祟各按本方；此章系苕城新著，别郡神司不同，吴下人可依此断，在他方问卜须另行细查

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:这套用星煞认鬼神的办法，是玄机里真正妙的诀窍，千金也不换，不要乱传。古时候正的（正统的）大概全天下都一样，现在的邪祟却各按本地不同；这一章是苕城新写的，别的郡的神司不同，凡是吴下的人，可以照这个来断，如果在别的地方问卜，还应该另外详细查。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** This way of recognising ghosts and spirits by the stars and killings is the truly marvellous secret of the mysterious mechanism, not to be traded for a thousand pieces of gold, and not to be spread about. In antiquity the orthodox ones were, on the whole, the same all under heaven, but now evil hauntings each follow their own locality; this chapter was newly written at Tiaocheng, and the gods and offices of other prefectures differ, so that for all people of Wu one may judge by this, and if divining in another place, one should look into it separately in detail.

**《易林补遗·搜决神鬼章第七十四·附总论之四:星煞值鬼诀》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1486 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 74, appended general discourse 4: ghosts by the stars and killings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1486*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此是玄机真妙诀， | 这是玄机里真正的妙诀， | This is the true marvellous secret of the mysterious mechanism, |
| 千金不换乱传扬。 | 千金也不换，不要乱传播。 | not to be traded for a thousand pieces of gold; do not spread it about. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1487 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1487*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 古之正人盖同天下， | 古时候正的大概全天下相同， | In antiquity the orthodox ones were, on the whole, the same all under heaven; |
| 今时邪祟各按本方， | 现在的邪祟各按本地不同； | nowadays evil hauntings each follow their own locality; |
| 此章系苕城新著， | 这一章是苕城新写的， | this chapter was newly written at Tiaocheng; |
| 其别郡神司不同， | 别的郡的神司不同， | the gods and offices of other prefectures differ; |
| 凡吴下人， | 凡是吴下的人， | for all people of Wu, |
| 可依此断， | 可以照这个来断； | one may judge by this; |
| 如他方问卜， | 如果在别的地方问卜， | if divining in another place, |
| 还宜另详。 | 还应该另外详细查。 | one should look into it separately in detail. |

> **按**:这是整个「搜决神鬼」一章和它的四段「总论」的适用范围声明：这套神名和星煞，书里自己说是苕城新著、只适用吴下（别的郡的神司不同，别的地方问卜要另外详查）。所以把它们当作通用规则用，是超出了书自己划定的范围。「古之正人盖同天下」的「正人」文字含混，很可能是「正神」（和下句「邪祟」相对），这里按「正统的」译；「苕城」按字面译作 Tiaocheng（苕溪一带的城）。「玄机真妙诀，千金不换乱传扬」是歌诀的结尾，收的是本节的星煞诀。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，是吴地的民间神祇和星煞，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the book's statement of the scope of the whole 'searching out ghosts and spirits' chapter and its four sections of 'general discourse': the book says itself that these names of gods and these stars and killings are newly written at Tiaocheng and apply only to Wu (the gods and offices of other prefectures differ, and in other places one must look into it separately in detail). To use them as general rules therefore goes beyond the scope the book itself draws. The 'zheng ren' in 'the orthodox ones of old' is loose in wording and is most likely 'zheng shen' (orthodox gods, against the 'evil hauntings' of the next line), and is translated as 'the orthodox ones'; 'Tiaocheng' is rendered literally as the town on the Tiao River. 'The true marvellous secret of the mysterious mechanism, not to be traded for a thousand pieces of gold' is the verse that closes, and it closes this section of the stars and killings. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; they are cults and stars of the Wu region that the book does not explain, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.52、LY.ILL.EL.54、LY.ILL.EL.43

---

## LY.ILL.EL.54 解禳通用法：病人占问鬼神，术人妄判神司、病家听信宰杀，倾家资而病未必愈、又造来生恶业，占卜的人也难逃；按病症请僧道持经、不宰杀——瘟疫合门斋素请戒僧、疟疾设灶神、胎产诵玉枢经、怪病念观音经、冤业梁皇职、亡魂地藏经、火殃诵火德经、精魅请天师或诵真武经、膈噎施食、屠宰索命戒杀放生、妄罚誓愿斋祭勾消或叩城隍回缴、动土告土设坛、覆宗绝嗣改修、侵占祠宇修复

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:病人占问是哪路鬼神的时候，术士随口乱判是哪位神司，病家听信了就宰杀牲畜，把有限的家财倾尽，病未必能好，还给来生造下恶业，连占卜的人也难免。所以解禳按病症来，请僧道念经、斋戒，不用宰杀：瘟疫是上帝降下的，全家一起吃素，虔诚地请戒行严的僧人持经，自然消解，或者请童男礼拜也能得吉；疟疾或有鬼邪，依通书的样式设灶神就能送走；胎产是九天圣母管的，请道士诵玉枢经；怪病、倚草附木的妖，请僧人念观音经；冤业相寻，用梁皇忏（原文作「梁皇职」）解；亡魂出现，用地藏经超度；火殃降下，选火闭日或火收日，请道士诵火德经；精魅所临，大的告天师请法，小的诵真武经驱走；得膈噎症的人，布施食物、吃淡口，能超度饿鬼；屠宰索命的，只有戒杀放生才能免；妄罚誓愿、冒犯天曹的，要设斋祭奠，勾消旧账，穷得还不起愿的可以向天曹递疏、叩城隍退回，富家许过猪羊牛的愿，如今穷了可以做粉牲、纸钱纸马，到天库地库勾消也行；动土兴工出了事要告土，按那个方位设坛，没力量的用粉团斋殷太岁；经商、江湖上的诵三官经，也保读书人的功名；持玉皇经、虔诚祝告药师佛求今世的延年；金刚经作来生的福利；求嗣立梓童清坛，婴幼要念大洞尊经；毁平坟墓、社坛的，不是求神能除，只有改修才能解；侮慢神明的要皈依三宝或许能散；侵占祠宇的只有修复，旧庙才得安全。易卦分类的是万物之情，不能全部备写，卜筮通神明之德，可以自己参详。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When a sick person divines about which ghost or spirit it is, the diviners wrongly judge the gods and offices at random, the sick household believes them and slaughters animals, exhausting its limited household means; the illness is not necessarily cured, and evil karma is made for the next life, which even the diviner can hardly escape. So exorcism follows the illness, inviting monks and Daoists to recite scriptures and keep fasts, without slaughter: epidemic is sent down by the Supreme Emperor, and the whole household eats vegetarian together and reverently invites a monk who keeps the precepts to recite the scripture, and it dissolves of itself, or boys performing worship can also bring good fortune; malaria, or where there are ghosts and evil, can be sent away by setting up the Kitchen God in the form of the almanac; childbirth is presided over by the Holy Mother of the Nine Heavens, and a Daoist is invited to recite the Yushu Scripture; for strange illnesses and demons that lean on grass and cling to trees, a monk chants the Guanyin Scripture; where grievances and karma pursue each other, the Liang Emperor rite (the text has 'Liang Emperor office') resolves it; where a dead soul appears, the Earth Store Scripture delivers it; if a fire calamity descends, choose a Fire Close day or a Fire Receive day and invite a Daoist to recite the Fire Virtue Scripture; where sprites have come, if great, appeal to the Heavenly Master to request rites, and if small, recite the Zhenwu Scripture to drive them away; one with a choking obstruction of the gullet gives food and keeps a bland mouth, which can deliver the hungry ghosts; where slaughtered animals demand a life, only abstaining from killing and releasing life can avoid it; those who rashly swore oaths and vows and offended the Heavenly Bureau must set a fast and offering to cancel the old debt, and one too poor to redeem the vow can present a memorial to the Heavenly Bureau and kowtow to the City God to return it, and a rich family that vowed pigs, sheep and oxen and is now poor can make flour animals, paper money and paper horses, and cancelling at the Heavenly Treasury and Earthly Treasury will do; when digging and building bring trouble, report to the Earth and set up an altar according to that quarter, and those without means offer flour balls as a fast to Tai Sui Yin; merchants and those on rivers and lakes recite the Three Officials Scripture, which also protects scholars' careers; reciting the Jade Emperor Scripture and reverently praying to the Medicine Buddha seeks long life in this world; the Diamond Sutra makes blessings for the next life; to seek an heir set up a pure altar to Zitong, and for infants recite the Great Cavern Honoured Scripture; where tombs and earth altars have been levelled, praying to the gods cannot remove it, and only rebuilding can resolve it; those who have insulted the gods must take refuge in the Three Jewels and may perhaps be dispersed; for encroaching on a shrine, only restoration, and then the old temple is safe. The hexagrams of the Yi classify the natures of all things and cannot all be written out in full; divination communicates with the virtue of the spirits, and one may examine it for oneself.

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1491–1493 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 1491–1493*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡人疾病占问鬼神，术人妄判神司，病家听信宰杀，倾有限之家资， | 凡是人得了病、占问鬼神的，术士随口乱判是哪位神司，病家听信了就宰杀牲畜，把有限的家财都倾尽了， | When people fall ill and divine about ghosts and spirits, the diviners wrongly judge the gods and offices at random, the sick household believes them and slaughters animals, exhausting its limited household means, |
| 病未必愈，造来生之恶业，卜亦难逃。 | 病未必好，还给来生造下恶业，占卜的人也难逃。 | the illness is not necessarily cured, evil karma is made for the next life, and the diviner too can hardly escape it. |
| 且如瘟疫，系上帝之了剌降，虽日时行亦不善而降殃， | 就说瘟疫，这是上帝降下的了剌（此处原文含混），虽然叫时行，也是因为不善才降下灾殃； | Take epidemic: it is a descent from the Supreme Emperor, the 'liao la' (the text is garbled here), and though called seasonal, it too descends as calamity because of what is not good; |
| 但当合门齐素虔请戒僧持通连经，自得消解。或童男礼拜亦可迪吉。 | 只应全家一起吃素，虔诚地请守戒的僧人持通连经，自然消解，或者请童男礼拜也能引来吉祥。 | one should have the whole household eat vegetarian together and reverently invite a monk who keeps the precepts to recite the Tonglian Scripture, and it dissolves of itself, or boys performing worship can also lead to good fortune. |
| 又如虐疾或有鬼邪，依通书之状式设灶神而可遣。 | 又如疟疾，或者有鬼邪，依通书的样式设灶神就能送走。 | Again, malaria, or where there are ghosts and evil: following the forms of the almanac, set up the Kitchen God and it can be sent away. |
| 胎产乃九天圣母所持，延道而诵玉枢经可致临盆之废。 | 胎产是九天圣母管的，请道士诵玉枢经，可以除去临盆的难处（此处文字含混）。 | Childbirth is presided over by the Holy Mother of the Nine Heavens; invite a Daoist to recite the Yushu Scripture, which can remove the trouble of labour (the wording is loose). |
| 怪病或倚草附木之妖，择僧而念观音经，自是弭灾之法。 | 怪病，或者倚草附木的妖，选僧人念观音经，这自然是平息灾祸的办法。 | For strange illnesses, or the demons that lean on grass and cling to trees, choose a monk to chant the Guanyin Scripture; this is naturally the way to still calamity. |
| 冤业相寻，梁皇职可以解，除亡魂出现，地藏经乃能超度。 | 冤业互相寻找，梁皇忏（原文作「梁皇职」）可以解；有亡魂出现，地藏经才能超度。 | Where grievances and karma pursue each other, the Liang Emperor rite (the text has 'Liang Emperor office') can resolve it; where a dead soul appears, the Earth Store Scripture can deliver it. |
| 火殃若降，择火闭日或火收日，延道诵火德经，火乍汝矣！ | 火灾如果降下，选火闭日或者火收日，请道士诵火德经，（下面「火乍汝矣」原文含混）。 | If a fire calamity descends, choose a Fire Close day or a Fire Receive day and invite a Daoist to recite the Fire Virtue Scripture (the words that follow, 'huo zha ru yi', are garbled). |
| 精魅所临，大则告天师而请法，小则诵真武经而驱遣。 | 精魅来临，大的要告天师请法，小的诵真武经驱赶。 | Where sprites and spectres have come, if great, appeal to the Heavenly Master to request rites, and if small, recite the Zhenwu Scripture to drive them away. |
| 症患膈噎者，惟施食淡口，能超饿鬼。 | 得膈噎症的，只要布施食物、吃淡口，能超度饿鬼。 | For one whose illness is a choking obstruction of the gullet, only give food and keep a bland mouth, which can deliver the hungry ghosts. |
| 之途屠宰索命者，独戒杀放生可免，旁生之趣， | 屠宰索命的这一路，只有戒杀放生才能免，这是旁生一趣的事。 | For the path of slaughter where the victims demand a life, only abstaining from killing and releasing life can avoid it; it is a matter of the animal realm. |
| 又若妄罚誓愿，忤犯天曹，须设斋祭旧而勾消。 | 又如妄罚誓愿、冒犯天曹的，必须设斋祭奠，勾消旧账。 | Again, those who rashly swear oaths and vows and offend the Heavenly Bureau must set a fast and offering to cancel the old debt. |
| 若贫难酬愿，可将天曹对疏，叩城隍而回缴， | 如果穷得难以还愿，可以向天曹递上对疏，叩城隍退回； | If too poor to redeem the vow, one may present a memorial to the Heavenly Bureau and kowtow to the City God to return it; |
| 或富家向许猪羊牛愿后，若贫穷可作粉牲钱马，到天库地库除消亦可。 | 或者富家向神许过猪羊牛的愿，后来穷了，可以做粉牲、纸钱纸马，到天库地库勾消也行。 | or a rich family that vowed pigs, sheep and oxen to the gods and has since grown poor can make flour animals, paper money and paper horses, and cancelling at the Heavenly Treasury and Earthly Treasury will also do. |
| 杜绝兴工动作告土。须按其方隅设坛，无力粉圆可斋殷太岁。 | 要杜绝动土兴工惹出的事，要向土地报告，必须按那个方位设坛，没有力量的用粉团斋祭殷太岁。 | To stop the trouble from starting building work and digging, report to the Earth, and one must set up an altar according to that quarter; those without means offer flour balls as a fast to Tai Sui Yin. |
| 商贾江湖诵三官经，而可保士子功名， | 经商的、走江湖的诵三官经，也能保读书人的功名； | Merchants and those on rivers and lakes recite the Three Officials Scripture, which can also protect scholars' careers; |
| 持玉皇经而虔祝药师佛祈当世之延年，金刚经作来生之福利。 | 持玉皇经，虔诚祝告药师佛，祈求今世延年；金刚经作来生的福利。 | reciting the Jade Emperor Scripture and reverently praying to the Medicine Buddha seeks long life in this world; the Diamond Sutra makes blessings for the next life. |
| 祷求嗣续，则建梓童清坛保安，婴幼须念大洞尊经， | 祷求有后，就建梓童清坛保平安；婴幼儿必须念大洞尊经。 | To pray for an heir, one builds a pure altar to Zitong for safety; for infants one must recite the Great Cavern Honoured Scripture. |
| 至如水火不通或拆桥断路而招谴、左右瘫痪或大秤小斗而生灾。 | 至于堵塞水火、拆桥断路而招来谴责，左右瘫痪或者大秤小斗而生出灾祸的，（此处原文没有说办法） | As for those who block water and fire or demolish bridges and cut roads and so invite reproach, those with paralysis of left and right, or those who use large scales and small measures and so bring on calamity (the text gives no remedy here), |
| 士庶覆宗盖为毁平冢墓军民绝嗣皆因起灭，社坛非求神而可除，惟改修而可解， | 士人百姓宗族覆灭，大多是毁平了坟墓；军民绝嗣，都是因为「起灭」（此处含混）；社坛不是求神能除的，只有改修才能解； | the overturning of a lineage among gentry and commoners is mostly from levelling tombs, and the lack of heirs among soldiers and people is all from 'starting and destroying' (garbled here); an earth altar cannot be removed by praying to the gods, and only rebuilding can resolve it; |
| 若艺侮神明，须皈依三宝或能求散。 | 如果侮慢神明，必须皈依三宝，或许能求得消散。 | if one has insulted the gods, one must take refuge in the Three Jewels and may perhaps seek dispersal. |
| 浸占祠宇惟修复，故刹方得安全， | 侵占祠宇的，只有修复，旧庙才能安全。 | For encroaching on a shrine, only restoration, and then the old temple is safe. |
| 易卦类万物之情因不悉备卜筮通神明之德，自可参详。 | 易卦分类万物的情状，不能全部写备，卜筮通神明的德，自然可以自己参详。 | The hexagrams of the Yi classify the natures of all things and cannot all be written out in full; divination communicates with the virtue of the spirits, and one may examine it for oneself. |

> **按**:这一节是「总论」的第五段（编号「之五」），也是整个搜决神鬼章的收尾：前面教人怎么认是哪路鬼神，这里教人认了以后怎么办——不杀生，按病症请僧道念经斋戒。开头一句话点明立场：术人乱判神司、病家听信宰杀，既花光家产、病未必好、又造来生恶业，连占卜的人也难免。和本章结语「恐人妄断，恒忧好杀而费财」（LY.ILL.EL.43）、易冒「祭祀莫善于解禳，解禳莫善于修身」（LY.ILL.YM.63、YM.64）是同一个态度。文字多处含混，译文里都标了：「了剌降」不成词；「持通连经」的「通连经」不知是哪部经；「虐疾」是「疟疾」（疟疾写作虐疾）；「可致临盆之废」的「废」字意思不明，译文按「可以除去临盆的难处」的大意；「梁皇职」应是「梁皇忏」（梁皇宝忏，超度冤业的忏法），原文写作「职」；「火乍汝矣」不成句，译文只标出；「之途屠宰索命者」的「之途」疑是「六道」之类，前面文字缺失，这里译作「屠宰索命的这一路」；「旁生之趣」是佛家说的「旁生趣」（畜生道）；「殷太岁」按字面译作 Tai Sui Yin；「梓童」「大洞尊经」按字面音译；「至如水火不通…而生灾」一句列了几种招祸的行为，书里没有接着写办法，「起灭」「艺侮」也不成词，译文里都直接标明。「火闭日、火收日」按字面译作「Fire Close day」「Fire Receive day」，应是建除十二神里的闭日、收日，书里没有解释。「易卦类万物之情，因不悉备」是说本书没有把万物的解禳全部写出，读的人可以自己参详。 这一段里神、经、法事的名字，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This is the fifth section of the 'general discourse' (numbered 'five'), and it closes the whole 'searching out ghosts and spirits' chapter: the earlier parts teach how to tell which ghost or spirit it is, and this teaches what to do once one knows: not to kill, but to invite monks and Daoists to recite scriptures and keep fasts according to the illness. The opening sentence states the position: diviners wrongly judging the gods and offices and sick households believing them and slaughtering animals both use up the household's means, may not cure the illness, and make evil karma for the next life, which even the diviner can hardly escape. It is the same attitude as this chapter's closing passage 'fearing people will judge at random, always worrying that they love killing and waste money' (LY.ILL.EL.43) and the Yi Mao's 'of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself' (LY.ILL.YM.63, YM.64). The wording is loose in many places, and the translation marks them: 'liao la jiang' is not a word; the 'Tonglian Scripture' is not known to be any particular scripture; 'nue ji' is the sickness 'nue ji' (malaria), written with a variant character; the character 'fei' in 'can bring about the fei of labour' has an unclear sense, and the translation gives the general sense 'can remove the trouble of labour'; 'Liang Emperor office' should be 'Liang Emperor repentance' (the Liang Emperor precious repentance, a rite to deliver karmic grievances), the original writing 'office' for 'repentance'; 'huo zha ru yi' is not a sentence and is only marked; the 'zhi tu' in 'zhi tu of slaughter, demanding life' is probably 'the six paths' or the like with earlier words missing, and is translated as 'the path of slaughter where the victims demand a life'; 'the animal realm' is the Buddhist 'pang sheng qu' (the realm of animals); 'Tai Sui Yin' is rendered literally; 'Zitong' and 'Great Cavern Honoured Scripture' are transliterated literally; the sentence 'as for those who block water and fire ... and bring on calamity' lists several kinds of conduct that call down misfortune but the book does not go on to give a remedy, and 'starting and destroying' and 'yi wu' are not words either, and the translation marks all of these directly. 'Fire Close day' and 'Fire Receive day' are literal, and are probably the Close and Receive days of the twelve day-spirits, which the book does not explain. 'The hexagrams of the Yi classify the natures of all things, and so cannot all be written out' says that this book has not written out the exorcism of all things in full, and the reader may examine it for himself. Names of gods, spirits, scriptures and rites in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; the book does not explain them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.53、LY.ILL.EL.55、LY.ILL.EL.43、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.YM.64、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

## LY.ILL.EL.55 请僧道看应爻：以应爻为主、福德为凭；应上子孙（福德）是受戒善士，父母是法术精专，官鬼是神鬼钦服；兄弟是奸欺伪妄多阻误，妻财是贪利好色、非通天之流；子孙临白虎是恶辈，福德带青龙是善人

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:请僧接道是为了招请贤者，要看的是应爻上站着什么。以应爻为主，以福德（子孙）为凭。应爻上是子孙（福德），这个僧道确实是受了戒、有行持的善人；父母临着应，法术精深专一；官鬼临着应，神鬼都敬服；兄弟临着应，多奸诈虚伪、常常阻误；妻财临着应，是贪婪图利、好色营财的人，不是能通天彻地的人。子孙临白虎，是恶人，心里容不下凶险；福德带青龙，是善人，有慈善，心里常存德行。占卦只看应爻，就知道休咎。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To invite monks and Daoists in order to summon the worthy, look at what stands on the Other line. The Other line is the main line, and Output (the fortune spirit) is the ground. Output on the Other line: he is a good man who has received the precepts and has real conduct; Resource on it: his rites and arts are refined and exclusive; Pressure on it: gods and ghosts admire and submit; Peer on it: crafty, deceitful and false, and often blocking and erring; Wealth on it: a man greedy for profit, lustful and scheming for wealth, not one of those who penetrate heaven and earth. Output on the White Tiger is a wicked man; Output (the fortune spirit) with the Azure Dragon is a good man, compassionate, whose heart always keeps virtue. In divining, look only at the Other line, and one knows good and ill.

**《易林补遗·僧道贤愚章第七十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1497 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 75: The worth of monks and Daoists", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1497*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以应爻为主，福德为凭。 | 以应爻为主，以福德为凭。 | The Other line is the main line, and Output (the fortune spirit) is the ground. |

**《易林补遗·僧道贤愚章第七十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1498 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 75: The worth of monks and Daoists", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1498*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 迎僧接道招贤者， | 迎请僧人、接待道士是为了招请贤者， | In inviting monks and receiving Daoists to summon the worthy, |
| 却看何人立应中。 | 却要看是什么人站在应爻里。 | look instead at who stands on the Other line. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1499 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1499*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 人生世间， | 人活在世上， | A person living in the world |
| 有念佛行善因果法缘、或祈福寿、或职悔业根， | 有念佛行善、因果法缘的，或者祈求福寿的，或者忏悔业根的， | may have the karmic bonds of reciting the Buddha's name and doing good, or pray for blessing and long life, or repent the roots of karma, |
| 如修斋设法、如炼度书符， | 比如修斋设法、炼度书符， | such as holding fasts and setting up rites, refining and delivering, and writing talismans; |

> **按**:这一章说的是请僧道做法事时，怎么看请来的这位僧道是贤是愚。章首小标是「以应爻为主，福德为凭」：占的是「所请的僧道」这个人，他站在应爻上，所以看应爻和它上面的六亲、六神。和这本书别的章一样，先是歌诀、再是解说；这里的解说文字多处含混，译文里标出。「职悔业根」按上下文读作「忏悔业根」；「慈惠降龙」「有法灵伏虎」用的是僧道降龙伏虎的典故，只按字面译；「以亲污积」不成句；「乃鄙入蝎荤」按「乃鄙人〔嗜〕荤」的大意译，并标含混；「盗助」「欺人机有慈善」都不成句，译文里保留并标出。「福德在时」的福德就是子孙，所以「子孙乃僧道也」和「福德即子孙也」是一个意思：子孙在这里既是福德、又是僧道本人。 这一段里神、经、法事的名字，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This chapter is about how to tell, when inviting monks and Daoists to perform rites, whether the one invited is worthy or foolish. The chapter's subtitle is 'the Other line is the main line, and Output is the ground': what is divined is the monk or Daoist invited, who stands on the Other line, so one looks at the Other line and the six relations and six spirits on it. As in the book's other chapters, verse comes first and then the commentary; the commentary here is loose in many places, and the translation marks them. 'Zhi hui ye gen' is read as 'repenting the roots of karma' from the context; 'the compassionate ones who subdue dragons' and 'the efficacious ones who subdue tigers' use the tale of monks and Daoists subduing dragons and tigers, and are translated only literally; 'yi qin wu ji' is not a sentence; 'nai bi ru xie hun' is translated in the general sense of 'a base man craving meat', and marked as garbled; 'dao zhu' and 'qi ren ji you ci shan' are not sentences, and the translation keeps them and marks them. The 'fortune spirit' in 'when the fortune spirit is present' is Output, so 'Output is the monk or Daoist' and 'the fortune spirit is Output' mean the same thing: Output here is both the fortune spirit and the monk or Daoist himself. Names of gods, spirits, scriptures and rites in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; the book does not explain them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.54、LY.ILL.EL.56、LY.ILL.EL.57

---

## LY.ILL.EL.56 请僧道法事成不成：官绝则神天不纳、六冲则僧道无缘（六冲不用）；子孙冲破则诵经如诳语、应空则礼忏如虚文、无子则忏礼徒然；官爻主设醮之场、无鬼无因果、官空无德无功；子旺则斋坛功德无亏、福德掌醮功程有力；福动化鬼、官来克世则作福反凶（求亲反疏、求荣反辱）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:法事成不成，要看几个关口：官鬼绝，神天不接受；卦是六冲，僧道没有缘分，所以六冲的卦不用。子孙用神管着自家的事，诵经宣偈要念得琳琅悦耳，如果遇到冲破，就只是白诵、成了谎话；僧道所在的应位是占卜人的用神，金经实偈要诚敬地宣扬，如果被冲，就白念、成了虚文；应爻落空，穿着袈裟礼拜也没有用，没有子孙，忏悔礼拜也是徒然。官爻主设醮的场地，没有鬼就没有因果，官空就没有德也没有功；子孙乘旺，斋坛的功德没有亏欠；福德掌管醮事，主事功有力。设斋修善的时候，福动却变成了鬼，就是想亲近反而疏远了；作福祈恩的时候，官鬼来克世爻，就是求荣反而得辱——都是要人感应、用心与天神相合，怎么可以亵渎而不加谨慎呢。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whether the rite succeeds depends on several points: if the Pressure line is cut off, the gods and heaven do not accept; if the hexagram is a Six-Clash, monks and Daoists have no affinity, so a Six-Clash hexagram is not used. Output as the Subject governs the affairs of my household, and reciting scriptures and proclaiming verses must be delivered in ringing tones; if it meets clash or break, the reciting is in vain and becomes lying words; the Other position of the monk or Daoist is the Subject of the one divining, and the golden scriptures and true verses must be proclaimed with sincerity and reverence; if it is clashed, the reciting is wasted and becomes an empty text; if the Other line is Void, what use is the robe and the bowing, and if there is no Output, penance and prostration are in vain. The Pressure line governs the site of the ritual; with no ghost there is no cause and effect, and with the Pressure line Void there is no virtue and no merit; with Output riding strength, the merit of the fast altar has no deficiency; Output holds the power of the ritual, and the work has strength. In holding a fast and cultivating good, if Output moves and changes into a ghost, then seeking closeness one grows distant; in performing blessing rites and praying for grace, if the Pressure line comes to control the Self, one seeks honour and gets shame instead; all of it asks for response and for the heart to accord with the heavenly gods, so how may one profane it without taking care?

| 感格天理昭回， | 感通要合于天理的回应， | and the moving of them must accord with the reply of heavenly principle; |
| 岂可亵渎不加谨慎乎？ | 怎么可以亵渎而不加谨慎呢？ | how may one profane it without taking care? |

> **按**:这一节接上一条（LY.ILL.EL.55）：上一条看请来的僧道是贤是愚，这一条看这场法事能不能成。它的判据基本是熟悉的：官鬼是神天的受纳（官绝则神天不纳）、子孙是诵经念偈的功德（子孙被冲破则徒诵）、应爻是僧道本人（应空则礼忏无益）、六冲是缘分散（六冲不用），福动化鬼、官来克世则作福反凶。「六冲」在这里的意思是「缘散」，和别处的用法（六冲卦病易消，LY.ILL.EL.22）不同——这里是说法事不宜遇六冲。「神天不纳因官绝」的「官绝」是官鬼绝。「鹤憋忏礼」的「鹤憋」不成词，译文里标出；「设旧」疑是「设醮」的误写，译作「holding the ritual」；「修齐」是「修斋」；「鬼煞临斋坛之事」不成句，译文标出；「谄之而不蒙福媚之而反见遭用人感应」一句文字散乱，译文只译出能读的部分。这一节的说理是：要人感应、要用心，所以不能亵渎神明而不谨慎。 这一段里神、经、法事的名字，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This section follows the previous entry (LY.ILL.EL.55): the previous one looks at whether the monk or Daoist invited is worthy or foolish, and this one looks at whether the rite can succeed. Its criteria are mostly familiar: the Pressure line is the acceptance by the gods and heaven (with the Pressure line cut off, the gods and heaven do not accept); Output is the merit of reciting scriptures and verses (with Output clashed or broken the reciting is in vain); the Other line is the monk or Daoist himself (with the Other line Void, penance is of no use); the Six-Clash scatters affinity (the Six-Clash is not used); and with Output moving and changing into a ghost, or the Pressure line controlling the Self, blessing rites bring misfortune instead. 'Six-Clash' here means 'affinity scattered', which differs from its use elsewhere (a Six-Clash hexagram makes an illness easy to dispel, LY.ILL.EL.22): here it says that a rite should not meet the Six-Clash. The 'guan jue' in 'because the Pressure line is cut off' is the Pressure line cut off. 'He bie chan li' contains 'he bie', which is not a word, and the translation marks it; 'she jiu' is probably a slip for 'she jiao' (holding the ritual), and is translated as 'holding the ritual'; 'xiu qi' is 'xiu zhai' (cultivating the fast); 'the ghost killing sits on the affairs of the fast altar' is not a sentence, and the translation marks it; the sentence 'flattering it brings no blessing, fawning on it one instead meets ... response' is scattered in wording, and only the readable part is translated. The reasoning of this section is: one must have response and must have the heart, and so one may not profane the gods without taking care. Names of gods, spirits, scriptures and rites in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; the book does not explain them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.55、LY.ILL.EL.57、LY.ILL.EL.22

## LY.ILL.EL.57 还赛解厄：以福神为主、用象为凭；因灾酬愿寻福德、凶吉再辨用爻是否得令；用弱忌兴灾未退、忌衰用旺患能消；父母动克子则洪恩浅、财动生官则邪魅招；世旺子强官受制则祭神之后别无妖、赛神后保平安、解厄后无咎

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:酬愿还赛、解厄，以福神（子孙）为主，以用象为凭。因为遭了灾要酬愿，就找福德；吉凶还要辨用爻得不得令。用爻弱而忌神兴，灾没有退；忌神衰而用爻旺，病患能消。父母发动克子孙，恩典浅薄承受不住；财爻发动生官鬼，邪魅就招来了。世爻旺、子孙强、官鬼受制，祭神之后就再没有妖祟；世是自己，旺则病必痊愈，子孙是福，强则压住鬼祟——这个强了，别的就必弱，福旺鬼将除，赛神之后保平安，解厄完了没有咎，禳解就吉，请祷就没有殃。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In redeeming vows and lifting calamity, Output (the fortune spirit) is the main line and the Subject image is the ground. Because of a calamity one redeems a vow, so look to Output; for ill or good one must still distinguish whether the Subject line has gained or lost the season. With the Subject weak and the Adversary rising, the calamity has not retreated; with the Adversary weak and the Subject strong, the affliction can dissolve. If Resource moves and controls Output, the great grace is shallow and cannot be borne; if Wealth moves and generates Pressure, evil spirits are invited. With the Self strong, Output strong and the Pressure line held in check, after sacrificing to the gods there is no further evil; the Self is me, and if strong the illness will surely be cured; Output is fortune, and if strong it suppresses the ghostly haunting, and where this is strong the rest must be weak: the fortune strong, the ghost is about to be removed; after giving thanks to the gods peace is assured, when lifting the calamity is done there is no blame, and exorcism then is auspicious and petitioning and praying has no misfortune.

**《易林补遗·还赛解厄章第七十六》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1518 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 76: Redeeming vows and lifting calamity", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1518*

> **按**:章首小标是「以福神为主，用象为凭」——福神就是子孙。这一章说的是「酬愿还赛、解厄」这类事：遭了灾或病去还愿、谢神，怎么看能不能成。这一章的判据是本书常见的一套：子孙（福神）强、官鬼受制，还赛就吉；父母克子孙、财生官，则不吉。「用弱忌兴」「忌衰用旺」和本书前面的用神、忌神读法一致（LY.ILL.EL.22：忌神旺发患多年）；「世旺则病必安痊，子强则压制鬼祟」是把世爻当作自己、子孙当作能压鬼的福。原文「惟子卦无凶，寻福德占爻则吉」的「子卦」按「子孙一卦」译，读法没有把握；「邹眉」是「皱眉」；「无青咎」的「青」字费解，疑是「责」（无责咎），这里保留原字并标出；「洪恩」是大恩（上天的恩典）。这一章没有说「还赛要不要杀牲」，和上一节（LY.ILL.EL.54）的立场并不冲突。 这一段里神、经、法事的名字，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** The chapter's subtitle is 'Output (the fortune spirit) is the main line and the Subject image is the ground' — the fortune spirit is Output. This chapter is about redeeming vows and giving thanks and lifting calamity: how to tell, when one redeems a vow or thanks the gods after a calamity or illness, whether it will succeed. Its criteria are a set common in this book: with Output (the fortune spirit) strong and the Pressure line held in check, redeeming the vow is auspicious; with Resource controlling Output and Wealth generating Pressure, it is not. 'Subject weak and Adversary rising' and 'Adversary weak and Subject strong' are consistent with the book's earlier reading of the Subject and the Adversary (LY.ILL.EL.22: the Adversary strong and moving makes an affliction of many years); 'the Self strong and the illness will surely be cured, Output strong and it suppresses the ghostly haunting' takes the Self as oneself and Output as the fortune that can press down the ghost. In the original 'as long as Output has no ominous sign, look to the divined line where Output sits', 'zi gua' (translated 'Output') is read as the Output hexagram, and the reading is not certain; 'zou mei' is 'zhou mei' (knitted brows); the 'qing' in 'wu qing jiu' is hard to understand and is probably 'ze' (blame), and here the original character is kept and marked; 'hong en' is great grace (heaven's grace). This chapter does not say whether redeeming a vow calls for slaughter, and this does not conflict with the position of the previous section (LY.ILL.EL.54). Names of gods, spirits, scriptures and rites in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; the book does not explain them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.56、LY.ILL.EL.54、LY.ILL.EL.22、LY.ILL.EL.39、LY.ILL.HJC.110、LY.ILL.YY.32、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.YM.64

## LY.ILL.GLJ.01 月将值日·胜光（月建午）占病：怪病气冲天、十生九死；司命溪河伤亡家先作祟宜禳度；午宫卦断大煞临门宜退谢、板木来全替去病

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在午、叫胜光的时候占病：病来得古怪，气往上冲，十个里有九个要死，活下来的也拖得很久；心里结着热，气息紊乱急促，又添疼痛，病情难说。作祟的是司命、溪河一类的神，和横死的亡魂、家里的祖先，要禳解超度。后面「午宫卦断」另有一段：大煞到了门上，适合送走它并还愿答谢；社司和朝鬼拖累病人好几年；板木来齐了替他，病就去了，病人慢慢平安痊愈。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Wu, named Shengguang, divining illness: the illness is strange, the breath surging up to the sky, nine of ten die and the one who lives lingers on; heat knots in the heart, the breath is disordered and hurried, pain is added, and the illness is hard to describe. The ones at work are spirits such as Siming and the streams and rivers, the dead by injury, and the household ancestors, and rites of averting and deliverance are called for. A further passage after 'the verdict for the Wu-palace hexagrams' says: the Great Killer is at the door, and it is fitting to dismiss it and give thanks; the Altar Official and the Court ghosts drag on for years; when the wooden boards have come, complete, to stand in for him, the illness goes and the sick person gradually finds peace and cure.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 118 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 118*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 胜光值日 | 胜光当值的日子 | When Shengguang rules the day |
| （月建值午则知胜光上卦所生） | （月建落在午上，就知道胜光在卦上生出的事。） | (when the Month falls on Wu, know what Shengguang gives rise to in the hexagram) |
| 胜光怪病气冲天 | 胜光当值时得病古怪，气往上冲天； | Shengguang: the illness is strange, the breath surging up to the sky; |
| 十生九死生留连 | 十个里有九个要死，活下来的也久久不好； | of ten, nine die, and the one who lives lingers on; |
| 心中结热气舛急 | 心里结着热，气息紊乱急促； | heat knots in the heart and the breath is disordered and hurried; |
| 更兼助痛病难言 | 再加上疼痛加重，病情难以说清； | pain is added besides, and the illness is hard to describe; |
| 司命溪河求禳度 | 司命、溪河这些神鬼，要去禳解超度； | Siming and the streams and rivers: seek to avert and release them by rites; |
| 伤亡为祸及家先 | 横死的亡魂作祸，还牵连到家里的祖先； | the dead by injury make trouble, and it reaches the household ancestors; |
| 午宫卦断 | 午宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Wu-palace hexagrams: |
| 大煞临门宜退谢 | 大煞到了门上，适合送走它并还愿答谢； | the Great Killer is at the door: it is fitting to dismiss it and give thanks; |
| 社司朝鬼累延年 | 社司和朝鬼，拖累病人好几年； | the Altar Official and the Court ghosts drag on for years; |
| 板木来全替去病 | 板木来齐了替他，病就去了； | the wooden boards come, complete, to stand in for him, and the illness goes; |
| 病人渐渐得安痊 | 病人慢慢得到平安痊愈。 | and the sick person gradually finds peace and cure. |

> **按**:这一条是「论十月将值日要决」十二首里的第一首。十二首的格式一样：先是小标题（某将值日，括号里写月建），然后是一首七言诗，前半写这个月份占病的症状和作祟的是谁，中间插一句「某宫卦断」，后半是这一宫的卦怎么断、怎么禳解。标题写的是「十月将」，实际列了十二将，从午（胜光）排起，到巳（太乙）为止，每将对着一个月（午=五月……巳=四月）；标题说「值日」，括号里写的却是「月建」「某月之事」，所以这里按月份读，书没有说日和月是怎么换算的。  这一首里的几个词书里都没有解释，译文只照字面：「司命」是掌管命运的神（民间也指灶神，书里另有「灶司」）；「伤亡」是横死、受伤而死的亡魂，「家先」是家里的祖先，「大煞」是一种凶煞，「社司」是社（土地神坛）的神，「朝鬼」不知所指；「板木」不知是什么，按上下文像是做法事时用来替身的木板（下一首说「板木未全凶怪梦」，板木没备齐就凶），这是读法，不是书里写的。「气舛急」的「舛」字按上下文读作气息紊乱，「怪病」的「怪」是说病来得古怪、像撞了什么，不是一个病名。「午宫卦断」书里没有说是指哪些卦，译文只写「午宫的卦」。
>
> **Note:** This is the first of the twelve pieces of the 'essentials of the month-generals ruling the day'. All twelve have the same form: a small heading (so-and-so rules the day, with the Month in brackets), then a seven-character verse whose first half gives the symptoms of this month's illness and who is at work, with a line 'the verdict for such-and-such palace hexagrams' in the middle, and a second half on how hexagrams of that palace are judged and how the matter is averted. The title says 'ten month-generals' but twelve are listed, from Wu (Shengguang) to Si (Taiyi), each answering to a month (Wu = the fifth month ... Si = the fourth month); the heading says 'rules the day' but the brackets speak of the Month and of 'the matters of such-and-such month', so the verses are read here by month, and the book does not say how day and month are converted.  Several words in this verse are not explained in the book and are rendered only literally: 'Siming' is the god in charge of fates (in popular religion also the Kitchen God, and the book elsewhere writes 'the Kitchen Official'); 'the dead by injury' are the souls of those who died violently or by wounds, 'the household ancestors' are the family's dead, 'the Great Killer' is a kind of fierce killer, 'the Altar Official' is the spirit of the village altar, and what the 'Court ghosts' are is not known; what the 'wooden boards' are is not known either, and in context they look like boards used as stand-ins in a rite (the next verse says 'when the wooden boards are not complete it is ominous, with strange dreams'), which is a reading and not what the book writes. In 'the breath is disordered and hurried' the character rendered 'disordered' is read from context; the 'strange' of 'strange illness' says the illness comes in an uncanny way, as if something had been run into, and is not the name of a disease. The book does not say which hexagrams 'the Wu-palace hexagrams' are, and the translation says only 'the Wu-palace hexagrams'.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

---

## LY.ILL.GLJ.02 月将值日·小吉（月建未）占病：头痛、三焦热闷腹疼、浑身沉重；墓杀最凶；未宫卦断伤亡死鬼土地作祟、请巫师禳让药方通

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在未、叫小吉的时候占病：头痛，三焦发热发闷，腹中疼痛，全身沉重，难以起身；飞来的墓杀最凶；卯方得的病会绕一圈又回来。「未宫卦断」那一段说：作祟的是横死的亡魂、死去的鬼和土地，让病人说胡话；板木没有备齐，就凶，多怪梦；要请巫师禳解让退，药方才通。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Wei, named Xiaoji, divining illness: headache, heat and oppression in the Triple Burner, pain in the belly, the whole body heavy so that he can hardly rise; the Tomb Killer that flies in is the most ominous; an illness taken in the Mao direction goes around and comes back. The passage 'the verdict for the Wei-palace hexagrams' says: the ones at work are the dead by injury, the dead ghosts and the Earth God, making the patient talk deliriously; if the wooden boards are not complete it is ominous, with strange dreams; a shaman should be asked to perform rites of averting and yielding, and then the medicine will work.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 119 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 119*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 小吉值日 | 小吉当值的日子 | When Xiaoji rules the day |
| （月建值末各小吉上卦所生） | （月建落在未上，名叫小吉，在卦上生出的事。） | (when the Month falls on Wei, named Xiaoji, what it gives rise to in the hexagram) |
| 小吉占病主头痛 | 小吉当值占病，主头痛； | Xiaoji, divining illness: it means headache; |
| 三焦热闷腹疼中 | 三焦发热发闷，腹中疼痛； | the Triple Burner is hot and oppressed, with pain in the belly; |
| 浑身沉重难起例 | 全身沉重，难以起身； | the whole body is heavy and he can hardly rise; |
| 飞来墓杀最为凶 | 飞来的墓杀最凶； | the Tomb Killer that flies in is the most ominous; |
| 卯方得病绕回转 | 在卯方得的病，会绕一圈又转回来； | an illness taken in the Mao direction goes around and turns back; |
| 南北社廊鬼相衡 | 南北方向社庙、廊下的鬼互相抵着； | the ghosts of the altar and the corridor in the south and north face each other; |
| 未宫卦断 | 未宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Wei-palace hexagrams: |
| 伤亡死鬼并土地 | 横死的亡魂、死去的鬼，和土地； | the dead by injury, the dead ghosts, and the Earth God; |
| 作崇令人谵语逢 | 作祟，让人说胡话，遇上 | they make trouble and make the patient talk deliriously, and meeting |
| 板木末全凶怪梦 | 板木没有备齐，就凶，多怪梦； | wooden boards not complete, it is ominous, with strange dreams; |
| 巫师禳让药方通 | 请巫师禳解让退，药方才通。 | have a shaman perform rites of averting and yielding, and the medicine will work. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。这一首里读不顺的地方：「月建值末各小吉」的「末」是「未」、「各」是「名」的误写；「浑身沉重难起例」的「例」按上下文读作倒、卧；「飞来墓杀」「卯方得病绕回转」「南北社廊鬼相衡」书里都没有解释，「相衡」译成「互相抵着」是照字面；「作崇令人谵语逢」最后一个「逢」字和下一句「板木末全凶怪梦」连不上，像是脱了字，译文把它留在句末；「板木末全」的「末」读作「未」（没有备齐），这是读法；「禳让」译成「禳解让退」也是照字面。「三焦」是中医里的三焦（上焦、中焦、下焦），书里没有展开。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. Places in this verse that do not read smoothly: in 'when the Month falls on Wei, named Xiaoji' the book prints the wrong characters for 'Wei' and for 'named', plainly misprints; 'the whole body is heavy and he can hardly rise' has a last character read from context as 'lie down' or 'fall'; the 'flying Tomb Killer', 'an illness taken in the Mao direction goes around and turns back' and 'the ghosts of the altar and the corridor in the south and north face each other' are not explained in the book, and 'face each other' is the literal sense; the final character of 'they make trouble and make the patient talk deliriously, and meeting' does not connect with the next line, 'wooden boards not complete, ominous, strange dreams', and looks as if a character has dropped out, so the translation leaves it at the end of the line; the 'not' of 'boards not complete' is printed with a character that is read as 'not yet' (not provided in full), which is a reading; 'rites of averting and yielding' is again the literal sense. The 'Triple Burner' is the Triple Burner of Chinese medicine (upper, middle and lower), which the book does not expand.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

## LY.ILL.GLJ.03 月将值日·传送（月建申）占病：头痛、手脚皮肤见血光、四肢拘急不思食；灶司引外伤亡、犯冤家咒咀；申宫卦断旺神有愿急须解、醮谢星辰祭土主王

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在申、叫传送的时候占病：头痛，手脚和皮肤上见血光，四肢抽紧，不想吃东西。作祟的是灶司引进来的外面的伤亡鬼、冤家和咒咀他的鬼，牵连到香火和家堂。「申宫卦断」那一段说：旺相的神有愿没还，赶紧要解；再适合许愿保平安，做醮酬谢星辰，在本方位祭土主王。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Shen, named Chuansong, divining illness: headache, bloodshed on the hands, feet and skin, the limbs cramped and no wish to eat. The ones at work are the dead by injury from outside whom the Kitchen Official has drawn in, enemies and the ghosts of those who cursed him, tied to the incense-fire and the family hall. The passage 'the verdict for the Shen-palace hexagrams' says: a strong spirit has a vow outstanding and it must be released quickly; it is also fitting to make a vow to keep him safe, to perform a thanksgiving offering to the stars, and to make offerings to the Earth Lord, the King, in the quarter concerned.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 120 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 120*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 传送值日 | 传送当值的日子 | When Chuansong rules the day |
| （中月名曰傅送上卦以断七月之事） | （申月名叫传送，拿它在卦上断七月的事。） | (the Shen month is called Chuansong; use it with the hexagram to judge the matters of the seventh month) |
| 传送值病主头痛 | 传送当值时得病，主头痛； | an illness under Chuansong means headache; |
| 脚手皮肤见血光 | 手脚、皮肤上见血光； | bloodshed shows on the hands, feet and skin; |
| 四肢拘急不思食 | 四肢抽紧，不想吃东西； | the limbs are cramped and he has no wish to eat; |
| 灶司引透外伤亡 | 灶司引着外面的伤亡鬼进来； | the Kitchen Official draws in the dead by injury from outside; |
| 犯着冤家咒咀鬼 | 冲犯了冤家和咒咀他的鬼； | he has offended enemies and the ghosts of those who cursed him; |
| 勾连香火及家堂 | 牵连到香火和家堂； | it is tied to the incense-fire and the family hall; |
| 申宫卦断 | 申宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Shen-palace hexagrams: |
| 旺神有愿急须解 | 旺相的神有愿没还，赶紧要解； | a strong spirit has a vow outstanding, and it must be released quickly; |
| 再宜立愿保安康 | 再适合许愿，保平安康健； | it is also fitting to make a vow, to keep him safe and well; |
| 醮谢星辰求福利 | 做醮酬谢星辰，求福求利； | perform a thanksgiving offering to the stars and ask for blessing and benefit; |
| 祭设当方土主王 | 在本方位设祭，祭土主王。 | set out offerings in the quarter concerned to the Earth Lord, the King. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。标题括号里的「中月名曰傅送」，「中」是「申」、「傅」是「传」的误写。「灶司引透外伤亡」的「引透」按上下文读作引进来；「咒咀」就是咒诅；「勾连」是牵连；「土主王」书里没有解释，译成「土主王」（当地的土主、那位王），只是字面。「旺神有愿」是说某个旺相的神明有过愿没有还，书里没有说是哪个神；GLJ.11 里有几乎一样的一句。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. In the heading's bracket 'the Shen month is called Chuansong' the character printed for 'Shen' and the one for 'Chuan' are misprints. 'The Kitchen Official draws in' reads the book's 'draws through' as 'draws in' from context; 'cursed' is the book's 'curse'; 'tied to' is 'linked with'; 'the Earth Lord, the King' is not explained in the book and is only the literal sense (the local Earth Lord, that King). 'A strong spirit has a vow outstanding' means that some thriving spirit was promised something that has not been paid; the book does not say which spirit, and GLJ.11 has an almost identical line.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

## LY.ILL.GLJ.04 月将值日·从魁（月建酉）占病：肺经炎如青蛇过竹筒、呕吐食不能通、寒热头疼；酉宫卦断犯天罗地网杀、家先十朝、怪神入宅宜设祭则沉病脱体

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在酉、叫从魁的时候占病：肺经发炎，像青蛇钻过竹筒，节节生忧，药难用上；呕吐，吃下去的东西又翻出来；忽冷忽热、头疼、心神不定；病退了一下，吃药也没有功效。「酉宫卦断」那一段说：冲犯了天罗地网煞，家先和十朝都在里面；怪神进了宅，适合设祭，设了祭，沉重的病就像风一样脱身而去。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on You, named Congkui, divining illness: the lung channel is inflamed, like a green snake passing through a bamboo tube, with worry at every joint and medicine hard to use; he retches and vomits and the food comes back; chills and fever, headache and a restless heart; even when it recedes for a time, taking medicine has no effect. The passage 'the verdict for the You-palace hexagrams' says: he has offended the Heaven's-Net and Earth's-Net Killer, with the household ancestors and the Ten Courts in it; uncanny spirits have entered the house and it is fitting to set out offerings, and then the deep illness leaves the body like the wind.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 121 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 121*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 从魁值日 | 从魁当值的日子 | When Congkui rules the day |
| （月建值如从上卦酉爻主八月事） | （月建落在酉（从魁）上，在卦上看酉爻，管八月的事。） | (when the Month falls on You, Congkui, look at the You line in the hexagram: it governs the matters of the eighth month) |
| 从魁占病肺经炎 | 从魁当值占病，肺经发炎； | Congkui, divining illness: the lung channel is inflamed; |
| 恰似青蛇过竹筒 | 就像青蛇钻过竹筒； | just like a green snake passing through a bamboo tube; |
| 节节生忧难用药 | 节节都是忧患，药难用上； | worry arises at every joint and medicine is hard to use; |
| 嗤呕番食不能通 | 呕吐，吃下的东西又翻出来，通不下去； | he retches and vomits, the food comes back, and nothing passes through; |
| 寒热头疼心不定 | 忽冷忽热，头疼，心神不定； | chills and fever, headache, a restless heart; |
| 近退服药也无功 | 病刚退下去一点，吃药也没有功效； | even when it recedes a little, taking medicine has no effect; |
| 酉宫卦断 | 酉宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the You-palace hexagrams: |
| 犯着天罗地网杀 | 冲犯了天罗地网煞； | he has offended the Heaven's-Net and Earth's-Net Killer; |
| 家先十朝在其中 | 家先和十朝都在里面； | the household ancestors and the Ten Courts are in it; |
| 怪神入宅宜设祭 | 怪神进了宅，适合设祭； | uncanny spirits have entered the house and it is fitting to set out offerings; |
| 沉病脱体去如风 | 沉重的病就脱身而去，像风一样。 | and the deep illness leaves the body like the wind. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。标题括号「月建值如从上卦酉爻主八月事」里的「如从」两字不通，按上下文像是「从魁」写错了，译文照「酉=从魁」读，这是读法。「肺经炎」的「炎」在这本书里别处常是「灾」的误写，但这里和「青蛇过竹筒」「呕」「寒热」放在一起，读作肺经发炎（发热）更顺，仍是读法。「嗤呕」的「嗤」不知本字，按上下文读作呕吐一类；「近退」译成「刚退下去一点」是照字面。「十朝」书里没有解释，下一首说「十朝鬼判宫」，像是阴间十殿一类，译文照写「十朝」，不下定义。「天罗地网煞」是一种凶煞，书里没有解释。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. In the heading's bracket 'when the Month falls on ... the You line governs the matters of the eighth month' the two characters rendered 'on ... Congkui' do not make sense as printed and look like a miswriting of 'Congkui', and the translation reads 'You = Congkui', which is a reading. The 'inflamed' of 'the lung channel is inflamed' is elsewhere in this book often a misprint for 'calamity', but placed beside 'a green snake passing through a bamboo tube', 'retching' and 'chills and fever' it reads better as the lung channel being inflamed (hot), again a reading. The first character of 'retches' is not known as a word and is read from context as retching or vomiting; 'recedes a little' is the literal sense of 'nearly recedes'. The book does not explain 'the Ten Courts', and the next verse says 'the ghost judge's palace of the Ten Courts', which looks like the Ten Courts of the underworld, and the translation writes 'the Ten Courts' without defining them. 'The Heaven's-Net and Earth's-Net Killer' is a kind of fierce killer, not explained in the book.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

## LY.ILL.GLJ.05 月将值日·河魁（月建戌）占病：难安如行船下急滩、烦闷呕逆、头疼作热加寒、骨节拘急；戌宫卦断日中出路骂神鬼、社司咒咀、退西方五墓煞

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在戌、叫河魁的时候占病：难以安稳，像行船冲下急滩；心里烦闷，又呕吐上逆；头疼发热，有时又加上发冷；四肢骨节更加拘紧。冲犯的是十朝的鬼判宫。「戌宫卦断」那一段说：白天在路上骂过神鬼，社司的咒咀名目很多，要把西方的五墓煞退送出去，免得病人的身体重得像山。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Xu, named Hekui, divining illness: he cannot be at ease, like a boat running down a rapid; the heart is vexed and oppressed, with retching and counterflow; headache with fever, sometimes with chills added; the joints of the limbs are more cramped still. What has been offended is the ghost judge's palace of the Ten Courts. The passage 'the verdict for the Xu-palace hexagrams' says: he cursed spirits and ghosts on the road at midday, the curses of the Altar Official come in many kinds, and the Five-Tombs Killer of the west is to be driven back, lest the sick body grow heavy as a mountain.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 122 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 122*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 河魁值日 | 河魁当值的日子 | When Hekui rules the day |
| （月建值戌名河魁主九月之事） | （月建落在戌上，名叫河魁，管九月的事。） | (when the Month falls on Xu, named Hekui, it governs the matters of the ninth month) |
| 河魁占疾主难安 | 河魁当值占病，主难以安稳； | Hekui, divining illness: it means he cannot be at ease; |
| 恰似行船下急滩 | 就像行船冲下急滩； | just like a boat running down a rapid; |
| 心中烦闷又呕逆 | 心里烦闷，又呕吐上逆； | the heart is vexed and oppressed, and there is retching and counterflow; |
| 头疼作热或加寒 | 头疼发热，有时又加上发冷； | headache with fever, or chills added; |
| 四肢骨节更拘急 | 四肢骨节更加拘紧； | the joints of the limbs are more cramped still; |
| 触犯十朝鬼判宫 | 冲犯了十朝的鬼判宫； | he has offended the ghost judge's palace of the Ten Courts; |
| 戌宫卦断 | 戌宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Xu-palace hexagrams: |
| 日中出路骂神鬼 | 白天在路上骂过神鬼； | he cursed spirits and ghosts on the road at midday; |
| 社司咒咀又多端 | 社司的咒咀，名目又多； | the curses of the Altar Official come in many kinds; |
| 番退西方五墓煞 | 要把西方的五墓煞翻转退送出去； | the Five-Tombs Killer of the west is to be turned back and driven off; |
| 免教病体重如山 | 免得病人的身体重得像山。 | lest the sick body grow heavy as a mountain. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。「日中出路骂神鬼」的「日中」译成白天、中午，「出路」译成在路上，都是照字面。「番退」的「番」读作翻，「番退西方五墓煞」译成「翻转退送」，书里没有给做法。「五墓煞」是一种凶煞，书里没有解释。「十朝鬼判宫」见 LY.ILL.GLJ.04 的说明。这一首在原文里和下一首登明挨在同一行里，没有分行。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. 'Midday' for the book's 'in the middle of the day' and 'on the road' for 'goes out on the road' follow the letter. The 'turned back' of 'the Five-Tombs Killer of the west is to be turned back' reads the book's 'turn' as 'overturn', and the book does not give a method. 'The Five-Tombs Killer' is a kind of fierce killer, not explained in the book. For 'the ghost judge's palace of the Ten Courts' see the note to LY.ILL.GLJ.04. In the original this verse and the next, Dengming, run on in the same line without a break.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

---

## LY.ILL.GLJ.06 月将值日·登明（月建亥）占病：颠狂、乱言、寒热头疼、对神见鬼；触犯山司木下、暗昧女伤亡；亥宫卦断半天午酉草野鬼、子时得疾难安好

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在亥、叫登明的时候占病：癫狂，躺在床上乱说话，忽冷忽热、头疼，心里郁结，对着神像看见鬼，怪异得很。冲犯的是山司和木下，还有暗昧的女性伤亡鬼。「亥宫卦断」那一段说：半空的、午酉方位的、野外的鬼，也是家里的二姓郎；子时得的病难以安好，要赶紧保祷，免得惶恐。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Hai, named Dengming, divining illness: madness, babbling in his bed, chills and fever, headache, a knot in the heart, seeing ghosts when facing the spirits, uncanny beyond measure. What has been offended is the Mountain Official and the one under the wood, and there are obscure women dead by injury. The passage 'the verdict for the Hai-palace hexagrams' says: the ghosts of the half-sky, of Wu and You, and of the wild fields, and also the household's 'two-surname lord'; an illness taken at the Zi hour is hard to settle, and he should be quickly prayed for, to spare him distress.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 122 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 122*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 登明值日 | 登明当值的日子 | When Dengming rules the day |
| （月建亥名登明主十月事） | （月建是亥，名叫登明，管十月的事。） | (the Month at Hai is named Dengming and governs the matters of the tenth month) |
| 登明得病主颠狂 | 登明当值得病，主癫狂； | Dengming, falling ill: it means madness; |
| 乱言论语在高床 | 躺在床上乱说话； | he babbles confusedly in his high bed; |
| 寒热头疼心内结 | 忽冷忽热，头疼，心里郁结； | chills and fever, headache, a knot in the heart; |
| 对神见鬼怪非常 | 对着神像看见鬼，怪异得很； | facing the spirits he sees ghosts, uncanny beyond measure; |
| 触犯山司并木下 | 冲犯了山司和木下； | he has offended the Mountain Official and the one under the wood; |
| 更有暗昧女伤亡 | 还有暗昧的女性伤亡鬼； | and there are also obscure women dead by injury; |
| 亥宫卦断 | 亥宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Hai-palace hexagrams: |
| 半天午酉并草野 | 半空的、午酉方位的、野外的鬼； | the ghosts of the half-sky, of Wu and You, and of the wild fields; |
| 亦是家二姓郎 | 也是家里的二姓郎； | and also the household's 'two-surname lord'; |
| 子时得疾难安好 | 子时得的病，难以安好； | an illness taken at the Zi hour is hard to settle; |
| 急宜保忧免栖惶 | 赶紧要保祷，免得忧虑惶恐。 | he should be quickly prayed for, to spare him worry and distress. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。「乱言论语」是乱说话；「木下」「山司」书里没有解释，译文只照字面；「暗昧女伤亡」是说暗中、昏昧的女性伤亡鬼，也是字面；「半天午酉并草野」「二姓郎」不知所指，译文照字面写，不下定义（第 11 首有「家先三五郎」，「郎」像是民间称某类神鬼的叫法，这是推测）；「子时得疾」是说这个病是在子时得的；「保忧」两字不通，译成「保祷」是读法，像是「保佑」「保安」一类，「栖惶」是惶惑不安。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. 'Babbles confusedly' is the book's 'speaks in disorder'; 'the one under the wood' and 'the Mountain Official' are not explained in the book and are only literal; 'obscure women dead by injury' is the sense of women in the dark and the dim who died by injury, again literal; 'the half-sky, Wu and You, and the wild fields' and 'two-surname lord' are not known and are written literally without defining them (verse 11 has 'the household ancestors and the Third and Fifth Lords', and 'lord' looks like a popular name for a kind of spirit, which is a guess); 'an illness taken at the Zi hour' says that this illness was taken at the Zi hour; the two characters of 'guard against worry' do not make sense as printed, and 'prayed for' is a reading, as if it were 'protect' or 'keep safe', while 'distress' is the book's word for being unsettled and perplexed.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

## LY.ILL.GLJ.07 月将值日·神后（月建子）占病：虚惊、寒热、腹中疾痛及肝心、浑身沉重、四肢疼痛；子宫卦断不思饮食又番吐、触犯山神土石精、宜禳土杀神及伤亡五道

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在子、叫神后的时候占病：虚惊，白天得病是鬼来侵犯；寒热时来，很蹊跷；腹中疼痛，牵连肝和心；全身沉重，转不了身；四肢疼痛，不能安宁。「子宫卦断」那一段说：不想吃喝，又翻着吐；冲犯了山神和土石精；手脚疼痛，一动就气喘，适合禳解土杀神；还有伤亡鬼和五道，要祈求、做福事，保平安。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Zi, named Shenhou, divining illness: a false alarm; an illness taken in the daytime is a ghost's invasion; chills and fever come at times and are very odd; pain in the belly, reaching the liver and the heart; the whole body heavy and unable to turn; the limbs ache and cannot settle. The passage 'the verdict for the Zi-palace hexagrams' says: no wish for food or drink, and vomiting again and again; he has offended the Mountain Spirit and the earth-and-stone sprites; the hands and feet ache and movement leaves him short of breath, and it is fitting to perform rites against the Earth-Killer spirits; there are also the dead by injury and the Five Ways, and he should pray and perform blessing rites to keep himself in peace.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 123 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 123*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 神后值日 | 神后当值的日子 | When Shenhou rules the day |
| （月建值子名神后主十一月事） | （月建落在子上，名叫神后，管十一月的事。） | (when the Month falls on Zi, named Shenhou, it governs the matters of the eleventh month) |
| 神后值卦主虚惊 | 神后当值，主虚惊； | when Shenhou rules the hexagram it means a false alarm; |
| 日中得病鬼来侵 | 白天得的病，是鬼来侵犯； | an illness taken in the daytime is a ghost's invasion; |
| 寒热时来远跷蹊 | 寒热不时地来，很蹊跷； | chills and fever come at times, and are very odd; |
| 腹中疾痛及肝心 | 腹中疼痛，牵连到肝和心； | pain in the belly, reaching the liver and the heart; |
| 浑身沉重转不得 | 全身沉重，转不了身； | the whole body is heavy and cannot turn; |
| 四肢疼痛末能安 | 四肢疼痛，不能安宁； | the limbs ache and cannot settle; |
| 子宫卦断 | 子宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Zi-palace hexagrams: |
| 不思饮食又番吐 | 不想吃喝，又翻着吐； | no wish for food or drink, and vomiting again and again; |
| 触犯山神土石精 | 冲犯了山神和土石精； | he has offended the Mountain Spirit and the earth-and-stone sprites; |
| 手足疾疼举动气急 | 手脚疼痛，一动就气喘； | the hands and feet ache and movement leaves him short of breath; |
| 宜禳土杀神 | 适合禳解土杀神； | it is fitting to perform rites against the Earth-Killer spirits; |
| 更有伤亡并五道 | 还有伤亡鬼和五道； | there are also the dead by injury and the Five Ways; |
| 祈求作福保安宁 | 祈求，做福事，保平安。 | pray, perform blessing rites, and keep him in peace. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。「日中得病」的「日中」译成白天；「远跷蹊」的「远」不知本字，按上下文读作很；「四肢疼痛末能安」的「末」读作「未」（不能）；「手足疾疼举动」和「气急宜禳土杀神」在原文里隔着一个空格，像是脱了字，译文把「举动气急」连着读（一动就气喘），这是读法；「土杀神」「五道」「土石精」书里都没有解释，译文只照字面。「神后值卦主虚惊」的「虚惊」是说受惊吓而无事，书里没有展开。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. 'In the daytime' is the book's 'in the middle of the day'; the first character of 'very odd' is not known as a word and is read from context as 'very'; 'cannot settle' reads the book's 'has not yet been able to settle' with the character for 'not yet' standing for 'cannot'; 'the hands and feet ache and movement' and 'short of breath, it is fitting to perform rites against the Earth-Killer spirits' are separated by a space in the original, as if a character had dropped out, and the translation reads 'movement ... short of breath' straight on (a movement leaves him gasping), which is a reading; 'the Earth-Killer spirits', 'the Five Ways' and 'the earth-and-stone sprites' are not explained in the book and are only literal. The 'false alarm' of 'it means a false alarm' says being frightened with no harm to follow, and the book does not expand it.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29

## LY.ILL.GLJ.08 月将值日·大吉（月建丑）占病：胸肋、寒热、大小便不通、不思饭食心中结；伏尸古墓同为祸；丑宫卦断社司五朝入宅、更犯五道伤亡、退北方凶恶杀、禳谢本命星辰

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:月建落在丑、叫大吉的时候占病：胸和肋疼，有时发冷有时发热，大小便都不通，不想吃饭，心里郁结。伏尸和古墓一同作祸，只是家先牵着引着。「丑宫卦断」那一段说：社司、五朝进了宅，要设祭给香火和土地；又冲犯了五道和伤亡鬼，要把北方的凶恶煞退送出去，向本命上的星辰禳解酬谢，化解流年的灾病厄运。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When the Month falls on Chou, named Daji, divining illness: the chest and ribs, now chills and now fever, neither urine nor stool passing, no wish for food, a knot in the heart. The Hidden Corpse and the ancient tomb make trouble together, only that the household ancestors lead them through and receive them. The passage 'the verdict for the Chou-palace hexagrams' says: the Altar Official and the Five Courts have entered the house, and offerings are to be set out to the incense-fire and the Earth God; the Five Ways and the dead by injury have also been offended, the fierce killer of the north is to be driven off, rites and thanks are to be made to the stars of his natal destiny, and the calamity, illness and misfortune of the passing year resolved.

**《断易鬼灵经·卷五·论十月将值日要决》原文** — 《断易鬼灵经》第 124 行 · *Duanyi Guiling Jing (The Ghost-and-Spirit Classic of Judging the Changes), "Volume 5: The essentials of the month-generals ruling the day", base text; Duanyi Guiling Jing, line 124*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 大吉值日 | 大吉当值的日子 | When Daji rules the day |
| （月建值丑名大吉主十二月事） | （月建落在丑上，名叫大吉，管十二月的事。） | (when the Month falls on Chou, named Daji, it governs the matters of the twelfth month) |
| 大吉占病主胸肋 | 大吉当值占病，主胸和肋； | Daji, divining illness: it means the chest and the ribs; |
| 或作寒来或作热 | 有时发冷，有时发热； | now chills come, now fever; |
| 小便大便已不通 | 大小便都不通； | neither urine nor stool passes; |
| 不思饭食心中结 | 不想吃饭，心里郁结； | no wish for food, a knot in the heart; |
| 伏尸古墓同为祸 | 伏尸和古墓一同作祸； | the Hidden Corpse and the ancient tomb make trouble together; |
| 只是家先透引接 | 只是家先牵着引着接着； | only that the household ancestors lead them through and receive them; |
| 丑宫卦断 | 丑宫的卦这样断： | The verdict for the Chou-palace hexagrams: |
| 社司五朝入宅来 | 社司、五朝进了宅； | the Altar Official and the Five Courts have entered the house; |
| 香火土地求祭设 | 对香火、土地，要设祭； | to the incense-fire and the Earth God, ask for offerings to be set out; |
| 更犯五道及伤亡 | 又冲犯了五道和伤亡鬼； | the Five Ways and the dead by injury have also been offended; |
| 用退北方凶恶杀 | 要把北方的凶恶煞退送出去； | the fierce killer of the north is to be driven off; |
| 禳谢本命上星辰 | 向本命上的星辰禳解酬谢； | make rites and thanks to the stars of his natal destiny; |
| 解释流年灾疾厄 | 化解流年的灾病厄运。 | and resolve the calamity, illness and misfortune of the passing year. |

> **按**:格式和名词见 LY.ILL.GLJ.01。「伏尸古墓」的「伏尸」书里没有解释，按字面是暗藏的尸；「家先透引接」读作家先牵引着、接应着（那些鬼），是读法；「五朝」不知所指，像是和「十朝」（见 GLJ.04）同类的叫法，不下定义；「小便大便已不通」的「已」读作都；「解释」是化解，不是解说。「本命上星辰」是病人本命（出生年）对应的星辰。
>
> **Note:** For the form and the terms see LY.ILL.GLJ.01. The book does not explain 'the Hidden Corpse', which literally is a corpse lying in hiding; 'the household ancestors lead them through and receive them' reads the book's 'lead through and receive' as the ancestors leading and meeting those ghosts, which is a reading; what 'the Five Courts' are is not known, and they look like a kind of thing with the 'Ten Courts' (see GLJ.04), and are not defined; the 'neither' of 'neither urine nor stool passes' reads the book's 'already' as 'both'; 'resolve' is the book's 'release', in the sense of undoing, not of explaining. 'The stars of his natal destiny' are the stars answering to the patient's natal year.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.29
