## LY.ILL.HZL.11 占鬼神·休囚为鬼旺相为神、本象家亲他宫外人；六爻定体：六爻公婆家亲佛道、五爻父母口愿土神、四爻叔伯土神半天、三爻兄弟门户境神、二爻夫妻土地家神、初爻小口灶君司命

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:休囚的是鬼，旺相的是神；在本象里的是家亲，在他宫里的是外人。六爻各有所主：六爻是公婆、家亲、佛道；五爻是父母、口愿、土神；四爻是叔伯、土神、半天；三爻是兄弟、门户、境神；二爻是夫妻、土地、家神；初爻是小口、灶君、司命。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Resting or trapped it is a ghost, strong it is a spirit; in the own image it is the family ancestors, in another palace an outsider. The six lines each govern something: the sixth line the in-laws, the family ancestors and Buddhist and Daoist spirits; the fifth the parents, vows and the Earth spirit; the fourth uncles, the Earth spirit and the half-sky spirit; the third brothers, the doors and the district spirit; the second husband and wife, the Earth God and the household spirit; the first the children, the Hearth Lord and the Life-Governing Spirit.

**《火珠林·占鬼神》原文** — 《火珠林》第 388–389 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining ghosts and spirits", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 388–389*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 休囚为鬼，旺相为神； | 休囚的是鬼，旺相的是神； | Resting or trapped it is a ghost, strong it is a spirit; |
| 本象家亲，他宫外人。 | 在本象里的是家亲，在他宫里的是外人。 | in the own image it is the family ancestors, in another palace an outsider. |
| (1)六爻定体： | （1）六爻各自所定的所主之体： | (1) What each of the six lines fixes as its body: |
| 六爻公婆家亲佛道 | 六爻：公婆、家亲、佛道 | the sixth line: the in-laws, the family ancestors, Buddhist and Daoist spirits |
| 五爻父母口愿土神 | 五爻：父母、口愿、土神 | the fifth line: the parents, vows, the Earth spirit |
| 四爻叔伯土神半天 | 四爻：叔伯、土神、半天 | the fourth line: uncles, the Earth spirit, the half-sky spirit |
| 三爻兄弟门户境神 | 三爻：兄弟、门户、境神 | the third line: brothers, the doors, the district spirit |
| 二爻夫妻土地家神 | 二爻：夫妻、土地、家神 | the second line: husband and wife, the Earth God, the household spirit |
| 初爻小口灶君司命 | 初爻：小口、灶君、司命 | the first line: the children, the Hearth Lord, the Life-Governing Spirit |
| (2)五行鬼： | （2）五行的鬼： | (2) The ghosts of the five phases: |

> **打架**:爻位配鬼神，几本书的配法不同。火珠林这里：初爻小口、灶君、司命；二爻夫妻、土地、家神；三爻兄弟、门户、境神；四爻叔伯、土神、半天；五爻父母、口愿、土神；六爻公婆、家亲、佛道。卜筮全书的六位表（LY.ILL.BSQS.02）：初爻井神、二爻司命、三爻家先、四爻土地、五爻社司、六爻天神。易林补遗（LY.ILL.EL.37）是「六爻上仓至圣坟墓之神、五爻中界东岳、四爻右仪门将军、三爻家堂郡王府城隍、二爻县城隍灶神」这一路。同一位置配的神不同：二爻火珠林是土地、家神，卜筮全书是司命，易林补遗是灶神；初爻火珠林是灶君、司命，卜筮全书是井神；六爻火珠林是佛道，卜筮全书是天神，易林补遗是坟墓之神。各家按各地的习俗立说，书里没有裁决。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Books match line positions to ghosts and spirits differently. The Huozhu Lin here: first line the children, the Hearth Lord, the Life-Governing Spirit; second husband and wife, the Earth God, the household spirit; third brothers, the doors, the district spirit; fourth uncles, the Earth spirit, the half-sky spirit; fifth parents, vows, the Earth spirit; sixth in-laws, family ancestors, Buddhist and Daoist spirits. The table of the six positions in the Bushi Quanshu (LY.ILL.BSQS.02): first line the well spirit, second the Life-Governing Spirit, third the family ancestors, fourth the Earth God, fifth the village altar spirit, sixth the heavenly spirit. The Yilin Buyi (LY.ILL.EL.37) runs along the lines of 'the sixth line the spirit of the high granary and the sage's tomb, the fifth the Eastern Peak of the middle realm, the fourth the general of the right gate, the third the household shrine, the prince and the city god of the prefecture, the second the county city god and the Kitchen God'. The spirit matched to the same position differs: for the second line the Huozhu Lin has the Earth God and the household spirit, the Bushi Quanshu the Life-Governing Spirit and the Yilin Buyi the Kitchen God; for the first line the Huozhu Lin has the Hearth Lord and the Life-Governing Spirit and the Bushi Quanshu the well spirit; for the sixth line the Huozhu Lin has Buddhist and Daoist spirits, the Bushi Quanshu the heavenly spirit and the Yilin Buyi the spirits of the tombs. Each sets it up by the customs of its own place, and the books do not settle it.

> **按**:这一节头两句是总纲：休囚的是鬼（作祟的死者），旺相的是神（正神）；鬼落在本象里是家里的先人（家亲），落在别的宫里是外人。「六爻定体」是一张按爻位配对象的表，原书是一格一格排的：每一爻三样，第一样像是六亲的位置（六爻公婆、五爻父母、四爻叔伯、三爻兄弟、二爻夫妻、初爻小口），后两样是鬼神（家亲、佛道；口愿、土神；土神、半天；门户、境神；土地、家神；灶君、司命），但书里没有说三样各是什么，这里只照原样列出，不分栏。「公婆」是丈夫的父母（或祖父母），「境神」是一方境内的神，「半天」是半天之神（取象于四爻在半空），原文没有解释，只是字面译。表末的「（2）五行鬼：」是下一节的小标题，连在同一行里，所以放在这一条的末尾。这一张表（初灶君司命、二土地家神、三门户境神……）和卜筮全书的六爻诸占定位里的鬼神一行（初井神、二司命、三家先、四土地、五社司、六天神，LY.ILL.BSQS.02）、易林补遗的按爻位断神鬼（LY.ILL.EL.37）是同一类，配法各家不同，见「打架」。
>
> **Note:** The first two sentences of this section are the general outline: resting or trapped it is a ghost (a dead person who brings a curse), strong it is a spirit (a proper god); a ghost falling in the own image is the family's ancestors (family ancestors), and falling in another palace it is an outsider. 'The six lines fix the body' is a table matching line positions to objects, laid out cell by cell in the original: three items to each line, the first seeming to be the position of a relation (the sixth line in-laws, the fifth parents, the fourth uncles, the third brothers, the second husband and wife, the first children), and the latter two being ghosts and spirits (family ancestors, Buddhist and Daoist; vows, the Earth spirit; the Earth spirit, the half-sky spirit; the doors, the district spirit; the Earth God, the household spirit; the Hearth Lord, the Life-Governing Spirit), but the book does not say what each of the three is, and here they are only listed as they stand without separating columns. 'Gong po' are the husband's parents (or grandparents), 'the district spirit' is the spirit of a territory, and 'the half-sky spirit' is the spirit of half-heaven (taking the image of the fourth line as being in mid-air), which the original does not explain, and they are rendered literally. The '(2) the ghosts of the five phases' at the end of the table is the small heading of the next section, run into the same line, and so it is placed at the end of this entry. This table (first Hearth Lord and Life-Governing Spirit, second Earth God and household spirit, third the doors and district spirit ...) is of the same kind as the row for spirits in the table of the positions of the lines in the Bushi Quanshu (first the well spirit, second the Life-Governing Spirit, third the family ancestors, fourth the Earth God, fifth the village altar spirit, sixth the heavenly spirit, LY.ILL.BSQS.02) and the Yilin Buyi's judging spirits by line position (LY.ILL.EL.37), and each puts the matching differently, see the dispute.

相关:LY.ILL.BSQS.02、LY.ILL.EL.37、LY.ILL.HZL.12、LY.ILL.HZL.13、LY.ILL.EL.29

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.12 占鬼神·五行鬼：金木横死、土时疫、火劳血、水落水；八纯卦：艮五圣、震天神、巽木神、离火神、乾功德、坤家将、坎落水、兑口愿

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:五行的鬼：金、木是横死，土是时疫，火是劳血，水是落水。八个纯卦：艮是五圣，震是天神，巽是木神，离是火神，乾是功德，坤是家将，坎是落水，兑是口愿。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The ghosts of the five phases: Metal and Wood are violent death, Earth an epidemic, Fire consumption with spitting blood, Water drowning. The eight pure hexagrams: Gen is the Five Sages, Zhen the heavenly spirit, Xun the tree spirit, Li the fire spirit, Qian merit, Kun the household generals, Kan drowning, Dui vows.

**《火珠林·占鬼神》原文** — 《火珠林》第 390–392 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining ghosts and spirits", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 390–392*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 金木，横死； | 金、木，是横死的鬼； | Metal and Wood: a violent death; |
| 土，时疫； | 土，是时疫； | Earth: an epidemic of the season; |
| 火，劳血； | 火，是劳伤吐血； | Fire: consumption with spitting blood; |
| 水，落水。 | 水，是落水。 | Water: drowning. |
| 八纯卦： | 八个纯卦： | The eight pure hexagrams: |
| 艮，五圣； | 艮，是五圣； | Gen: the Five Sages; |
| 震，天神； | 震，是天神； | Zhen: the heavenly spirit; |
| 巽，木神； | 巽，是木神； | Xun: the tree spirit; |
| 离，火神； | 离，是火神； | Li: the fire spirit; |
| 乾，功德； | 乾，是功德； | Qian: merit and virtue; |
| 坤，家将； | 坤，是家将； | Kun: the household generals; |
| 坎，落水； | 坎，是落水； | Kan: drowning; |
| 兑，口愿。 | 兑，是口愿。 | Dui: vows. |

> **按**:「五行鬼」是鬼爻属哪一行，就是哪一种死者：金木是横死（意外而死）、土是时疫（死于瘟疫）、火是劳血（劳伤吐血）、水是落水（淹死）。「八纯卦」是鬼落在八个纯卦里对应的神：艮五圣、震天神、巽木神、离火神、乾功德、坤家将、坎落水、兑口愿。「功德」是为功德而许的斋供，「家将」是家里供的将军神，「口愿」是口头许下的愿，这三处原文没有解释，是字面加常识的译法。五行鬼的分法和易林补遗的「金鬼配六兽」等（LY.ILL.EL.30 到 EL.34）、易冒的五行值鬼是同一类，各家说法大同小异：金鬼易林补遗说是「刀下之魂、喘咳横亡之鬼」，这里说是横死；火鬼易林补遗是「毒疮痨之魂、带血焚烧之鬼」，这里说是劳血；水鬼两处都是落水一类。
>
> **Note:** 'The ghosts of the five phases' says that whichever phase the ghost line belongs to is the kind of dead person: Metal and Wood a violent death (dying by accident), Earth an epidemic (dying of a plague), Fire consumption (wasting and spitting blood), Water drowning. 'The eight pure hexagrams' gives the spirit matched to a ghost falling in each of the eight pure hexagrams: Gen the Five Sages, Zhen the heavenly spirit, Xun the tree spirit, Li the fire spirit, Qian merit, Kun the household generals, Kan drowning, Dui vows. 'Merit' is a fast offering vowed for merit, the 'household generals' are the general spirits worshipped in the house, and 'vows' are vows spoken aloud; the original does not explain these three, and they are rendered literally with common sense. This way of dividing the ghosts by five phases is of the same kind as the Yilin Buyi's 'a Metal ghost matched to the six beasts' and so on (LY.ILL.EL.30 to EL.34) and the Yi Mao's ghost by five phases, and the books are much alike: for the Metal ghost the Yilin Buyi says 'the soul of one killed by a blade, the ghost of one who died of coughing and panting', where this says violent death; for the Fire ghost the Yilin Buyi says 'the soul of a poisonous sore or consumption, the ghost of burning with blood', where this says consumption with blood; for the Water ghost both say drowning.

相关:LY.ILL.HZL.11、LY.ILL.HZL.13、LY.ILL.EL.30、LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.EL.46

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.13 占鬼神·五乡独发克日克世取之：父母家先、子孙小儿、妻财妇婢、兄弟阳人、官鬼横恶；本象鬼动是家亲、旁爻鬼动是外人；土鬼是当处灵验之鬼、旺相为神休囚为鬼；《易林》察祸推其鬼处

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:五种六亲独发，克日、克世的取它，各有两层意思：父母是家先，子孙是小儿，妻财是妇人婢女，兄弟是阳人（男人），官鬼是横恶，以上随爻取。本象里的鬼动是家亲，旁爻的鬼动是外人；比如乾卦壬午鬼动是家亲，大有卦己巳鬼动是外人。土鬼是当地灵验的鬼，俗称神：旺相是神，休囚是鬼；动爻克世克日，也可以取来断祟。《易林》说：察祸要推它的鬼在什么地方，再拿身去配六亲，相克招生，就看见祸的端的。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When one of the five quarters moves alone, take the one that controls the Day or the Self; each has two senses: Resource is the ancestors of the house, Output the small children, Wealth women and maids, Peer men (yang people), the Pressure line violent evil; the above follow the line. A ghost moving in the own image is the family ancestors, a ghost moving on a side line is an outsider; for example, in Qian a ghost moving on Ren Wu is the family ancestors, and in Dayou a ghost moving on Ji Si is an outsider. An Earth ghost is a ghost efficacious in the place, commonly called a spirit: strong it is a spirit, resting or trapped it is a ghost; a moving line controlling the Self or the Day can also be taken to judge a curse. The Yilin says: to examine a calamity, work out where its ghost is, and then match the Body to the six relations; control brings generation, and the true beginning of the calamity is seen.

**《火珠林·占鬼神》原文** — 《火珠林》第 393–397 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining ghosts and spirits", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 393–397*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 五乡独发，克日克世取之，各有两义： | 五种六亲独发，克日、克世的取它，各有两层意思： | When one of the five quarters moves alone, take the one that controls the Day or the Self; each has two senses: |
| 父母家先，子孙小儿，妻财妇婢，兄弟阳人，官鬼横恶，以上随爻。 | 父母是家先，子孙是小儿，妻财是妇人婢女，兄弟是阳人，官鬼是横恶，以上随爻取。 | Resource is the ancestors of the house, Output the small children, Wealth women and maids, Peer men (yang people), the Pressure line violent evil; the above follow the line. |
| 或问：本象家先，他官外人，何也？ | 有人问：本象是家先、他宫是外人，是什么道理？ | Someone asks: in the own image it is the ancestors of the house and in another palace an outsider, what is the principle? |
| 答曰：本象鬼动是家亲，旁爻鬼动是外人。假如乾卦壬午鬼动是家亲；大有卦己巳鬼动，是外人也。 | 答：本象里的鬼动是家亲，旁爻的鬼动是外人；比如乾卦壬午鬼动是家亲，大有卦己巳鬼动是外人。 | The answer: a ghost moving in the own image is the family ancestors, a ghost moving on a side line is an outsider; for example, in Qian a ghost moving on Ren Wu is the family ancestors, and in Dayou a ghost moving on Ji Si is an outsider. |
| 问：土鬼何也？曰： | 问：土鬼是什么？答： | Asked: what is an Earth ghost? The answer: |
| 此乃当处灵验之鬼，俗谓之神者也，旺相为神，休囚为鬼，动爻克世克日，亦可取祟。 | 这是当地灵验的鬼，俗称为神；旺相是神，休囚是鬼，动爻克世克日，也可以取来断祟。 | this is a ghost efficacious in the place, commonly called a spirit: strong it is a spirit, resting or trapped it is a ghost; a moving line controlling the Self or the Day can also be taken to judge a curse. |
| 《易林》云：察祸推其鬼处，还将身配六亲，相克招生，便见祸之端的。 | 《易林》说：察祸要推它的鬼在什么地方，再拿身去配六亲，相克招生，就看见祸的端的。 | The Yilin says: to examine a calamity, work out where its ghost is, and then match the Body to the six relations; control brings generation, and the true beginning of the calamity is seen. |

> **按**:「五乡」指五种六亲（父母、子孙、妻财、兄弟、官鬼）这几个位置，「独发」是只有一爻动，「各有两义」是每一种六亲各有两层意思：一层是它本来的人物，一层是它作为鬼的样子（父母是家先，子孙是小儿，妻财是妇婢，兄弟是阳人，官鬼是横恶）。原文「他官外人」的「官」是「宫」之误，问答里同样，这里按「宫」译。「本象」是本卦本身，「他宫」是别的宫；乾卦的官鬼是午火，「壬午」是乾卦第四爻，鬼在本象里动，所以是家亲；大有卦（离宫）的鬼是亥水，「己巳」是巳火，和大有所属的宫（乾宫）相比是别宫的爻，所以是外人——原文没有把这层推理写出来，这一句我只按字面译。「《易林》」是书名，译作 Yilin。「身」是卦身。「相克招生」是被克的一方带来生机，原文没有解释。这一节和易林补遗的「六亲内外动断是谁的鬼」（LY.ILL.EL.40）是同一类。
>
> **Note:** The 'five quarters' are the five relations (Resource, Output, Wealth, Peer, the Pressure line), 'moving alone' is only one line moving, and 'each has two senses' is that each relation has two layers: the person it originally is, and how it looks as a ghost (Resource the ancestors of the house, Output small children, Wealth women and maids, Peer yang people, the Pressure line violent evil). The 'guan' of 'ta guan wai ren' in the original is a miswriting of 'gong' (palace), and so in the questions, and it is rendered palace here. The 'own image' is the hexagram itself and 'another palace' is a different palace; in the sentence about Qian and Dayou, the original does not spell out the reasoning behind 'Ren Wu' and 'Ji Si' and only the words are rendered here. The 'Yilin' is the title of a book. The 'Body' is the hexagram Body. 'Control brings generation' says that the one controlled brings a chance of life, and the original does not explain it. This section is of the same kind as the Yilin Buyi's 'which of the six relations moving inside or outside tells whose ghost it is' (LY.ILL.EL.40).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《海底眼》:异文(相似 0.88) 第 883 行:易镜云：察祸推其鬼处，还将身配六亲，相克相生，便见祸之端

相关:LY.ILL.EL.40、LY.ILL.HZL.11、LY.ILL.HZL.12、LY.ILL.HZL.14

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.14 占鬼神·附六神：青龙善恶经文蘸祀庙香、无气带刑自缢死；朱雀花幡口愿符命灶神、劳死鬼；勾陈天曹勅士、黄病路死鬼；螣蛇夜梦惊恐上许下保福、夜梦见鬼；白虎金刘神作犯白虎刀伤鬼；玄武上真北阴神、落水阴鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:附六神。青龙：善恶、经文、蘸祀、庙香；无气带刑，是自缢死的鬼。朱雀：花幡、口愿、符命、灶神；无气带刑，是劳死的鬼。勾陈：天曹、勅士；无气带刑，是黄病、路死的鬼。螣蛇：夜梦、惊恐、上许下保福；无气带刑，是夜梦见鬼。白虎：金刘神、作犯白虎、刀伤鬼；无气带刑，是刀伤鬼。玄武：上真、北阴神；无气带刑，是落水的阴鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Appended, the six spirits. The Azure Dragon: good and evil, scriptures, sacrificial rites, temple incense; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of hanging. The Vermilion Bird: flower banners, spoken vows, talismans, the Kitchen God; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost who died of toil. Hook Earth: the Heavenly Bureau, the commissioned officers; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of jaundice, or of dying on the road. The Coiling Snake: dreams at night, fright, vows made above and thanks given below; without qi and bearing a punishment, seeing ghosts in dreams at night. The White Tiger: the Jin Liu spirit, the offence-making White Tiger, the ghost of a blade wound; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of a blade wound. The Dark Tortoise: the high true one, the North Yin spirit; without qi and bearing a punishment, a yin ghost of drowning.

**《火珠林·占鬼神》原文** — 《火珠林》第 398–401 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining ghosts and spirits", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 398–401*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 附： | 附带说明： | Appended: |
| 六神 | 六神： | the six spirits: |
| 青龙：善恶、经文、蘸祀、庙香。无气带刑，自缢死。 | 青龙：善恶、经文、蘸祀、庙香。无气带刑，是自缢死的鬼。 | The Azure Dragon: good and evil, scriptures, sacrificial rites, temple incense; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of hanging. |
| 朱雀：花幡、口愿、符命、灶神。无气带刑，劳死鬼。 | 朱雀：花幡、口愿、符命、灶神。无气带刑，是劳死的鬼。 | The Vermilion Bird: flower banners, spoken vows, talismans, the Kitchen God; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost who died of toil. |
| 勾陈：天曹、勅士。无气带刑，黄病路死鬼。 | 勾陈：天曹、勅士。无气带刑，是黄病、路死的鬼。 | Hook Earth: the Heavenly Bureau, the commissioned officers; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of jaundice, or of dying on the road. |
| 螣蛇：夜梦、惊恐、上许下保福。无气带刑，夜梦见鬼。 | 螣蛇：夜梦、惊恐、上许下保福。无气带刑，是夜梦见鬼。 | The Coiling Snake: dreams at night, fright, vows made above and thanks given below; without qi and bearing a punishment, seeing ghosts in dreams at night. |
| 白虎：金刘神、作犯白虎、刀伤鬼。无气带刑，刀伤鬼。 | 白虎：金刘神、作犯白虎、刀伤鬼。无气带刑，是刀伤鬼。 | The White Tiger: the Jin Liu spirit, the offence-making White Tiger, the ghost of a blade wound; without qi and bearing a punishment, a ghost of a blade wound. |
| 玄武：上真、北阴神。无气带刑，落水阴鬼。 | 玄武：上真、北阴神。无气带刑，是落水的阴鬼。 | The Dark Tortoise: the high true one, the North Yin spirit; without qi and bearing a punishment, a yin ghost of drowning. |

> **按**:这一节把六神各自的神和死鬼列出来：每一神前半是它管的神（青龙善恶、经文、蘸祀、庙香；朱雀花幡、口愿、符命、灶神……），后半「无气带刑」是这一神所临的爻无气又带刑时的鬼（青龙自缢死、朱雀劳死、勾陈黄病路死、螣蛇夜梦见鬼、白虎刀伤、玄武落水阴鬼）。「蘸祀」是醮祀，「上许下保福」是上面许愿、下面还愿保福，「金刘神」「作犯白虎」的意思原文没有解释，字面译。「上真」是高一级的真人，「北阴」是北方阴神。原文「六神」二字连在上一行「附：」的后面，又接在「青龙：」前面，所以「附」「六神」分成两个小句。这一节和易林补遗的「总论六神值诀」（LY.ILL.EL.47）是同一类，神名和鬼名各有出入。
>
> **Note:** This section lists for each of the six spirits its gods and its dead ghost: the first half of each is the gods it governs (the Azure Dragon good and evil, scriptures, sacrificial rites, temple incense; the Vermilion Bird flower banners, spoken vows, talismans, the Kitchen God ...), and the second half 'without qi and bearing a punishment' is the ghost when the line the spirit sits on is without qi and bears a punishment (the Azure Dragon a ghost of hanging, the Vermilion Bird died of toil, Hook Earth jaundice or dying on the road, the Coiling Snake seeing ghosts in dreams, the White Tiger a blade wound, the Dark Tortoise a yin ghost of drowning). 'Sacrificial rites' is a jiao offering, 'vows made above and thanks given below' is a vow made above and redeemed below to keep fortune, and what the 'Jin Liu spirit' and the 'offence-making White Tiger' mean the original does not explain, and they are rendered literally. The 'high true one' is a true one of a higher rank and the 'North Yin' is the yin spirit of the north. In the original the two characters for 'the six spirits' run on from 'appended' on the line before and run into 'the Azure Dragon:' after, so 'appended' and 'the six spirits' are made two small pieces. This section is of the same kind as the Yilin Buyi's 'general discussion of the six spirits on the ghost' (LY.ILL.EL.47), with differences in the names of gods and ghosts.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.47、LY.ILL.HZL.13、LY.ILL.HZL.15、LY.ILL.TXF.04

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.15 占鬼神·凡祭赛有三：祀上帝取藏爻中鬼神祗用月建、神堂家庙用日辰；皆要生合卦身不宜刑冲、不要动爻克害刑冲；合生福利而吉、带刑冲反招祸；卦爻中鬼自化入墓必有不了再牵之患

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:祭赛（祭神还愿）有三种：祭上帝，就取伏藏的爻里的鬼神，要用月建；神堂家庙，要用日辰。都要生合卦身，不宜刑冲，也不要动爻来克害刑冲。若合而生，福利而吉；若带刑冲，反而招祸。卦爻中的鬼自己化入墓，必有了不完、再牵连的祸患。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Offerings and thanksgiving are of three kinds: for sacrificing to the High God take the ghost or spirit in the hidden line and use the Month; for a shrine hall or a family temple use the Day. All must generate and combine with the Body of the hexagram, must not clash or punish, and a moving line must not attack, harm, punish or clash with it. If they combine and generate it is fortunate and lucky; if it bears punishment or clash it invites calamity instead. If the ghost in the hexagram lines itself changes into a tomb, there will surely be an unfinished matter that drags on again.

**《火珠林·占鬼神》原文** — 《火珠林》第 402 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining ghosts and spirits", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 402*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡祭赛有三，如祀上帝，即取藏爻中鬼神祗，当用月建；神堂家庙，当用日辰。 | 祭赛有三种：祭上帝，就取伏藏的爻里的鬼神，要用月建；神堂家庙，要用日辰。 | Offerings and thanksgiving are of three kinds: for sacrificing to the High God take the ghost or spirit in the hidden line and use the Month; for a shrine hall or a family temple use the Day. |
| 皆要生合卦身，不宜刑冲，亦不要动爻克害刑冲。 | 都要生合卦身，不宜刑冲，也不要动爻来克害刑冲。 | All must generate and combine with the Body of the hexagram, must not clash or punish, and a moving line must not attack, harm, punish or clash with it. |
| 如合生，福利而吉；若带刑冲，反招祸。 | 若合而生，福利而吉；若带刑冲，反而招祸。 | If they combine and generate it is fortunate and lucky; if it bears punishment or clash it invites calamity instead. |
| 卦爻中鬼自化入墓，必有不了再牵之患。 | 卦爻中的鬼自己化入墓，必有了不完、再牵连的祸患。 | If the ghost in the hexagram lines itself changes into a tomb, there will surely be an unfinished matter that drags on again. |

> **按**:原文「凡祭赛有三」只列出两种（祀上帝、神堂家庙），第三种没有写出来，这里按原文译，不补。「藏爻中鬼神祗」是伏藏的爻里的鬼神，「祗」是地神；「当用月建」「当用日辰」是说祭上帝取月建为主、神堂家庙取日辰为主（月建管大，日辰管近）。「皆要生合卦身」的「卦身」译作 the Body of the hexagram。这一节讲还愿酬神时怎么看祭，和易林补遗的「还赛解厄」（LY.ILL.EL.57）、「解禳通用法」（LY.ILL.EL.54）是同一类；这里的判据是生合卦身则吉、刑冲则祸。
>
> **Note:** The original 'offerings and thanksgiving are of three kinds' lists only two (sacrificing to the High God, and the shrine hall and family temple), and the third is not written, and here it is rendered as the original and not filled in. 'The ghost or spirit in the hidden line' is the ghost or spirit in the concealed line, and 'zhi' is an earth deity; 'use the Month' and 'use the Day' says that sacrifice to the High God takes the Month as the main and the shrine hall and family temple take the Day (the Month rules the great, the Day the near). The 'Body' of 'must generate and combine with the Body of the hexagram' is rendered the Body of the hexagram. This section tells how to look at a sacrifice when paying a vow to the gods, and is of the same kind as the Yilin Buyi's 'giving thanks and resolving misfortune' (LY.ILL.EL.57) and 'the general method of ward-offs' (LY.ILL.EL.54); the criterion here is that generating and combining with the Body is lucky and punishment or clash is calamity.

相关:LY.ILL.EL.57、LY.ILL.EL.54、LY.ILL.HZL.14

---

## LY.ILL.HZL.16 占失物鬼祟(占祟)：阳鬼为男阴鬼为女、伏藏返对而取；占贼占祟以鬼阴阳为用；占祟鬼无正形但以支干取之、鬼动单为少阳拆为少阴重为太阳交为太阴、分老少何人看应爻最切

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:阳鬼是男，阴鬼是女；若是伏藏，就反过来取。占贼、占祟，以鬼的阴阳为用，占定是男是女，要看得的是哪一个官（鬼）。占祟，鬼没有正形，只用地支天干来取：鬼动，单（阳）是少阳，拆（阴）是少阴，重是太阳，交是太阴；要分老少是什么人，只看应爻最切。书中举了一个占祟的例子：遁之姤卦，是子孙鬼，一男一女，和首饰之类的东西有牵连，坟墓在西北。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A yang ghost is male, a yin ghost is female; if it is hidden, take it the other way round. In divining a thief or a curse take the yin or yang of the ghost as the Subject, and fix male or female by which official (ghost) is obtained. In divining a curse the ghost has no true form, and it is taken only by the branch and stem: a moving ghost, single (yang) is lesser yang, split (yin) lesser yin, double is greater yang and crossed is greater yin; to tell what person is old or young look only at the Other line, which is the closest fit. The book gives an example of divining a curse: the hexagram Dun changing to Gou is an Output ghost, one man and one woman, bound up with things such as hairpins and bracelets, and the grave in the northwest.

**《火珠林·占失物鬼祟(占祟一段)》原文** — 《火珠林》第 364–365 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining lost things and ghostly curses (the part on curses)", base text; Huozhu Lin, lines 364–365*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 阳鬼为男，阴鬼为女； | 阳鬼是男，阴鬼是女； | A yang ghost is male, a yin ghost is female; |
| 若是伏藏，返对而取。 | 若是伏藏，就反过来取。 | if it is hidden, take it the other way round. |
| 占贼占祟，以鬼阴阳为用，占主女男看得何官。 | 占贼、占祟，以鬼的阴阳为用，占定是男是女，要看得的是哪一个官。 | In divining a thief or a curse take the yin or yang of the ghost as the Subject, and fix male or female by which official (ghost) is obtained. |

**《火珠林·占失物鬼祟(占祟一段)》原文** — 《火珠林》第 365 行 · *Huozhu Lin (The Fire-Pearl Forest), "Divining lost things and ghostly curses (the part on curses)", base text; Huozhu Lin, line 365*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占祟， | 占祟的时候， | In divining a curse |
| 鬼无正形， | 鬼没有正形， | the ghost has no true form, |
| 但以支干取之。 | 只用地支天干来取。 | and it is taken only by the branch and stem. |
| 鬼动以单为少阳， | 鬼动，单（阳）是少阳， | A moving ghost, single (yang) is lesser yang, |
| 拆为少阴， | 拆（阴）是少阴， | split (yin) is lesser yin, |
| 重为太阳， | 重是太阳， | double is greater yang |
| 交为太阴。 | 交是太阴。 | and crossed is greater yin. |
| 如分老少何人， | 要分老少是什么人， | To tell what person is old or young |
| 但看应爻最切。 | 只看应爻最切。 | look only at the Other line, which is the closest fit. |

**书中卦例** — 《火珠林》第 32–34 行 · *Case from the book; Huozhu Lin, lines 32–34*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又请占祟例为式，答曰： | 又请求占祟的例子来做样子，答： | Asked again for an example of divining a curse to serve as a pattern, the answer: |
| 如遁之姤卦，此卦是子孙鬼，一男一女为钗钏等事物来沉滞，男儿赤，性燥，女儿洁白，性刚；其坟墓现在西北，恐有动犯，告之则吉。 | 比如遁之姤卦，这一卦是子孙鬼，一男一女，因为钗钏一类的东西来沉滞，男孩子赤色、性子燥，女孩子洁白、性子刚；他们的坟墓现在西北，恐怕有人动犯，告诉他们就吉。 | Take the hexagram Dun changing to Gou: this hexagram is an Output ghost, one man and one woman, stalled because of things such as hairpins and bracelets, the boy red and of a dry temper, the girl white and of a firm temper; their grave is now in the northwest, and it is feared that someone has disturbed it, and if told of it there is luck. |
| 问曰：何以知之？ | 问：凭什么知道？ | Asked: how is it known? |
| 曰：二爻丙午火是鬼，化辛亥水是子孙，丙午纳音属水，化辛亥又属水，如二乾宫子孙，故曰子孙鬼也； | 答：二爻丙午火是鬼，化出辛亥水是子孙，丙午的纳音属水，化出的辛亥又属水，好像二个乾宫的子孙，所以叫子孙鬼； | The answer: the second line Bing Wu Fire is the ghost, changing out Xin Hai Water which is Output, the nayin of Bing Wu is Water and the Xin Hai it changes to is also Water, like two Output lines of the Qian palace, so it is called an Output ghost; |
| 一男丙午，一女辛亥也； | 一男是丙午，一女是辛亥； | the one man is Bing Wu and the one woman is Xin Hai; |
| 火主赤，金主白，火燥金刚，皆以五行之性言之也； | 火主赤，金主白，火燥金刚，都是按五行的性子来说的； | Fire governs red and Metal white, Fire is dry and Metal firm, all said by the nature of the five phases; |
| 为钗钏者，辛亥乃钗钏金也； | 是钗钏，是因为辛亥是钗钏金； | the hairpins and bracelets, because Xin Hai is hairpin-and-bracelet Metal; |
| 言坟墓在西北者，水墓在戌，又火绝在亥，亥，西北也； | 说坟墓在西北，是因为水的墓在戌，又火绝在亥，亥是西北； | the grave being in the northwest, because the tomb of Water is at Xu, and Fire is in extinction at Hai, and Hai is the northwest; |
| 墓有犯者，艮属土，化巽为木，木去克土山。 | 墓有人动犯，是因为艮属土，化出巽是木，木去克土山。 | the grave being disturbed, because Gen belongs to Earth and Xun that it changes into is Wood, and Wood goes to control the mountain of Earth. |

> **按**:这一条前两段（L364—L365）是火珠林「占失物鬼祟」里占祟的那一半；前面「占贼」的那几句（阳宫鬼出现主男鬼、伏藏主女……）是占失物的，归失物那个问题域，这里不收。「以鬼阴阳为用，占主女男看得何官」的后半句意思读不确定，「得何官」是得到的是哪个官（鬼），字面译。「单」「拆」「重」「交」是四种爻的写法：单是阳爻不动（少阳）、拆是阴爻不动（少阴）、重是阳爻动（太阳）、交是阴爻动（太阴）。第三段是书里举的占祟的例子，出在全书前面「易中明义」一节里，按书中例收，原文里两行之间隔了一个空行，连着读。例里「一男丙午，一女辛亥」、「钗钏金」、「火绝在亥」等按原文译。「如二乾宫子孙」读不确定，字面译。
>
> **Note:** The first two passages of this entry (L364-L365) are the half of the Huozhu Lin's 'divining lost things and ghostly curses' that is about divining a curse; the earlier sentences about divining a thief ('a yang-palace ghost appearing means a male ghost, hidden means female ...') are about lost things and belong to that question domain, and are not taken here. The second half of 'take the yin or yang of the ghost as the Subject, fix male or female by which official is obtained' is not certain in sense, and 'which official is obtained' is rendered literally. 'Single', 'split', 'double' and 'crossed' are four ways of writing a line: single is a yang line still (lesser yang), split is a yin line still (lesser yin), double is a yang line moving (greater yang) and crossed is a yin line moving (greater yin). The third passage is an example of divining a curse given in the book, in the section 'What is made plain in the Changes' near the front of the whole book, and it is taken as a case from the book; in the original a blank line falls between two of its lines, and it is read as continuous. In the example 'the one man is Bing Wu and the one woman is Xin Hai', 'hairpin-and-bracelet Metal' and 'Fire is in extinction at Hai' are rendered as in the original. 'Like two Output lines of the Qian palace' is not certain and is rendered literally.

相关:LY.ILL.HZL.13、LY.ILL.EL.41、LY.ILL.HZL.11

---

## LY.ILL.TXF.11 世人有疾病者亦有贤愚之分：贤者以医药为重、愚者以祷告为先，占者要察其情状；鬼神儒家本不言、为患者之占不得不据理推详以为阴功默佑；木主东岳天齐圣帝等、水是河泊水府等、土是瘟蝗疫疠中央土府之神、金是衔刀五道飞廉白虎金神七杀等、火是南方火部赤睛朱发鹤神等，各连着相应的伤亡之鬼；二爻鬼为灶神、三爻鬼为香火神、子孙动为福神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世上的人有了病，也有贤愚的分别：贤的把医药放在首位，愚的把祷告放在前头；占卜的人不能不察看他的情状，一眼看去就能知道高下，这样贤的愚的都可以和他说得上话，这门学问才能流传。至于鬼神，儒家本来不说这些，现在为病人占卜，不得不按道理推详，当作暗中的阴功和默佑。木鬼：是东岳天齐圣帝、七十二司、三十六案的神，还有自吊而亡的；水鬼：是河泊水府、沿江七十二庙的神，还有溺水而亡的；土鬼：是瘟蝗疫疠、中央土府的神，还有饥饿绝食而亡的；金鬼：是衔刀、五道、飞廉、白虎、金神、七杀、吟呻、三杀、九良星杀、丧殃、丧杀之神，还有自刎、被刑、阵亡，一切伤亡；火鬼：是南方火部、赤睛朱发的鹤神、毛头毒火之神，还有赴汤蹈火而亡的。如果二爻是鬼，就是灶神；三爻是鬼，是香火神；子孙动是福神。照这样推，万无一失。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** People with illness are also divided into the worthy and the foolish: the worthy put medicine first, the foolish put prayer first; the one who divines must not fail to observe his circumstances, and one look at the person shows high or low, so that worthy and foolish can both be spoken to and this way can circulate. As for ghosts and spirits, the Confucians did not speak of them originally; now, for divining for the sick, one cannot but reason it out from principle, taking it as hidden merit and silent protection. A Wood ghost: Tianqi, the Holy Emperor of the Eastern Peak, the seventy-two departments and the spirits of the thirty-six cases, and also those who hanged themselves; a Water ghost: the River Count and the Water Mansion, the spirits of the seventy-two temples along the river, and also the drowned; an Earth ghost: epidemic, locusts and plague, the spirits of the central Earth Mansion, and also those who died of hunger and starvation; a Metal ghost: the knife-bearing, the Five Roads, Feilian and the White Tiger, the Golden Spirit, the Seven Killings, Yinshen, the Three Killings, the Nine Good Star killings, the spirits of Mourning Calamity and the Mourning Killing, and also every wounding and death by cutting the throat, by execution or in battle; a Fire ghost: the southern Fire Department, the crane spirit with red eyes and vermilion hair, the shaggy-headed poison-fire spirit, and also those who died going into boiling water and fire. If the ghost is on the second line it is the Kitchen God; if on the third line it is the incense-fire spirit; Output moving is the fortune god. Reasoning from this, not one error in ten thousand.

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5008 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5008*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世人有疾病者，亦有贤愚之分。 | 世上的人有了疾病，也有贤愚的分别。 | Among people with illness there are also the worthy and the foolish. |
| 贤者以医药为重，愚者以祷告为先。 | 贤的把医药放在重要的位置，愚的把祷告放在前头。 | The worthy put medicine first, the foolish put prayer first. |
| 占者不可不察其情状。 | 占卜的人不可以不察看他的情状。 | The one who divines must not fail to observe his circumstances. |
| 一览其人，可知高下，庶几贤愚，皆可共语，其道方能流行。 | 一眼看去这个人，就能知道高下，这样贤愚都可以和他说得上话，这门学问才能流传。 | One look at the person shows high or low, so that worthy and foolish can both be spoken to, and only then can this way circulate. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5009 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5009*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼神者： | 说到鬼神： | As for ghosts and spirits: |
| 儒家本不言此， | 儒家本来不说这些， | the Confucians did not speak of them originally; |
| 今为患者之占， | 现在为患病的人占卜， | now, for divining for the sick, |
| 不得不据理推详， | 不得不按道理推详， | one cannot but reason it out from principle, |
| 以为阴功默佑。 | 当作暗中的阴功和默佑。 | taking it as hidden merit and silent protection. |

**《天玄赋》注(注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 5010 行 · *Commentary on the Tianxuan Fu (commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 5010*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 木主东岳天齐圣帝，七十二司，三十六案之神，并自吊伤亡。 | 木鬼：是东岳天齐圣帝、七十二司、三十六案的神，还有自吊而亡的。 | A Wood ghost: Tianqi, the Holy Emperor of the Eastern Peak, the seventy-two departments and the spirits of the thirty-six cases, and also those who hanged themselves. |
| 水是河泊水府，沿江七十二庙之神，并溺水伤亡。 | 水鬼：是河泊水府、沿江七十二庙的神，还有溺水而亡的。 | A Water ghost: the River Count and the Water Mansion, the spirits of the seventy-two temples along the river, and also the drowned. |
| 土是瘟蝗疫疠，中央土府之神，并饥饿绝食伤亡。 | 土鬼：是瘟蝗疫疠、中央土府的神，还有饥饿绝食而亡的。 | An Earth ghost: epidemic, locusts and plague, the spirits of the central Earth Mansion, and also those who died of hunger and starvation. |
| 金是衔刀、五道，飞廉、白虎，金神、七杀，吟呻、三杀，九良星杀，丧殃、丧杀之神，并自刎、被刑、阵亡，一应伤亡。 | 金鬼：是衔刀、五道、飞廉、白虎、金神、七杀、吟呻、三杀、九良星杀、丧殃、丧杀之神，还有自刎、被刑、阵亡，一切伤亡。 | A Metal ghost: the knife-bearing, the Five Roads, Feilian and the White Tiger, the Golden Spirit, the Seven Killings, Yinshen, the Three Killings, the Nine Good Star killings, the spirits of Mourning Calamity and the Mourning Killing, and also every wounding and death by cutting the throat, by execution or in battle. |
| 火是南方火部，赤睛朱发鹤神、毛头毒火之神，并赴汤蹈火伤亡。 | 火鬼：是南方火部、赤睛朱发的鹤神、毛头毒火之神，还有赴汤蹈火而亡的。 | A Fire ghost: the southern Fire Department, the crane spirit with red eyes and vermilion hair, the shaggy-headed poison-fire spirit, and also those who died going into boiling water and fire. |
| 若二爻鬼为灶神，三爻鬼为香火神，子孙动为福神。 | 如果二爻的鬼是灶神，三爻的鬼是香火神，子孙动是福神。 | If the ghost on the second line is the Kitchen God, the ghost on the third line the incense-fire spirit, and Output moving the fortune god, |
| 依此推之，万无一失。 | 照这样推，万无一失。 | reasoning from this, not one error in ten thousand. |

> **按**:这一节是天玄赋对「鬼神」的态度和名单。态度：「鬼神者，儒家本不言此」——本来不说，但因为病人占卜，不得不据理推详，当作「阴功默佑」；这和黄金策、易冒把神鬼只当大类看的态度接近，和易林补遗把神名逐个点出的做法不同。名单按五行给：木（东岳天齐圣帝等）、水（河泊水府）、土（瘟蝗疫疠、中央土府）、金（衔刀五道飞廉白虎金神七杀等）、火（南方火部、鹤神、毒火之神），每类还带着相应的死法。这一套和黄金策「五行推鬼神」（LY.ILL.HJC.97）、易隐「鬼崇·神与鬼的名目」（LY.ILL.YY.28）、易林补遗的五行诀（LY.ILL.EL.46）是同一类名单，野鹤则批评把金官指为关公岳公等具体神（LY.ILL.ZS.24）。「二爻鬼为灶神、三爻鬼为香火神、子孙动为福神」和易林补遗「按爻位断神鬼」（LY.ILL.EL.37）、黄金策「六爻推鬼神」（LY.ILL.HJC.101）一致。「吟呻」「三杀」「九良星杀」「丧殃」照字面译；「赤睛朱发鹤神」「毛头毒火之神」是形容火部神的样子，按字面译。「自吊」是上吊自尽。 卜筮大全的这一章后面还有「虎易评注」（今人加的），本库不引。 这一段里神、鬼、星煞的名字，书里没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。
>
> **Note:** This section is the Tianxuan Fu's attitude to and list of 'ghosts and spirits'. The attitude: 'as for ghosts and spirits, the Confucians did not speak of them' — they did not originally, but for the divining of the sick one cannot but reason it out from principle, taking it as 'hidden merit and silent protection'; this is close to the Huangjin Ce and the Yi Mao, which treat gods and ghosts only as broad kinds, and unlike the Yilin Buyi, which names the gods one by one. The list is given by the five phases: Wood (Tianqi, the Holy Emperor of the Eastern Peak and so on), Water (the River Count and the Water Mansion), Earth (epidemic, locusts and plague, the central Earth Mansion), Metal (the knife-bearing, the Five Roads, Feilian, the White Tiger, the Golden Spirit, the Seven Killings and so on) and Fire (the southern Fire Department, the crane spirit, the poison-fire spirit), each with its matching kind of death. This is the same kind of list as the Huangjin Ce's 'the five phases and the gods and ghosts' (LY.ILL.HJC.97), the Yi Yin's 'the names of gods and ghosts' (LY.ILL.YY.28) and the Yilin Buyi's verse on the five phases (LY.ILL.EL.46), while the Old Man of Yehe criticises pointing a Metal Pressure line at particular gods such as Guan Gong and Yue Gong (LY.ILL.ZS.24). 'A ghost on the second line is the Kitchen God, a ghost on the third line the incense-fire spirit, Output moving the fortune god' agrees with the Yilin Buyi's 'ghosts and spirits by line position' (LY.ILL.EL.37) and the Huangjin Ce's 'the six lines and gods and ghosts' (LY.ILL.HJC.101). 'Yinshen', 'the Three Killings', 'the Nine Good Star killings' and 'Mourning Calamity' are rendered literally; 'the crane spirit with red eyes and vermilion hair' and 'the shaggy-headed poison-fire spirit' describe how the gods of the Fire Department look, and are rendered literally. 'Zi diao' is hanging oneself. After this chapter the Buyi Daquan also has 'Huyi's commentary' (added by a modern hand), which this library does not quote. Names of gods, spirits and stars in this passage are given by transliteration or plain rendering; the book does not explain them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says.

相关:LY.ILL.TXF.10、LY.ILL.TXF.12、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.101、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.EL.37、LY.ILL.EL.46、LY.ILL.ZSZ.07

## LY.ILL.WWJQ.02 用神表·占邪崇、雷霆、鬼神用官鬼爻作用神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:文王金钱课的用神表说：占官、官府、功名、（妻占父）、乱臣、贼盗、邪崇、雷霆、鬼神、词讼，凡是以上这些，都用官鬼爻作用神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The table of the Subject in the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says: in divining officials, the authorities, fame and rank, (a wife divining the father), rebellious ministers, thieves and robbers, evil curses, thunder, ghosts and spirits, and lawsuits, in all of these use the Pressure line as the Subject.

**《文王金钱课·用神》原文** — 《文王金钱课》第 36 行 · *Wenwang Jinqian Ke (King Wen's Coin Readings), "The Table of the Subject", base text; Wenwang Jinqian Ke, line 36*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占官、官府、功名、妻占父、乱臣、贼盗、邪崇、雷霆、鬼神、词讼、凡以上俱官鬼爻为用神。 | 占官、官府、功名、（妻占父）、乱臣、贼盗、邪崇、雷霆、鬼神、词讼，凡是以上这些，都用官鬼爻作用神。 | In divining officials, the authorities, fame and rank, a wife divining the father, rebellious ministers, thieves and robbers, evil curses, thunder, ghosts and spirits, and lawsuits, in all of the above use the Pressure line as the Subject. |

> **按**:这一行列了官鬼爻作用神的各件事，「邪崇」「雷霆」「鬼神」就是占病时的「鬼」：疾病由什么鬼神作祟，在别的书里也是看官鬼（LY.ILL.BSYG.01、LY.ILL.EL.29）。「妻占父」按上下文应是「妻占夫」（妻占丈夫以官鬼为用神），原文如此，译文照字面。「崇」是「祟」的写法，按「curse」译。
>
> **Note:** This line lists the matters for which the Pressure line is the Subject, and 'evil curses', 'thunder' and 'ghosts and spirits' are the 'ghost' of divining illness: what ghost or spirit afflicts an illness is also read from Pressure in other books (LY.ILL.BSYG.01, LY.ILL.EL.29). 'A wife divining the father' should from the context be 'a wife divining the husband' (a wife divining her husband takes Pressure as the Subject), as in the original, and the translation is literal. The character for 'curse' is written in its variant form, and is rendered 'curse'.

相关:LY.ILL.BSYG.01、LY.ILL.EL.29、LY.ILL.WWJQ.01

## LY.ILL.YM.12 土鬼独发而祷于疾病者九死一生：用有气则祷土以回生、用无援则开墓以葬死；东南土薄犯土忌禳土得生者十六七，西北土厚不畏土禁祈而无功

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:常常试到：土鬼独自发动、又为疾病祈祷的，九死一生。有人说，用爻有气，祷告土神就能回生；用爻没有援助，就是开墓来葬死人，这话确实可信。注引《天元赋》说：土和煞一起发动，一定成为黄泉下的客；《补遗》说：夏天论，土爻带鬼发动，凶多吉少，祸难禳解。大体上东南土地薄，犯了土的忌讳、禳土而得生的，十个有六七个；西北土地厚，不怕土的禁忌，所以祈祷也没有功效。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** I have often tried it: where an Earth ghost moves alone and one prays over an illness, nine die and one lives. Some say that if the Subject line has force, praying to the earth spirit restores life, and if it has no help it is opening a tomb to bury the dead; this is truly credible. The note quotes the Tianyuan Fu: when Earth and killing stars both rise, he is sure to become a guest of the Yellow Springs; and the Buyi: in summer, an Earth line carrying a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. In general the earth of the southeast is thin, and of those who have offended earth taboos and exorcised the earth, six or seven in ten live; the earth of the northwest is thick and does not fear earth prohibitions, so prayer there has no effect.

**《易冒·疾病章第五十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1196–1197 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 55: Illness", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1196–1197*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 常试土鬼独发，而祷于疾病者，九死一生，或曰用有气，则祷土以回生，用无援，则开墓以葬死，良可信也。 | 常常试到：土鬼独自发动、又为疾病祈祷的，九死一生；有人说，用爻有气，祷告土神就能回生；用爻没有援助，就是开墓来葬死人，这话确实可信。 | I have often tried it: where an Earth ghost moves alone and one prays over an illness, nine die and one lives; some say that if the Subject line has force, praying to the earth spirit restores life, and if it has no help it is opening a tomb to bury the dead; this is truly credible. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1198 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1198*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 天元赋曰：土煞俱兴，定作黄泉之客； | 《天元赋》说：土和煞一起发动，一定成为黄泉下的客； | The Tianyuan Fu says: when Earth and killing stars both rise, he is sure to become a guest of the Yellow Springs; |
| 补遗云：夏论土爻临鬼动，多凶少吉祸难禳。 | 《补遗》说：夏天论，土爻带鬼发动，凶多吉少，祸难禳解。 | the Buyi says: in summer, an Earth line carrying a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. |
| 大抵东南土薄，有犯土忌，禳土得生者，十有六七，西北土厚，不畏土禁，所以祈而无功也。 | 大体上东南土地薄，犯了土的忌讳、禳土而得生的，十个有六七个；西北土地厚，不怕土的禁忌，所以祈祷也没有功效。 | In general the earth of the southeast is thin, and of those who have offended earth taboos and exorcised the earth, six or seven in ten live; the earth of the northwest is thick and does not fear earth prohibitions, so prayer there has no effect. |

> **按**:「天元赋」不知是哪部书，「补遗」按上下文是《易林补遗》，书里没有说明。「土禁」「土忌」是动土等犯土神的忌讳。这一条把「土鬼独发要祷」讲成地域的差别：东南土薄、犯土忌的多，禳土有效；西北土厚，祈祷无功。
>
> **Note:** The 'Tianyuan Fu' is not known as a work, and the 'Buyi' is by context the Yilin Buyi, which the book does not say. 'Earth prohibitions' and 'earth taboos' are the taboos of offending the earth spirit, by breaking ground and the like. This entry turns 'an Earth ghost moving alone calls for prayer' into a matter of region: in the thin-earthed southeast, where earth taboos are often broken, exorcising the earth works; in the thick-earthed northwest, prayer is without effect.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.11、LY.ILL.YM.13、LY.ILL.HJC.100、LY.ILL.ZS.25

## LY.ILL.YM.42 鬼神章总纲：欲知鬼神情状亦以筮而得之；天神地祗以官鬼类其象，不外二仪四象五行八卦及六神诸星；索鬼神之性（生前偏好）、司（上帝命司何事）、成（何因缘而成）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:古时候的祭祀，不是那位神的主祭人就不当「尸」，不是鬼的同类就不享用祭品，所以没有越位的祭祀、没有轻慢的祭祀。后来的人有祟就做禳解、求福就去祈祷，鬼神的名目就多起来了；虽然这样，吉事有神帮忙，凶事有鬼凭依，史书传记里记的，并不全是虚妄。想知道鬼神的情形，也可以用占筮得到。天神地祇，就用官鬼来归类它的象；取象、画出它的样子，离不开两仪、四象、五行、八卦，再加上六神、诸星来给它成形，用有无来衡量，用上下来定位。探求这位鬼神的性情、所掌管的事，以及它怎么成为鬼神的，就可以推断了。注解释这三样：「性」是生前偏爱的性情，「司」是上帝命它掌管什么事，「成」是因为什么缘由成为鬼神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the sacrifices of old, one who was not the rightful master did not stand as the impersonator, and a ghost did not savour what was not of its kind, so there were no sacrifices beyond one's station and no profane offerings. Later ages exorcised when there was a haunting and prayed for blessing, and so ghosts and spirits multiplied; even so, good fortune has spirits to help and misfortune has ghosts to possess, and what the histories and traditions record is not all false. To learn the state of ghosts and spirits, this too can be obtained by divination. For heavenly spirits and earthly deities, the image is classified by the Pressure line; the image is taken and drawn, and it does not go beyond the two modes, the four images, the five phases and the eight trigrams, together with the six spirits and the various stars to give it form, presence or absence to measure it, and above or below to place it. Seek the nature of the ghost or spirit, what it governs, and how it came to be one, and it can be worked out. The note explains the three: 'nature' is the disposition it favoured in life, 'what it governs' is what the Supreme Lord appointed it to govern, and 'how it came to be' is by what cause it became a ghost or spirit.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1262 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1262*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 古之祀祭，非其主不尸，鬼非其类不馨，故无越祭、无渎祀也。 | 古时候的祭祀，不是那位神的主祭人就不当「尸」，不是鬼的同类就不享用祭品的香气，所以没有越位的祭祀、没有轻慢的祭祀。 | In the sacrifices of old, one who was not the rightful master did not stand as the impersonator, and a ghost did not savour what was not of its kind, so there were no sacrifices beyond one's station and no profane offerings. |
| 后世乃以有祟而禳福而祈者，而鬼神繁兴矣，虽然，吉有神相，凶有鬼凭，史传所载，非尽诬也。 | 后来的人因为有祟就做禳解、为求福就去祈祷，鬼神的名目就多起来了；虽然这样，吉事有神在帮忙，凶事有鬼在凭依，史书传记里记的，并不全是虚妄。 | Later ages exorcised when there was a haunting and prayed for blessing, and so ghosts and spirits multiplied; even so, good fortune has spirits to help and misfortune has ghosts to possess, and what the histories and traditions record is not all false. |
| 欲知鬼神情状，则亦以筮而得之。 | 想知道鬼神是什么情形，也可以用占筮得到。 | To learn the state of ghosts and spirits, this too can be obtained by divination. |

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1263 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1263*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫天神地祗，则以官鬼而类其象，象而像之，其不外乎二仪、四象、五行、八卦，及六神、诸星以形之，有无以度之，上下以位之。 | 天神地祇，就用官鬼来归类它的象；取象、画出它的样子，离不开两仪、四象、五行、八卦，再加上六神、诸星来给它成形，用有无来衡量它，用上下来定它的位置。 | For heavenly spirits and earthly deities, the image is classified by the Pressure line; the image is taken and drawn, and it does not go beyond the two modes, the four images, the five phases and the eight trigrams, together with the six spirits and the various stars to give it form, presence or absence to measure it, and above or below to place it. |

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1264 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1264*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 索其鬼神之性，鬼神之司，及鬼神所由成，而可推矣。 | 探求这位鬼神的性情、所掌管的事，以及它是怎么成为鬼神的，就可以推断了。 | Seek the nature of the ghost or spirit, what it governs, and how it came to be one, and it can be worked out. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1265 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1265*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 性，索其鬼神生前偏好之性； | 「性」，是探求这位鬼神生前偏爱的性情； | 'Nature' is seeking the disposition the ghost or spirit favoured in life; |
| 司，索其鬼神上帝命司何事； | 「司」，是探求上帝命这位鬼神掌管什么事； | 'what it governs' is seeking what the Supreme Lord appointed the ghost or spirit to govern; |
| 成，索其以何因缘而成鬼神也。 | 「成」，是探求它因为什么缘由成为鬼神。 | 'how it came to be' is seeking by what cause and condition it became a ghost or spirit. |

> **按**:「尸」是祭祀时代表神或亡者接受祭品的人。「二仪」指阴阳，「四象」是老阳、少阴、少阳、老阴一类的四象。这一章通篇的办法在这里立好了：鬼神只由官鬼爻来看，看的方法有五行、八卦（宫）、六神、诸星、六爻、六亲、十二支；要探的有三样，性、司、成，后面「金木水火土」各有一段，把这三样分开说。开头几句里作者的态度是，鬼神不是全然虚妄的，但也不主张一味祭祀，这一点到章末（LY.ILL.YM.63、YM.64）才收拢。
>
> **Note:** The 'impersonator' (shi) is the person who stands for the god or the dead in a sacrifice and receives the offerings. The 'two modes' are yin and yang, and the 'four images' are the four of old yang, young yin, young yang and old yin. The method of the whole chapter is set up here: ghosts and spirits are read only from the Pressure line, by the five phases, the eight trigrams (palaces), the six spirits, the stars, the six lines, the six relations and the twelve branches; there are three things to seek, nature, charge and origin, and the later paragraphs on Metal, Wood, Water, Fire and Earth each speak of these three separately. In the opening lines the author's attitude is that ghosts and spirits are not wholly false, but he does not urge sacrifice on every occasion, and this is only drawn together at the end of the chapter (LY.ILL.YM.63, YM.64).

相关:LY.ILL.YM.41、LY.ILL.YM.43、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.YM.64、LY.ILL.HJC.93、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.YY.27

## LY.ILL.YM.43 官鬼属阳则阳神、属阴则阴神：阴神如王母、大悲、水母、太山、天妃、蚕母、子母、催生、太君娘娘之属，要因地（同神异名）、因神（男女）、因事（问蚕得阴官乃蚕母）而考

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻属阳的，就是阳神；属阴的，就是阴神。属阴的官鬼，像王母、大悲、水母、太山、天妃、陈周、圣妃、蚕母、子母、催生、太君、娘娘这一类，也应当按地方、按人、按事去考察，这里只是举个大概。因为有的神是同一位而名字不同，这叫因地，比如在北方叫子母娘娘，在南方叫太君娘娘；男女有别叫因神，为什么事叫因事，比如问蚕的事，得到阴的官鬼，就是蚕母。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A Pressure line that is yang is a yang spirit, and one that is yin is a yin spirit. Where the Pressure line is yin, as with the Queen Mother, the Great Compassionate, the Water Mother, Taishan, the Heavenly Consort, Chen and Zhou, the Holy Consort, the Silkworm Mother, the Child Mother, the Birth Hastener, the Grand Lady and the Ladies, one should also examine by place, by person and by matter; this only gives an outline. For there are spirits who are the same but named differently, which is 'by place': in the north she is the Lady of Child and Mother, in the south the Lady Grand Dame. Difference of man and woman is 'by spirit', and what the matter is about is 'by matter': for instance, when asking about silkworms, a yin Pressure line is the Silkworm Mother.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1265 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1265*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫官鬼之爻属阳，则阳神也，属阴，则阴神也。 | 官鬼爻属阳的，就是阳神；属阴的，就是阴神。 | A Pressure line that is yang is a yang spirit, and one that is yin is a yin spirit. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1266 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1266*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属阴，如王母、大悲、水母、太山、天妃、陈周、圣妃、蚕母、子母、催生、太君、娘娘之属，亦当因地、因人、因事而考之，此则举其略尔。 | 官鬼属阴的，像王母、大悲、水母、太山、天妃、陈周、圣妃、蚕母、子母、催生、太君、娘娘这一类，也应当按地方、按人、按事去考察，这里只是举个大概。 | Where the Pressure line is yin, as with the Queen Mother, the Great Compassionate, the Water Mother, Taishan, the Heavenly Consort, Chen and Zhou, the Holy Consort, the Silkworm Mother, the Child Mother, the Birth Hastener, the Grand Lady and the Ladies, one should also examine by place, by person and by matter; this only gives an outline. |
| 盖有神同而名异，曰因地，如在北为子母娘娘，在南为太君娘娘； | 因为有的神是同一位而名字不同，这叫因地，比如在北方叫子母娘娘，在南方叫太君娘娘； | For there are spirits who are the same but named differently, which is 'by place': in the north she is the Lady of Child and Mother, in the south the Lady Grand Dame; |
| 男女相别曰因神，为何事曰因事，如问蚕，得阴官乃蚕母也。 | 男女有别叫因神，为什么事叫因事，比如问蚕的事，得到阴的官鬼，就是蚕母。 | difference of man and woman is 'by spirit', and what the matter is about is 'by matter': for instance, when asking about silkworms, a yin Pressure line is the Silkworm Mother. |

> **打架**:阴阳是分「神」和「鬼」，还是分男神女神：易冒说官鬼属阳是阳神、属阴是阴神，举的阴神是王母、天妃、蚕母这些女神，阴的仍是神，不是鬼；易隐说「阳鬼为神、阴官为鬼」，卜筮正宗的王洪绪说「阳为神为男，阴为鬼为女」（LY.ILL.YY.27、LY.ILL.HJC.94），阴就是鬼；黄金策旧注和增删卜易的觉子则按旺衰分，旺相是神、休囚是鬼（LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.ZS.23）。易冒在这里分的是阳神和阴神，神与鬼另在后面「生前之性」几段里分（LY.ILL.YM.49 起）。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether yin and yang divide 'spirit' from 'ghost' or male deity from female: the Yi Mao says a yang Pressure line is a yang spirit and a yin one a yin spirit, and its yin examples are goddesses such as the Queen Mother, the Heavenly Consort and the Silkworm Mother, so yin is still a spirit and not a ghost; the Yi Yin says 'a yang ghost is a spirit, a yin Pressure is a ghost', and Wang Hongxu of the Bushi Zhengzong says 'yang is a spirit and male, yin is a ghost and female' (LY.ILL.YY.27, LY.ILL.HJC.94), so yin is a ghost; the old commentary on the Huangjin Ce and Jue Zi of the Zengshan Buyi divide by strength, strong being a spirit and resting or trapped a ghost (LY.ILL.HJC.94, LY.ILL.ZS.23). The Yi Mao here divides yang spirits from yin spirits, and divides spirit from ghost later, in the paragraphs on how each was in life (from LY.ILL.YM.49).

> **按**:这一份名单里的神名，书里一个也没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定；「大悲」「太山」「陈周」也是照字面写。「因地、因神、因事」三样是考察同一位神的三个角度：同神异名靠地方分，男女靠神分，要问什么事靠事分。
>
> **Note:** Names of gods and goddesses in the lists are given by transliteration or by a plain rendering; the book does not explain any of them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says. 'Great Compassionate', 'Taishan' and 'Chen and Zhou' are likewise written as they stand. 'By place, by spirit, by matter' are three angles from which to examine the same deity: the same deity under different names is told by place, male and female by the spirit, and what is being asked by the matter.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.42、LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.YY.27、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.23

## LY.ILL.YM.44 官鬼属金则武神：天将、关帝、伍公、岳王、玄坛、总官、七煞、金刚、伤司、丧煞之属，及称元帅将军金甲神、威武斗勇成神者

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼属金的，就是武神。像天将、关帝、伍公、岳王、玄坛、总官、七煞、金刚、伤司、丧煞这一类；有的地方称作元帅、将军、金甲神的，以及靠威武善斗成了神的，都可以类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is Metal, it is a martial spirit, as with the Heavenly Generals, Emperor Guan, Lord Wu, Prince Yue, the Dark Altar, the Chief Official, the Seven Killings, the Vajra, the Injuring Office and the Mourning Killing; and those locally called marshals, generals and golden-armoured spirits, and those who became spirits by martial might and fighting courage, can all be reasoned by analogy.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1268 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1268*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 属金，则武神也。 | 属金的，就是武神。 | If it is Metal, it is a martial spirit. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1269 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1269*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属金，如天将、关帝、伍公、岳王、元坛、总官、七煞、金刚、伤司、丧煞之属，盖方隅有称元帅将军金甲之神，及威武斗勇成神者，皆可类推。 | 官鬼属金的，像天将、关帝、伍公、岳王、元坛、总官、七煞、金刚、伤司、丧煞这一类；有的地方称作元帅、将军、金甲神的，以及靠威武斗勇成了神的，都可以类推。 | Where the Pressure line is Metal, as with the Heavenly Generals, Emperor Guan, Lord Wu, Prince Yue, the Dark Altar, the Chief Official, the Seven Killings, the Vajra, the Injuring Office and the Mourning Killing, and those locally called marshals, generals and golden-armoured spirits, and those who became spirits by martial might and fighting courage, all can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **打架**:要不要把金鬼指成关帝、岳王这样的具体某位神：易冒举天将、关帝、伍公、岳王等作例子，但同一章的末尾说「必实指鬼神而泥之则诬」（LY.ILL.YM.63）；增删卜易的李我平点名批评《易冒》指定关岳（LY.ILL.ZS.25），觉子也认为金官指为关公岳公等武神不对，武神有封有未封、各地不同，要问病人许愿未酬的是哪座庙（LY.ILL.ZS.24）。易冒自己也只把这些当作例子，不当作定论。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether a Metal ghost should be pinned to a particular god such as Emperor Guan or Prince Yue: the Yi Mao gives the Heavenly Generals, Emperor Guan, Lord Wu, Prince Yue and the rest as examples, but at the end of the same chapter says 'to point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim' (LY.ILL.YM.63); Li Woping of the Zengshan Buyi names the Yi Mao in criticising the pinning to Guan and Yue (LY.ILL.ZS.25), and Jue Zi also holds that calling a Metal Pressure line Guan Gong or Yue Gong is wrong, since martial spirits are enfeoffed or not and differ from place to place, and one should ask which temple the patient vowed to without redeeming (LY.ILL.ZS.24). The Yi Mao itself takes these only as examples, not as conclusions.

> **按**:「元坛」是「玄坛」避讳的写法，下面属火那一条里也有。这份名单里的神名，书里一个也没有解释，这里按字面译或音译，不作认定。「方隅有称元帅将军金甲之神」是说各地方对武神的称呼不同，按当地的称呼归到金里。
>
> **Note:** 'Yuan Altar' is the taboo-avoiding form of 'Xuan Altar' (the Dark Altar), and appears again in the Fire list below. Names of gods and goddesses in the lists are given by transliteration or by a plain rendering; the book does not explain any of them, and none is identified here beyond what the book says. 'Locally called marshals, generals and golden-armoured spirits' means that the names given to martial spirits differ from place to place, and whatever the local name, it is filed under Metal.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.43、LY.ILL.YM.45、LY.ILL.YM.49、LY.ILL.YM.63、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

## LY.ILL.YM.45 官鬼属水则河海江神：普陀、真武、云台、北斗、海神、龙王、河神、大王、杨将、施相、天妃、晏公、祠山、周王、汪公、水母、井神池神之属；各方水神随占人之民俗土风，漕河尊大王、东海尊天妃、南河尊周王

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼属水的，就是河、海、江的神。像普陀、真武、云台、北斗、海神、龙王、河神、大王、杨将、施相、天妃、晏公、祠山、周王、汪公、水母、水圣、井神、池神、水伤神这一类，以及因水而成神的，都可以类推。九河百川，各个地方各有掌管的神，百姓的尊称各不相同，要随着占卜人所在的民俗和风土，用五行八卦、六神诸星去取象，才算射中靶心。大体上水神、火神灵验显赫的多，没法一一细说，各地不一样：漕河尊大王，东海尊天妃，南河尊周王，洞庭尊湘妃，鄱阳是小姑，长江是杨四将军，各有各的分管。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is Water, it is the spirits of river, sea and great river, as with Putuo, Zhenwu, Yuntai, the Dipper, the Sea God, the Dragon King, the River God, the Great King, General Yang, Lord Shi, the Heavenly Consort, Lord Yan, Cishan, Prince Zhou, Lord Wang, the Water Mother, the Water Sage, the Well God, the Pond God and the water-wound spirits, and those who became spirits through water. For the nine rivers and the hundred streams each place has its own governing spirit, and the honorifics the common people give differ from place to place; following the customs and local ways of the one who divines, take the image by the five phases, the eight trigrams, the six spirits and the various stars, and one hits the mark. In general the spirits of water and fire are often powerfully manifest and cannot be set out in detail, differing from place to place: the grain-transport canal honours the Great King, the Eastern Sea the Heavenly Consort, the southern river Prince Zhou, Dongting the Xiang Consort, Poyang has the Little Aunt, and the Yangtze General Yang the Fourth, each with its own domain.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1270 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1270*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 属水，则河海江神也。 | 属水的，就是河、海、江的神。 | If it is Water, it is the spirits of river, sea and great river. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1271 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1271*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属水，如普陀、真武、云台、北斗、海神、龙王、河神、大王、杨将、施相、天妃、晏公、祠山、周王、汪公、水母、水圣、井神、池神、水伤神之属，及由水成神可类。 | 官鬼属水的，像普陀、真武、云台、北斗、海神、龙王、河神、大王、杨将、施相、天妃、晏公、祠山、周王、汪公、水母、水圣、井神、池神、水伤神这一类，以及因水而成神的，都可以类推。 | Where the Pressure line is Water, as with Putuo, Zhenwu, Yuntai, the Dipper, the Sea God, the Dragon King, the River God, the Great King, General Yang, Lord Shi, the Heavenly Consort, Lord Yan, Cishan, Prince Zhou, Lord Wang, the Water Mother, the Water Sage, the Well God, the Pond God and the water-wound spirits, and those who became spirits through water, all can be reasoned by analogy. |
| 盖九河百川，方隅各有所司之神，黎庶尊称，各有相别，随占人之民俗土风，以五行八卦、六神诸星，象而像之，则射的矣。 | 九河百川，各个地方各有掌管的神，百姓的尊称各不相同，要随着占卜人所在的民俗和风土，用五行八卦、六神诸星去取象，这样才是射中靶心。 | For the nine rivers and the hundred streams each place has its own governing spirit, and the honorifics the common people give differ from place to place; following the customs and local ways of the one who divines, take the image by the five phases, the eight trigrams, the six spirits and the various stars, and one hits the mark. |
| 大抵水火之神，灵显者多，无能细陈，方隅各异，犹若漕河尊大王，东海尊天妃，南河尊周王，洞庭尊湘妃，鄱阳小姑，长江杨四将军，各有所分也。 | 大体上水神、火神灵验显赫的多，没法一一细说，各地方不一样，比如漕河尊大王，东海尊天妃，南河尊周王，洞庭湖尊湘妃，鄱阳湖是小姑，长江是杨四将军，各有各的分管。 | In general, the spirits of water and fire are often powerfully manifest, and cannot be set out in detail, differing from place to place: for instance, the grain-transport canal honours the Great King, the Eastern Sea honours the Heavenly Consort, the southern river honours Prince Zhou, Dongting honours the Xiang Consort, Poyang has the Little Aunt, and the Yangtze has General Yang the Fourth, each with its own domain. |

> **按**:神名的处理见 LY.ILL.YM.44 的注。这一条比别的四行多讲了一点道理：各地的水神名号不同，要随占卜人所在的地方风俗去取象，不是拿一张名单去对。「射的」是射中靶心。
>
> **Note:** Names are treated as in the note to LY.ILL.YM.44. This entry gives more of a reason than the other four phases: the names of water spirits differ from region to region, so the image is to be taken according to the customs of the place of the one who divines, and not by matching against a list. 'Hits the mark' is hitting the bull's-eye.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.YM.46、LY.ILL.YM.50、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.HJC.113、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.46 官鬼属木则文神及倚草附木之妖：东岳、家堂、山神、树神、五圣、园神、九良、茶筵、三茅、九天、鲁班、花神、三郎、道君、文昌、南木神之属，及凭依山木树木器皿成神者

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼属木的，就是文神，以及依托在草木上的精怪。像东岳、家堂、山神、树神、五圣、园神、九良、茶筵、三茅、九天、鲁班、花神、三郎、道君、文昌、南木神这一类，以及各地有依附山木、树木、器皿而成神的，都可以类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is Wood, it is a civil spirit, and the demons that lean on grass and cling to trees, as with the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Mountain God, the Tree God, the Five Sages, the Garden God, the Nine Good Ones, the Tea Feast, the Three Mao, the Nine Heavens, Lu Ban, the Flower God, the Third Lord, the Daoist Lord, Wenchang and the Southern Wood God, and those places where a spirit has arisen from a mountain, a tree or a vessel it clings to; all can be reasoned by analogy.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1272 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1272*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 属木，则文神也，及倚草附木之妖也。 | 属木的，就是文神，以及依托在草木上的精怪。 | If it is Wood, it is a civil spirit, and the demons that lean on grass and cling to trees. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1273 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1273*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属木，如东岳、家堂、山神、树神、五圣、园神、九良、茶筵、三茅、九天、鲁班、花神、三郎、道君、文昌、南木神之属，及方隅有凭依山木、树木、器皿成神者，皆可类推。 | 官鬼属木的，像东岳、家堂、山神、树神、五圣、园神、九良、茶筵、三茅、九天、鲁班、花神、三郎、道君、文昌、南木神这一类，以及各地有依附山木、树木、器皿而成神的，都可以类推。 | Where the Pressure line is Wood, as with the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Mountain God, the Tree God, the Five Sages, the Garden God, the Nine Good Ones, the Tea Feast, the Three Mao, the Nine Heavens, Lu Ban, the Flower God, the Third Lord, the Daoist Lord, Wenchang and the Southern Wood God, and those places where a spirit has arisen from a mountain, a tree or a vessel it clings to, all can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **按**:神名的处理见 LY.ILL.YM.44 的注。「文神」和金的「武神」相对（LY.ILL.YM.44）：文昌、鲁班是文事、工艺的神，东岳、家堂、山神、树神是草木土地一类的神，都算木。
>
> **Note:** Names are treated as in the note to LY.ILL.YM.44. 'Civil spirit' is the counterpart of the Metal 'martial spirit' (LY.ILL.YM.44): Wenchang and Lu Ban are spirits of letters and crafts, and the Eastern Peak, the household shrine, the Mountain God and the Tree God are spirits of plants and land, all counted as Wood.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.45、LY.ILL.YM.47、LY.ILL.YM.51、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.47 官鬼属火则火神、生育雷电之司：离神、电神、南斗、火雷、韦陀、玄坛、华光、五神、五赫、三煞、灶神、炉神、炮神、香火、南堂、利市、生育神之属，及由火成神者

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼属火的，就是火神，是掌管生育、雷电的。像离神、电神、南斗、火雷、韦陀、元坛、华光、五神、五赫、三煞、灶神、旗纛（？）、炉神、炮神、香火、南堂、利市、生育神这一类，以及因火而成神的，都可以类推。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is Fire, it is a fire spirit, in charge of childbirth, thunder and lightning, as with the Li spirit, the Lightning God, the Southern Dipper, Fire and Thunder, Weituo, the Dark Altar, Huaguang, the Five Spirits, the Five Blazes, the Three Killings, the Kitchen God, the banner-standard (?), the Furnace God, the Cannon God, the incense fire, the Southern Hall, the Market Luck and the birth-giving spirits, and those who became spirits through fire; all can be reasoned by analogy.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1274 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1274*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 属火，则火神也，生育雷电之司也。 | 属火的，就是火神，是掌管生育、雷电的。 | If it is Fire, it is a fire spirit, in charge of childbirth, thunder and lightning. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1275 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1275*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属火，如离神、电神、南斗、火雷、韦陀、元坛、华光、五神、五赫、三煞、灶神、旗纛？、炉神、炮神、香火、南堂、利市、生育神之属，及由火成神可类。 | 官鬼属火的，像离神、电神、南斗、火雷、韦陀、元坛、华光、五神、五赫、三煞、灶神、旗纛（？）、炉神、炮神、香火、南堂、利市、生育神这一类，以及因火而成神的，可以类推。 | Where the Pressure line is Fire, as with the Li spirit, the Lightning God, the Southern Dipper, Fire and Thunder, Weituo, the Dark Altar, Huaguang, the Five Spirits, the Five Blazes, the Three Killings, the Kitchen God, the banner-standard (?), the Furnace God, the Cannon God, the incense fire, the Southern Hall, the Market Luck and the birth-giving spirits, and those who became spirits through fire, can be reasoned by analogy. |

> **按**:神名的处理见 LY.ILL.YM.44 的注。「旗纛？」后面的问号是转录者标的，表示对这两个字没有把握，这里照录、没有改；那个问号在原文里把这一句切成了两段，这里按一句译。
>
> **Note:** Names are treated as in the note to LY.ILL.YM.44. The question mark after 'banner-standard' is the transcriber's own, showing doubt about the two characters, and is copied as it stands; in the original it cuts this sentence into two, and it is translated here as one sentence.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.46、LY.ILL.YM.48、LY.ILL.YM.52、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.48 官鬼属土则土神及山川社稷之司：地祗、太岁、城隍、社稷、土神、庙神、宅神、伽蓝、皮肠、当境、本家土地之属；土部名号非一（飞游作犯斫伐坟墓坑厮金神吟神伏尸螣蛇天曹土），当以八宫六神诸星配辨；西北土厚知地祗、东南土薄多土曹

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼属土的，就是土神，以及掌管山川社稷的。像地祇、太岁、城隍、社稷、土神、庙神、宅神、伽蓝、皮肠、当境、本家土地这一类，以及因为在当地有功绩而成神的，都是。土这一部的神灵，圣号、曹名不止一种，有飞游、作犯、斫伐、坟墓、坑厮、金神、吟神、伏尸、螣蛇、天曹土等称呼，要用八宫、六神、诸星配合起来辨别，比如白虎带土官，就断成金神七煞。大体上西北土地厚，只知道有地祇；东南土地薄，所以有很多土曹的名号。所以要因人、因地、因事，变通的方法很多。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Pressure line is Earth, it is an earth spirit, and those in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of soil and grain, as with the Earth Spirit, the Tai Sui, the City God, the Altars of Soil and Grain, earth gods, temple gods, house gods, the Sangharama, the Skin and Intestines, the Local Boundary God and the family's own Earth God, and those who became spirits through merit in a locality. The spirits of the Earth department have more than one holy title and office name, such as Flying Wanderer, Offence-maker, Felling, Grave, Pit Rascal, Golden Spirit, Chanting Spirit, Buried Corpse, Coiling Snake and Heavenly Bureau Earth; one must tell them apart by combining the eight palaces, the six spirits and the various stars, so that for example an Earth Pressure line with the White Tiger is judged the Golden Spirit or the Seven Killings. In general the earth of the northwest is thick and people know only the Earth Spirit, while the earth of the southeast is thin and so has many bureau titles of the earth. So one goes by person, place and matter, and the ways of adapting are many.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1276 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1276*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 属土，则土神也，及山川社稷之司也。 | 属土的，就是土神，以及掌管山川社稷的。 | If it is Earth, it is an earth spirit, and those in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of soil and grain. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1277 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1277*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼属土，如地祗、太岁、城隍、社稷、土神、庙神、宅神、伽蓝、皮肠、当境、本家土地之属，及由地方功勣为神俱是。 | 官鬼属土的，像地祇、太岁、城隍、社稷、土神、庙神、宅神、伽蓝、皮肠、当境、本家土地这一类，以及因为在当地有功绩而成神的，都是。 | Where the Pressure line is Earth, as with the Earth Spirit, the Tai Sui, the City God, the Altars of Soil and Grain, earth gods, temple gods, house gods, the Sangharama, the Skin and Intestines, the Local Boundary God and the family's own Earth God, and those who became spirits through merit in a locality, all are included. |
| 盖土部神灵、圣号、曹名非一，有飞游、作犯、斫伐、坟墓、坑厮、金神、吟神、伏尸、螣蛇、天曹土诸称，当以八宫、六神、诸星，配合辨别，若白虎土官，乃断金神七煞。 | 土这一部的神灵，圣号、曹名不止一种，有飞游、作犯、斫伐、坟墓、坑厮、金神、吟神、伏尸、螣蛇、天曹土等称呼，要用八宫、六神、诸星配合起来辨别，比如白虎带土官，就断成金神七煞。 | The spirits of the Earth department have more than one holy title and office name, such as Flying Wanderer, Offence-maker, Felling, Grave, Pit Rascal, Golden Spirit, Chanting Spirit, Buried Corpse, Coiling Snake and Heavenly Bureau Earth; one must tell them apart by combining the eight palaces, the six spirits and the various stars, so that for example an Earth Pressure line with the White Tiger is judged the Golden Spirit or the Seven Killings. |
| 大凡西北土厚，惟知有地祗，东南土薄，故多土曹之号也。 | 大体上西北土地厚，只知道有地祇；东南土地薄，所以有很多土曹的名号。 | In general the earth of the northwest is thick and people know only the Earth Spirit, while the earth of the southeast is thin and so has many bureau titles of the earth. |
| 所以因人、因地、因事、通变多端尔。 | 所以要因人、因地、因事，变通的方法很多。 | So one goes by person, place and matter, and the ways of adapting are many. |

> **按**:神名的处理见 LY.ILL.YM.44 的注。土部的称呼里「螣蛇」在这里是土部一个神煞的名字，不是六神里的螣蛇，原文没有再分；「土曹」是掌管土地的曹官。西北土厚、东南土薄的说法，和前面土鬼独发那一条（LY.ILL.YM.12）里东南土薄、犯土忌多、西北土厚不畏土禁，是同一个地理判断。
>
> **Note:** Names are treated as in the note to LY.ILL.YM.44. Among the titles of the Earth department 'Coiling Snake' is the name of one earth star here, not the Coiling Snake of the six spirits, and the original does not distinguish them further; 'bureau of the earth' is the office that governs the land. The saying that the northwest earth is thick and the southeast earth thin is the same geographical judgment as in the entry on an Earth ghost moving alone (LY.ILL.YM.12), where in the southeast the earth is thin and earth taboos are often broken, and in the northwest it is thick and earth prohibitions are not feared.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.47、LY.ILL.YM.53、LY.ILL.YM.12、LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.HJC.100、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.49 金鬼的来历：生前好勇（性属金），或亡于干戈锋镝、病丧于喉肺、为义为利，是成鬼之因；成鬼能善则上帝命其所司，位西方、权秋令、主兵事，按金象考

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前好勇的鬼，是就它的性情说，所以属金；或者死在兵器锋镝之下，以及病死在喉、肺，为义、为利而死，这是就它怎样成鬼说，所以也归金类。成了鬼又能行善的，上帝命它掌管的事，如果位在西方、权在秋令、主管兵事，就按金象来考察。注说：这是察看鬼神的来历。金鬼一定生前好勇，就性情说，它死在刀兵之下；就它成为鬼神说，这位神掌管什么事，可能属水、属火、属土、属金、属木；就所掌管的属性说，比如关帝、岳王的神勇和义属金，七煞、丧煞，体属金、掌管杀伐，也属金。遁甲里的七煞是庚，这就叫体属金。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A ghost who loved courage in life is Metal, speaking of its nature; or one who died under weapons and arrowheads, or of illness of the throat or lungs, or over righteousness or profit, is spoken of as how it became a ghost, and so is also classed as Metal. One who, having become a ghost, can do good, and whom the Supreme Lord has appointed to a charge, if its seat is in the west, its authority in the autumn season and its charge in military affairs, is examined by the Metal image. The note says: this is to examine where the ghost or spirit came from. A Metal ghost must have loved courage in life; by nature, that ghost died by blade and weapon; as to its becoming a ghost or spirit, what this spirit governs may be Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood; as to what its charge belongs to, for example, the valour and righteousness of Guan and Yue belong to Metal, and the Seven Killings and the Mourning Killing, whose body is Metal and whose charge is killing, belong to Metal. The Seven Killings of the Dunjia is Geng, which is what is meant by a body of Metal.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1278 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1278*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前好勇之鬼，言其性故属金，或亡于干戈锋镝之下，及病丧于喉、肺、义、利，言其成鬼，故金类之。 | 生前好勇的鬼，是就它的性情说，所以属金；或者死在兵器锋镝之下，以及病死在喉、肺，为义、为利而死，这是就它怎样成鬼说，所以归入金类。 | A ghost who loved courage in life is Metal, speaking of its nature; or one who died under weapons and arrowheads, or of illness of the throat or lungs, or over righteousness or profit, is spoken of as how it became a ghost, and so is classed as Metal. |
| 成鬼而能有善，上帝命其所司，若位于西方，权于秋令，主于兵事，则金象而考也。 | 成了鬼又能行善的，上帝命它掌管的事，如果位在西方、权在秋令、主管兵事，就按金象来考察。 | One who, having become a ghost, can do good, and whom the Supreme Lord has appointed to a charge, if its seat is in the west, its authority in the autumn season and its charge in military affairs, is examined by the Metal image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1279 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1279*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此察鬼神所由来。 | 这是察看鬼神是怎么来的。 | This is to examine where the ghost or spirit came from. |
| 金鬼，其鬼必生前好勇，以性言之，其鬼亡于刀兵； | 金鬼，这个鬼生前一定好勇，就性情说，它死在刀兵之下； | A Metal ghost must have loved courage in life; by nature, that ghost died by blade and weapon; |
| 以成鬼神言之，其神所司何事，或水或火或土或金或木； | 就它成为鬼神说，这位神所掌管的是什么事，可能属水、属火、属土、属金、属木； | as to its becoming a ghost or spirit, what this spirit governs may be Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood; |
| 以所司属言之，如关岳神勇义属金，七煞丧煞，体金司煞属金也。 | 就所掌管的属性说，比如关帝、岳王的神勇和义属金，七煞、丧煞，体属金、掌管杀伐的也属金。 | as to what its charge belongs to, for example, the valour and righteousness of Guan and Yue belong to Metal, and the Seven Killings and the Mourning Killing, whose body is Metal and whose charge is killing, belong to Metal. |
| 遁甲七煞乃庚，是谓体金。 | 遁甲里的七煞就是庚，这就叫体属金。 | The Seven Killings of the Dunjia is Geng, which is what is meant by a body of Metal. |

> **按**:这一条讲的是 LY.ILL.YM.42 里「性、司、成」三样怎么用。「性」是生前的性情（好勇），「成」是怎么成鬼的（死于刀兵），「司」是成神以后上帝派它管什么（西方、秋令、兵事）；三样各自可以属金，也可以三样属的不一样（注里说「所司」可以属水火土金木任何一行）。「体金」是本体属金：七煞在遁甲里是庚，庚是金，所以七煞体金；关、岳靠神勇与义归金，是靠所司的性质。
>
> **Note:** This entry shows how the three of 'nature, charge and origin' in LY.ILL.YM.42 are used. 'Nature' is the disposition in life (love of courage), 'origin' is how it became a ghost (died by blade), and 'charge' is what the Supreme Lord set it to govern after it became a spirit (the west, autumn, military affairs); each of the three can be Metal, or the three can belong to different phases (the note says the 'charge' may belong to any of Water, Fire, Earth, Metal or Wood). 'Body of Metal' means the substance is Metal: the Seven Killings in the Dunjia is Geng, and Geng is Metal, so the Seven Killings has a body of Metal, while Guan and Yue go under Metal through the nature of their charge, valour and righteousness.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.48、LY.ILL.YM.50、LY.ILL.YM.42、LY.ILL.YM.44、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.50 水鬼的来历：生前谋利于水，或亡于江海风波汤卤，病丧于腰肾茶酒，凡司河泊舟楫，按水象考；出身显圣者亦可推而变化无穷

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前靠水谋利的，或者死在江海风浪、开水卤水之中，以及病死在腰、肾，因茶、酒而死的，凡是掌管河泊舟船的，就按水象来考察。注说：凡是靠出身、来历显了圣的，也可以这样推，变化就无穷无尽了；大慈在南海显道，三官从龙神那里受生，都仿照这样。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who sought profit by water in life, or died in river and sea, wind and waves, or scalding water and brine, or of illness of the waist and kidneys, or over tea and wine, and whose charge is rivers, moorings, boats and oars, is examined by the Water image. The note says: those who manifested as holy beings from their origins can likewise be worked out, and then the variations are without end; Great Compassion manifested the Way in the Southern Sea, and the Three Officials received their birth from the Dragon Spirit; follow this.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1280 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1280*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前谋利于水，或亡于江海风波汤卤之中，及病丧于腰、肾、茶、酒，凡司河泊舟楫，则水象而考也。 | 生前靠水谋利的，或者死在江海风浪、开水卤水之中，以及病死在腰、肾，因茶、酒而死的，凡是掌管河泊舟船的，就按水象来考察。 | One who sought profit by water in life, or died in river and sea, wind and waves, or scalding water and brine, or of illness of the waist and kidneys, or over tea and wine, and whose charge is rivers, moorings, boats and oars, is examined by the Water image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1281 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1281*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡出身显圣，亦可推之，则变化无穷矣。 | 凡是靠出身显了圣的，也可以这样推，变化就无穷无尽了。 | Those who manifested as holy beings from their origins can likewise be worked out, and then the variations are without end. |
| 大慈显道南海，三官生受龙神，仿此。 | 大慈在南海显道，三官从龙神那里受生，都仿照这样。 | Great Compassion manifested the Way in the Southern Sea, and the Three Officials received their birth from the Dragon Spirit; follow this. |

> **按**:「凡出身显圣」原文没有再解释，这里译成「凡是靠出身显了圣的」。「大慈」指大慈大悲的观世音；「三官生受龙神」的读法原文没有讲，这里按字面译。这一条和金的那一条（LY.ILL.YM.49）是平行的：性（谋利于水）、成（亡于江海风波）、司（河泊舟楫），最后一句提醒神的来历还有另一条路——靠出身显圣，那就没有一定之规了。
>
> **Note:** The original does not explain 'those who manifested as holy beings from their origins', and it is rendered as it stands. 'Great Compassion' is the Guanyin of great compassion and mercy; how 'the Three Officials received birth from the Dragon Spirit' is to be read is not stated, and it is translated literally. This entry runs parallel to the Metal one (LY.ILL.YM.49): nature (seeking profit by water), origin (dying in river and sea) and charge (rivers, moorings, boats); the last sentence warns that a spirit may also come by another road, manifesting from its origins, for which there is no fixed rule.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.49、LY.ILL.YM.51、LY.ILL.YM.45、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.51 木鬼的来历：生前陶情花乡、立业桑麻，或亡于笞杖树木之下、丧于捆缚疯癫，凡司栽植砍伐，按木象考；两可不能一见则审累重（南木神应水官者体木司水）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前在花乡里陶冶性情、靠桑麻立业的，或者死在笞杖、树木之下，死于捆绑、疯癫的，凡是掌管栽种砍伐的，就按木象来考察。注说：凡是两种都说得通、不能只取一种的，要审察哪种分量重，比如南木神对应水官的，是体属木、掌管水。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life took his pleasure in the land of flowers and made his living from mulberry and hemp, or died under the rod or beneath a tree, or by binding or madness, and whose charge is planting and felling, is examined by the Wood image. The note says: where either reading is possible and one cannot be settled on by a single look, one must weigh which carries more; for example, the Southern Wood God answering a Water Pressure line has a body of Wood and a charge of Water.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1283–1284 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1283–1284*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前陶情花乡，立业桑麻，或亡于笞杖树木之下，丧于捆缚、疯癫，凡司栽植砍伐，则木象而考也。 | 生前在花乡里陶冶性情、靠桑麻立业的，或者死在笞杖、树木之下，死于捆绑、疯癫的，凡是掌管栽种砍伐的，就按木象来考察。 | One who in life took his pleasure in the land of flowers and made his living from mulberry and hemp, or died under the rod or beneath a tree, or by binding or madness, and whose charge is planting and felling, is examined by the Wood image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1285 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1285*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡两可不能一见，当审累重，如南木神应水官者，体木司水也。 | 凡是两种都说得通、不能只取一种的，应当审察哪种分量重，比如南木神对应水官的，是体属木、掌管水。 | Where either reading is possible and one cannot be settled on by a single look, one must weigh which carries more; for example, the Southern Wood God answering a Water Pressure line has a body of Wood and a charge of Water. |

> **按**:这一条的注比正文重要：它给了一条判别规则，当一位神的「体」（本身属哪一行）和它的「所司」（掌管的属哪一行）不是同一行，不能只取一个，要看哪个分量重；例是南木神，本身是木神，可是掌管水，所以官鬼是水时要按「体木司水」去读。这是对 LY.ILL.YM.49 里「性、司、成」三样可以各属一行的补充。
>
> **Note:** The note of this entry matters more than the text: it gives a rule of discrimination, that when a spirit's 'body' (which phase it is itself) and its 'charge' (which phase what it governs is) are not the same phase, one cannot take only one, and must weigh which carries more; the example is the Southern Wood God, a Wood spirit by nature yet governing water, so that when the Pressure line is Water it is read as 'body Wood, charge Water'. It supplements what LY.ILL.YM.49 says, that nature, charge and origin may each belong to a different phase.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.50、LY.ILL.YM.52、LY.ILL.YM.46、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.52 火鬼的来历：生为炉煅之事，死于烈焰疮痍血衂胎产，凡司鼎釜孕育，按火象考；宜推而广之

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前做炉锻的事，死在烈火、疮痍、流血、胎产中的，凡是掌管鼎釜和孕育的，就按火象来考察。注说：应当推广开来，用五行、六神、诸星去配。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life worked at furnace and forge, or died in raging flame, sores and wounds, bleeding, or childbirth, and whose charge is cauldrons and pots and pregnancy, is examined by the Fire image. The note says: one should extend it further, matching it with the five phases, the six spirits and the various stars.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1286 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1286*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生为炉煅之事，死于烈焰疮痍，血衂胎产，凡司鼎釜孕育，则火象而考也。 | 生前做炉锻的事，死在烈火、疮痍、流血、胎产中的，凡是掌管鼎釜和孕育的，就按火象来考察。 | One who in life worked at furnace and forge, or died in raging flame, sores and wounds, bleeding, or childbirth, and whose charge is cauldrons and pots and pregnancy, is examined by the Fire image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1287 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1287*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 宜推而广之，五行六神诸星配之。 | 应当推广开来，用五行、六神、诸星去配。 | One should extend it further, matching it with the five phases, the six spirits and the various stars. |

> **按**:「血衂」的「衂」同「衄」，流血、鼻出血；这里译作流血。这一条和 LY.ILL.YM.49–LY.ILL.YM.51、YM.53 是平行的，只是火的注只有一句「宜推而广之」，没有像木那条一样给出判别规则。
>
> **Note:** 'Xue nu' (bleeding) uses a variant of the character for nosebleed and is rendered as bleeding. This entry runs parallel to LY.ILL.YM.49 to YM.51 and YM.53, except that the note on Fire has only the one sentence 'one should extend it further', and gives no rule of discrimination as the one on Wood does.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.51、LY.ILL.YM.53、LY.ILL.YM.47、LY.ILL.HJC.97、LY.ILL.YY.28

## LY.ILL.YM.53 土鬼的来历：生前土技农耕，死于瘟疫脾胃土石之难，凡司动作，按土象考；惟灵显东南

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:生前做土工、务农耕作的，死在瘟疫、脾胃的病、土石之难中的，凡是掌管动土动作的，就按土象来考察。注说：只是灵验多显现在东南。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** One who in life had an earth craft or farmed, or died of epidemic, illness of the spleen and stomach, or the calamity of earth and stone, and whose charge is works and movement, is examined by the Earth image. The note says: only, its power is manifest chiefly in the southeast.

**《易冒·鬼神章第五十七》原文** — 《易冒》第 1288 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 57: Ghosts and Spirits", base text; Yi Mao, line 1288*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 生前土技农耕，死于瘟疫脾胃土石之难，凡司动作，则土象而考也。 | 生前做土工、务农耕作的，死在瘟疫、脾胃的病、土石之难中的，凡是掌管动土动作的，就按土象来考察。 | One who in life had an earth craft or farmed, or died of epidemic, illness of the spleen and stomach, or the calamity of earth and stone, and whose charge is works and movement, is examined by the Earth image. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1289 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1289*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 惟灵显东南。 | 只是灵验多显现在东南。 | Only, its power is manifest chiefly in the southeast. |

> **按**:「惟灵显东南」承接土神那条（LY.ILL.YM.48）：东南土薄，土曹的名号多，土鬼的灵验也多显在东南。这一条和 LY.ILL.YM.49–LY.ILL.YM.52 合起来，是五行各自的「性、成、司」的一张表。
>
> **Note:** 'Only its power is manifest in the southeast' follows the entry on Earth spirits (LY.ILL.YM.48): in the southeast the earth is thin and the bureau titles are many, and the power of an Earth ghost is chiefly manifest there too. Together with LY.ILL.YM.49 to YM.52 this makes a table of the 'nature, origin and charge' of each of the five phases.

相关:LY.ILL.YM.52、LY.ILL.YM.54、LY.ILL.YM.48、LY.ILL.HJC.98、LY.ILL.YY.28
