## LY.ILL.YY.30 鬼崇·是谁的鬼：父母伏鬼化鬼阳父阴母、兄弟阳兄弟阴姐妹、妻财阳妻奴阴妾婢、子孙阳男阴女；鬼在本宫内卦同姓、他宫外卦异姓；交重大鬼、单拆小鬼；衰墓老年旧鬼、生旺壮年新鬼、胎养孩童鬼；乾宫祖宗与父、坤宫祖妣与母、震坎艮男、巽离兑女；几个鬼按一水二火三木四金五土之数，旺相加倍、休如数、囚死减半

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看是谁的鬼：父母爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是父亲，阴爻是母亲；兄弟爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是兄弟，阴爻是姐妹；妻财爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是妻子和奴仆，阴爻是妾和婢女；子孙爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。鬼在本宫内卦的，是同姓的鬼；在别的宫、外卦的，是异姓的鬼。发动的是大鬼，不动的是小鬼；衰弱、入墓的是年老的旧鬼，生旺的是壮年的新鬼，胎、养是孩童的鬼（阳男阴女）。按八卦：乾宫的鬼是祖宗和父亲，坤宫的鬼是祖母和母亲，震、坎、艮宫的鬼是男的，巽、离、兑宫的鬼是女的。问有几个鬼，按一水、二火、三木、四金、五土的数来推，旺相的加倍，休的照原数，囚、死的减半。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whose ghost it is: Resource with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost — yang is the father, yin the mother; Peer with a ghost hidden beneath or changing into one — yang brothers, yin sisters; Wealth with a ghost — yang a wife or manservant, yin a concubine or maid; Output with a ghost — yang male, yin female. A ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace is a ghost of the same surname, and one in the outer trigram of another palace a ghost of another surname. A moving ghost is a great ghost and a still one a small ghost; weak or in a tomb, an old ghost of long standing; born or strong, a ghost of the prime of life, newly dead; at Womb or Nurture, a child's ghost (yang a boy, yin a girl). By the eight trigrams: a ghost in the Qian palace is an ancestor or the father, in Kun a grandmother or the mother, in Zhen, Kan or Gen male, in Xun, Li or Dui female. To ask how many ghosts there are, work by the numbers Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five: if strong, double it; if resting, as given; if trapped or dead, halve it.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又父母伏鬼化鬼者，阳父阴母也。 | 又，父母爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是父亲，阴爻是母亲。 | Also Resource with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang is the father, yin the mother. |
| 兄弟伏鬼化鬼者，阳兄弟，阴姐妹也。 | 兄弟爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是兄弟，阴爻是姐妹。 | Peer with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, brothers; yin, sisters. |
| 妻财伏鬼化鬼者，阳妻奴阴妾婢也。 | 妻财爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是妻子和奴仆，阴爻是妾和婢女。 | Wealth with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, a wife or manservant; yin, a concubine or maid. |
| 子孙伏鬼化鬼者，阳男阴女也。 | 子孙爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。 | Output with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost: yang, male; yin, female. |
| 鬼在本宫内卦，同姓鬼也。 | 鬼在本宫的内卦，是同姓的鬼。 | A ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace: a ghost of the same surname. |
| 鬼在他宫外卦，异姓鬼也。 | 鬼在别的宫、外卦，是异姓的鬼。 | A ghost in the outer trigram of another palace: a ghost of another surname. |
| 交重，大鬼。 | 发动的，是大鬼。 | Moving: a great ghost. |
| 单拆，小鬼。 | 不动的，是小鬼。 | Still: a small ghost. |
| 衰墓，老年旧鬼。 | 衰弱、入墓的，是年老的旧鬼。 | Weak or in a tomb: an old ghost of long standing. |
| 生旺，壮年新鬼。 | 生旺的，是壮年的新鬼。 | Born or strong: a ghost of the prime of life, newly dead. |
| 胎养，孩童鬼，阳男阴女也。 | 胎、养，是孩童的鬼，阳爻是男孩，阴爻是女孩。 | At Womb or Nurture: a child's ghost, yang a boy, yin a girl. |
| 又八卦乾宫鬼，祖宗与父。 | 又按八卦：乾宫的鬼是祖宗和父亲。 | Again by the eight trigrams: a ghost in the Qian palace is an ancestor or the father. |
| 坤宫鬼，祖妣与母也。 | 坤宫的鬼是祖母和母亲。 | A ghost in the Kun palace is a grandmother or the mother. |
| 震坎艮宫鬼，男也。 | 震、坎、艮宫的鬼是男的。 | A ghost in the Zhen, Kan or Gen palace is male. |
| 巽离兑宫鬼，女也。 | 巽、离、兑宫的鬼是女的。 | A ghost in the Xun, Li or Dui palace is female. |
| 问有几鬼，以一水二火三木四金五土之数推之。 | 问有几个鬼，按一水、二火、三木、四金、五土的数来推。 | To ask how many ghosts there are, work by the numbers: Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five. |
| 旺相加倍，休如数，囚死减半也。 | 旺相的加倍，休的照原数，囚、死的减半。 | If strong, double it; if resting, as given; if trapped or dead, halve it. |

> **按**:「交重」是发动的爻，「单拆」是不动的爻（单是阳爻、拆是阴爻）。「祖妣」是祖母。「坎」是中男，「艮」是少男，「巽」是长女，「离」是中女，「兑」是少女，这几宫的鬼按男女分。黄金策的旧注和王洪绪也有按长生、帝旺、衰弱、胎养分年龄、按乾巽分方位的说法（LY.ILL.HJC.94），这里多了按六亲、按本宫他宫分同姓异姓、按卦宫分男女和按五行之数推几个鬼几条；卦身推是谁的鬼见 LY.ILL.HJC.102。
>
> **Note:** 'Jiaozhong' is a moving line and 'danchai' a still line (dan a yang line, chai a yin line). 'Zubi' is the grandmother. Kan is the middle son, Gen the youngest son, Xun the eldest daughter, Li the middle daughter and Dui the youngest daughter, and the ghosts of these palaces are divided by sex. The old commentary and Wang Hongxu on the Huangjin Ce also have the age by Birth, Peak, decline, Womb and Nurture and the direction by Qian and Xun (LY.ILL.HJC.94); this adds telling by relation which ghost it is, the same or a different surname by home palace or another, sex by trigram, and the number of ghosts by the number of the element; for telling whose ghost it is by the Body see LY.ILL.HJC.102.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.29、LY.ILL.YY.31、LY.ILL.HJC.94、LY.ILL.HJC.102、LY.ILL.HJC.104

---

## LY.ILL.YY.31 鬼崇·祭礼（曹子虚）：祈禳用何祭礼看鬼之食神；鬼属甲食在丙禄居巳宜炒鸡煎腐，乙食丁禄午宜干脯炒荳，丙食戊禄巳，丁食己禄午，戊食庚禄申宜三牲馒首果饼，己食辛禄酉宜鸡肉鱼馄饨，庚食壬禄亥宜猪首三牲，辛食癸禄子宜池鱼血羹麦面，壬食甲禄寅宜三牲时果新蔬，癸食乙禄卯宜鸡鹅鸭蔬果鸭蛋；送于何方以鬼爻支神定；子带龙雀素祭、带勾蛇虎武荤盘、伏化水鬼水父佛经、火鬼火父道醮、化财伏财福礼、化兄伏兄演戏众保

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:曹子虚说：祈禳用什么祭品礼数，要看鬼的食神。鬼属甲，食神在丙，禄在巳，用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒、礼、纸钱纸马送走；鬼属乙，食神在丁，禄在午，用干肉脯、炒豆、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属丙，食神在戊，禄在巳，同属甲的祭品；属丁，食神在己，禄在午，同属乙的祭品；属戊，食神在庚，禄在申，用三牲、馒头、果饼、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属己，食神在辛，禄在酉，用鸡肉、鱼、馄饨、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属庚，食神在壬，禄在亥，用猪头、三牲、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属辛，食神在癸，禄在子，用池里的鱼、血羹、麦面、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属壬，食神在甲，禄在寅，用三牲、时鲜的果子、新蔬菜、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属癸，食神在乙，禄在卯，用鸡、鹅、鸭、蔬果、鸭蛋、酒、礼、纸钱纸马。送到哪个方向，以鬼爻的地支来定。如果是神，祈祷的礼数把子孙当作福神：子孙带青龙、朱雀的，适合素祭；带勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武的，适合荤盘；子孙伏在水鬼或水父母下、或变出水鬼水父母的，适合念佛经；伏在火鬼或火父母下、或变出火鬼火父母的，适合道教的醮；子孙变出财、或伏在财下的，适合福礼；变出兄、或伏在兄下的，适合演戏、大家一起保祷。这是祈禳的占法。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Cao Zixu says: what sacrifice and rite to use for prayer and exorcism depends on the ghost's food-star. If the ghost belongs to Jia, the food-star is Bing and the emolument is at Si, and one sends it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses; Yi: food-star Ding, emolument at Wu, dried meat, fried beans, wine, gifts and paper money and horses; Bing: food-star Wu, emolument at Si, as for Jia; Ding: food-star Ji, emolument at Wu, as for Yi; Wu: food-star Geng, emolument at Shen, the three sacrificial animals, steamed buns, fruit cakes, wine and gifts; Ji: food-star Xin, emolument at You, chicken, fish, wonton, wine and gifts; Geng: food-star Ren, emolument at Hai, a pig's head, the three animals, wine and gifts; Xin: food-star Gui, emolument at Zi, pond fish, blood soup, wheat noodles, wine and gifts; Ren: food-star Jia, emolument at Yin, the three animals, seasonal fruit, fresh vegetables, wine and gifts; Gui: food-star Yi, emolument at Mao, chicken, goose, duck, vegetables and fruit, duck eggs, wine and gifts. The direction in which to send it is fixed by the branch of the Pressure line. If it is a spirit, in the rites of prayer Output is taken as the spirit of blessing: Output carrying the Dragon and the Bird, a vegetarian sacrifice; carrying the Hook, Snake, Tiger and Tortoise, a meat platter; hidden beneath a Water ghost or Water Resource, or changing into one, Buddhist scripture; beneath a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, or changing into one, a Daoist ritual; changing into or beneath Wealth, a blessing rite; changing into or beneath Peer, a staged play and communal protection. This is the divination of prayer and exorcism.

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3019*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 曹子虚曰，祈禳用何祭礼，则视鬼之食神也。 | 曹子虚说：祈禳用什么祭品礼数，就看鬼的食神。 | Cao Zixu says: what sacrifice and rite to use for prayer and exorcism depends on the ghost's food-star. |
| 如鬼属甲，则食在丙而禄居巳也。 | 比如鬼属甲，食神在丙，禄在巳。 | For example, if the ghost belongs to Jia, its food-star is Bing and its emolument is at Si. |
| 宜用炒鸡煎腐酒礼财马送之也。 | 适合用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | Send it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3019–3021 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, lines 3019–3021*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼属乙，则食在丁而禄居午也，宜用干脯炒荳酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属乙，食神在丁，禄在午，适合用干肉脯、炒豆子、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Yi, its food-star is Ding and its emolument is at Wu; send it off with dried meat, fried beans, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属丙，则食在戊而禄居巳也，宜用炒鸡煎腐酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属丙，食神在戊，禄在巳，适合用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Bing, its food-star is Wu and its emolument is at Si; send it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属丁，则食在己而禄居午也，宜用干脯炒荳酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属丁，食神在己，禄在午，适合用干肉脯、炒豆子、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ding, its food-star is Ji and its emolument is at Wu; send it off with dried meat, fried beans, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属戊，则食在庚而禄居申也，宜用三牲馒首果饼酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属戊，食神在庚，禄在申，适合用三牲、馒头、果饼、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Wu, its food-star is Geng and its emolument is at Shen; send it off with the three sacrificial animals, steamed buns, fruit cakes, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属己，则食在辛而禄居酉也，宜用鸡肉鱼馄饨酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属己，食神在辛，禄在酉，适合用鸡肉、鱼、馄饨、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ji, its food-star is Xin and its emolument is at You; send it off with chicken, fish, wonton, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属庚，则食在壬而禄居亥也，宜用猪首三牲酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属庚，食神在壬，禄在亥，适合用猪头、三牲、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Geng, its food-star is Ren and its emolument is at Hai; send it off with a pig's head, the three sacrificial animals, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属辛，则食在癸而禄居子也，宜用池鱼血羹麦面酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属辛，食神在癸，禄在子，适合用池里的鱼、血羹、麦面、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Xin, its food-star is Gui and its emolument is at Zi; send it off with pond fish, blood soup, wheat noodles, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属壬，则食在甲而禄居寅也，宜用三牲时果新蔬酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属壬，食神在甲，禄在寅，适合用三牲、时鲜果子、新蔬菜、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Ren, its food-star is Jia and its emolument is at Yin; send it off with the three sacrificial animals, seasonal fruit, fresh vegetables, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 鬼属癸，则食在乙而禄居卯也，宜用鸡鹅鸭蔬果鸭蛋酒礼财马送之也。 | 鬼属癸，食神在乙，禄在卯，适合用鸡、鹅、鸭、蔬菜水果、鸭蛋、酒和礼、纸钱纸马送走它。 | If the ghost belongs to Gui, its food-star is Yi and its emolument is at Mao; send it off with chicken, goose, duck, vegetables and fruit, duck eggs, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses. |
| 若夫送于何方，则以鬼爻之支神定之也。 | 至于送到哪个方向，就以鬼爻的地支来定。 | As for the direction in which to send it, it is fixed by the branch of the Pressure line. |
| 其在于神，则祈祷之礼，以子孙为福神也。 | 如果是神，祈祷的礼数，把子孙当作福神。 | If it is a spirit, in the rites of prayer Output is taken as the spirit of blessing. |
| 子带龙雀者，宜素祭。 | 子孙带青龙、朱雀的，适合素祭。 | If Output carries the Dragon and the Bird, a vegetarian sacrifice suits. |
| 子带勾蛇虎武者，宜荤盘。 | 子孙带勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武的，适合荤盘。 | If Output carries the Hook, Snake, Tiger and Tortoise, a meat platter suits. |
| 子伏水鬼水父下，或化水鬼水父者，宜佛经。 | 子孙伏在水鬼或水父母下，或者变出水鬼水父母的，适合念佛经。 | If Output is hidden beneath a Water ghost or Water Resource, or changes into a Water ghost or Water Resource, Buddhist scripture suits. |
| 子伏火鬼火父下，或化火鬼火父者，宜道醮。 | 子孙伏在火鬼或火父母下，或者变出火鬼火父母的，适合道教的醮。 | If Output is hidden beneath a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, or changes into a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, a Daoist ritual suits. |
| 子化财伏财者，宜福礼，子化兄伏兄者，宜演戏众保也。 | 子孙变出财、或伏在财下的，适合福礼；子孙变出兄、或伏在兄下的，适合演戏、大家一起保祷。 | If Output changes into Wealth or is hidden beneath Wealth, a blessing rite suits; if Output changes into Peer or is hidden beneath Peer, a staged play and communal protection suit. |
| 此祈禳之占也。 | 这是祈禳的占法。 | This is the divination of prayer and exorcism. |

> **按**:这一条在原书里跨了 3019、3021 两行。「食神」按字面是「鬼所属的天干所生的天干」（甲生丙、乙生丁……），「禄居某」是那个食神的禄位（丙禄在巳、丁禄在午、戊禄在巳、己禄在午、庚禄在申、辛禄在酉、壬禄在亥、癸禄在子、甲禄在寅、乙禄在卯）；书里没有另作解释，这里按上下文理解。「三牲」是牛、羊、猪（或猪、鸡、鱼），「财马」是纸钱纸马，「醮」是道教的法事。书里在这一段没有给出为什么某个天干配某种祭品。
>
> **Note:** In the original this entry runs across lines 3019 and 3021. The 'food-star' (shishen) is literally the stem generated by the stem the ghost belongs to (Jia generates Bing, Yi generates Ding ...), and 'emolument at' is that food-star's emolument seat (Bing at Si, Ding at Wu, Wu at Si, Ji at Wu, Geng at Shen, Xin at You, Ren at Hai, Gui at Zi, Jia at Yin, Yi at Mao); the book does not explain this separately and it is understood here by context. The 'three sacrificial animals' are ox, sheep and pig (or pig, chicken and fish), 'caima' is paper money and paper horses, and 'jiao' is a Daoist rite. The book gives no reason in this passage why a given stem is matched with a given offering.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.30、LY.ILL.YY.32

---

## LY.ILL.YY.32 鬼崇·旧愿：子孙伏鬼下动克世用、或伏岁杀下动伤世用，必有旧愿未还；旺相愿大、休囚愿小；问因何许愿以官鬼为用——鬼伏世下用下是自己许、伏父母财子下是为父母妻子许、伏鬼下是因病讼许、伏兄下是因兄弟朋友争斗赌博许；六亲化出鬼亦然

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:又有旧愿没还的：子孙伏在鬼下面、发动克世爻、用爻，或者子孙伏在岁煞下面、发动伤世爻、用爻的，一定是有旧愿没有还；旺相的愿大，休囚的愿小。问是因为什么许的愿，以官鬼为用：鬼伏在世爻下、用爻下的，是为自己许的愿；鬼伏在父母、财、子孙下的，是为父母、妻子、子女许的愿；鬼伏在鬼下的，是因为病、官司许的愿；鬼伏在兄下的，是为兄弟朋友、争斗赌博许的愿。六亲变出鬼的也是这样（出自《源髓诀》）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** There is also an old vow not yet paid: Output hidden beneath the ghost and moving to control the Self and Subject, or Output hidden beneath the Year-killing star and moving to harm the Self and Subject, means surely an old vow is not yet paid; if strong the vow is large, if resting or trapped small. To ask why the vow was made, the Pressure line is the Subject: a ghost hidden beneath the Self or the Subject line, a vow made for oneself; hidden beneath Resource, Wealth or Output, made for parents, wife or children; hidden beneath a ghost, made over illness or lawsuit; hidden beneath Peer, made over brothers, friends, brawls or gambling. The same holds when a relation changes out into a ghost (from the Yuan Sui Jue).

**《易隐·疾病占》原文** — 《易隐》第 3021 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining illness", base text; Yi Yin, line 3021*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又有旧愿未还者，但子孙伏鬼下动克世用，或子孙伏岁杀下动伤世用者，必有旧愿未还也。 | 又有旧愿没还的：子孙伏在鬼下面、发动克世爻、用爻，或者子孙伏在岁煞下面、发动伤世爻、用爻的，一定是有旧愿没有还。 | There is also an old vow not yet paid: Output hidden beneath the ghost and moving to control the Self and Subject, or Output hidden beneath the Year-killing star and moving to harm the Self and Subject: there is surely an old vow not yet paid. |
| 旺相愿大，休囚愿小也。 | 旺相的愿大，休囚的愿小。 | If strong, the vow is large; if resting or trapped, small. |
| 至问因何许愿，以官鬼为用也。 | 至于问是因为什么许的愿，以官鬼为用。 | As for asking why the vow was made, the Pressure line is the Subject. |
| 鬼伏世下用下者，因自己许也，鬼伏父母财子下，因父母妻子许，鬼伏鬼下因病讼许，鬼伏兄下因兄弟朋友争斗赌博许也。 | 鬼伏在世爻下、用爻下的，是为自己许的愿；鬼伏在父母、财、子孙下的，是为父母、妻子、子女许的愿；鬼伏在鬼下，是因为病、官司许的愿；鬼伏在兄爻下，是为兄弟朋友、争斗赌博许的愿。 | A ghost hidden beneath the Self or the Subject line: a vow made for oneself; hidden beneath Resource, Wealth or Output: made for parents, wife or children; hidden beneath a ghost: made over illness or lawsuit; hidden beneath Peer: made over brothers, friends, brawls or gambling. |
| 六亲化出鬼者亦然(出源髓诀)。 | 六亲变出鬼的也是这样（出自《源髓诀》）。 | The same holds when a relation changes out into a ghost (from the Yuan Sui Jue). |

> **按**:「岁杀」按字面是岁上的煞，书里没有再解释。「出源髓诀」的「源髓诀」是易隐引的书名，书里没有再介绍。这一条和黄金策讲的「持世则未酬旧愿，伏为有口无心」（LY.ILL.HJC.110）是同一类判断：都是从鬼爻的位置看是不是有旧愿未还；黄金策看鬼是不是持世、伏世下，这里另看子孙伏在鬼下。
>
> **Note:** 'The Year-killing star' (suisha) is literally a killing star of the year and the book does not explain further. The 'Yuan Sui Jue' is a work the Yi Yin cites and the book does not introduce. This entry is a judgment of the same kind as the Huangjin Ce's 'holding the Self: the old vow is not paid; hidden: a spoken word with no heart in it' (LY.ILL.HJC.110): both tell from the position of the Pressure line whether an old vow is unpaid; the Huangjin Ce looks at whether the ghost holds the Self or is hidden beneath it, and this looks in addition at Output hidden beneath the ghost.

相关:LY.ILL.YY.31、LY.ILL.YY.33、LY.ILL.HJC.109、LY.ILL.HJC.110

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.23 占鬼神看鬼爻的五行与旺衰：鬼休囚或空破墓绝都不是鬼神作祟；旺相是神、休囚是鬼——金是武神西方神/刀剑亡鬼，木是文神东方神/依草附木之妖或刑杖悬梁之鬼，水是河海北方神/水死之鬼，火是火神南方神雷公电母窖灶神/汤火焚烧之鬼，土是土神中央神山川社稷神/墙倒屋塌土死之鬼

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占鬼神，卦里的鬼要是休囚，或者空、破、墓、绝，都不是鬼神在作祟。鬼爻旺相是神，休囚衰弱是鬼：临金，旺相是武神和西方的神，衰弱是死于刀剑的鬼；临木，旺相是文神和东方的神，衰弱是依草附木的妖、或被杖责悬梁而死的鬼；临水，旺相是河海和北方的神，衰弱是死在池井江湖里的鬼；临火，旺相是火神和南方的神、雷公电母、窖灶的神，衰弱是被汤火烧死的鬼；临土，旺相是土神和中央的神、掌管山川社稷的神，衰弱是墙倒屋塌压死的鬼。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining ghosts and spirits, if the Pressure line in the hexagram is resting or trapped, or Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, it is not the harm of ghosts or spirits. A strong Pressure line is a god and a weak one a ghost: on Metal, if strong a martial god and a god of the west, if weak a ghost that died by sword or blade; on Wood, if strong a civil god and a god of the east, if weak a goblin clinging to grass and trees or a ghost that died by the rod or by hanging from a beam; on Water, if strong a god of river and sea and of the north, if weak a ghost that drowned in a pond, well, river or lake; on Fire, if strong the god of fire and a god of the south, the Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning, the gods of the cellar and the stove, if weak a ghost that died by scalding or burning; on Earth, if strong the god of the soil and a god of the centre and a god in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of the land and grain, if weak a ghost that died buried under a fallen wall or collapsed house.

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13199–13203 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 13199–13203*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 觉子曰：凡占鬼神，卦中鬼值休囚，及空破墓绝，皆非鬼神之害也。 | 觉子说：凡是占鬼神，卦里的鬼落在休囚上，以及空、破、墓、绝的，都不是鬼神在作祟。 | Juezi says: in all divinations about ghosts and spirits, if the Pressure line in the hexagram is resting or trapped, or Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, it is not the harm of ghosts or spirits. |
| 鬼临金，旺相者，武神及西方之神； | 鬼临金，旺相的，是武神和西方的神； | Pressure on Metal, if strong: a martial god or a god of the west; |
| 衰弱休囚者，刀剑身亡之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是死于刀剑的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died by sword or blade. |
| 鬼临木，旺相者，文神及东方之神； | 鬼临木，旺相的，是文神和东方的神； | Pressure on Wood, if strong: a civil god or a god of the east; |
| 衰弱休囚者，依草附木之妖，或是刑杖悬梁之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是依草附木的妖，或是被杖责、悬梁而死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a goblin that clings to grass and trees, or a ghost that died by the rod or by hanging from a beam. |
| 鬼临水，旺相者，河海及北方之神； | 鬼临水，旺相的，是河海和北方的神； | Pressure on Water, if strong: a god of river and sea or of the north; |
| 衰弱休囚者，池井江湖水死之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是死在池、井、江、湖里的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that drowned in a pond, well, river or lake. |
| 鬼临火，旺相者，火神及南方之神，雷公电母，窖灶之神； | 鬼临火，旺相的，是火神和南方的神，雷公电母，窖灶的神； | Pressure on Fire, if strong: the god of fire or a god of the south, the Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning, the gods of the cellar and the stove; |
| 衰弱休囚者，汤火焚烧之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是被汤火烧死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died by scalding or burning. |
| 鬼临土，旺相者，土神及中央之神，及掌管山川社稷之神； | 鬼临土，旺相的，是土神和中央的神，以及掌管山川社稷的神； | Pressure on Earth, if strong: the god of the soil or a god of the centre, or a god in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of the land and grain; |
| 衰弱休囚者，墙倒屋塌土死之鬼。 | 衰弱休囚的，是墙倒屋塌、被土压死的鬼。 | if weak, resting or trapped: a ghost that died buried under a fallen wall or collapsed house. |

> **打架**:怎样认是哪一路神鬼：黄金策旧注（LY.ILL.HJC.50）按日辰的地支推（子是北斗或北阴，亥是溺死鬼），王洪绪按生合世爻或用爻的那一爻带的六神认；觉子这里按鬼爻自己的五行和旺衰认（旺相是神，休囚是鬼），又说鬼休囚或空破墓绝根本不是鬼神作祟。三家的办法各不相同。
>
> **Where they disagree:** How to tell which sort of spirit or ghost it is: the old commentary of the Huangjin Ce (LY.ILL.HJC.50) reasons from the branch of the Day (Zi is the Northern Dipper or the North Yin, Hai a drowned ghost), and Wang Hongxu from the six spirits carried by the line that generates or combines with the Self or Subject line; Juezi here tells by the phase and strength of the Pressure line itself (strong is a god, resting or trapped a ghost), and adds that Pressure that is resting or trapped, Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct is not the harm of ghosts and spirits at all. The three ways all differ.

> **按**:「武神」是关公、岳飞一类的神；「文神」是文昌、孔子一类，书里没有列名单，译文按字面。「刑杖悬梁」是被杖打死、吊死。「雷公电母」是司雷电的神，「窖灶」是地窖和灶，「社稷」是土神和谷神。
>
> **Note:** 'Martial gods' are gods such as Guan Yu and Yue Fei, and 'civil gods' are of the kind of Wenchang and Confucius; the book gives no list, and the translation follows the letter. 'The rod or hanging from a beam' is being beaten to death or hanged. The 'Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning' are the gods of thunder and lightning, 'cellar and stove' are the underground store and the hearth, and 'the altars of the land and grain' are the gods of soil and grain.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.24、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.24 不必按五行硬指哪路神：金官指为关公岳公等武神不对，武神有封有未封各地不同；须问病人许愿未酬或污秽了哪座庙，把所疑的神占一卦，鬼爻旺或动就是此神，不上卦再占别处；得罪正神用香花纸马祭，招惹邪祟用浆纸钱夜静时送（金鬼向西、水鬼向北）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:古时候按五行分是哪路神，比如金官就说是天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公这些武神，觉子认为不对：武神有朝廷敕封的、有没有封的，各地风俗又不同，书里没记的更多，不可能一座庙一座庙地祭。该问病人是不是在哪座武神庙里许了愿没有还、或者在哪座庙里弄脏糟蹋了；凡是心里怀疑哪位神，就指着那位神占一卦，鬼爻旺或发动就是这位神，不上卦再占别处的神。鬼临木火水土的，都这样办。得罪了正神的，用香花纸马祭；招惹了邪祟的鬼，用浆水和纸钱，夜深人静时送走，金鬼向西送，水鬼向北送。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In ancient times the gods were told apart by the five phases, for example a Metal Pressure was said to be a heavenly general, Guan Gong, a Vajra, Wu Gong, Yue Gong and the other martial gods; Juezi thinks this wrong: martial gods include those enfeoffed by imperial decree and those not, local customs differ, and those not recorded in books are still more, so one cannot sacrifice at temple after temple. One should ask the patient whether he vowed something in the temple of some martial god and has not paid it, or defiled and abused some temple; whatever god he suspects in his heart, point to that god and divine, and if the Pressure line is strong or moving it is that god, and if it is not in the hexagram divine the god of some other place. Pressure on Wood, Fire, Water or Earth is treated in the same way. If he has offended an orthodox god, sacrifice with incense, flowers and paper horses; if he has provoked an evil haunting ghost, use rice-water and paper money and send it off in the stillness of the night, a Metal ghost to the west and a Water ghost to the north.

**觉子(李文辉,《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13204 行 · *Juezi (Li Wenhui, Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13204*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 按，古以五行而分其神，如金官，谓之天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公，诸武神，余以为非也。 | 按：古时候按五行来分是哪路神，比如金官就说是天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公这些武神，我认为不对。 | Note: in ancient times the gods were told apart by the five phases, for example a Metal Pressure was said to be a heavenly general, Guan Gong, a Vajra, Wu Gong, Yue Gong and the other martial gods; I think this is wrong. |
| 夫武神，有敕封者，有未封者，有各方土俗之不同，书之未载者更多，岂能庙庙而祭之？ | 武神有朝廷敕封的，有没有封的，各地风俗又不一样，书里没有记载的更多，哪里能一座庙一座庙地祭过去？ | Martial gods include those enfeoffed by imperial decree and those not, and local customs differ from place to place, and those not recorded in books are still more; how could one sacrifice at temple after temple? |
| 然则奈何？ | 那么该怎么办呢？ | Then what is to be done? |
| 须问病人，或于某武神庙中许愿未酬乎？ | 要问病人，是不是在某位武神的庙里许过愿、还没有还？ | One must ask the patient whether he vowed something in the temple of some martial god and has not yet paid it. |
| 或于何庙污秽作践乎？ | 或者是不是在哪座庙里弄脏了、糟蹋了？ | Or whether he defiled or abused some temple. |
| 凡心有所疑者，则指其神而占之。 | 凡是心里有怀疑的，就指着那位神来占。 | Whatever god he suspects in his heart, point to that god and divine. |
| 鬼爻或旺或动，是此神也； | 鬼爻旺相或者发动，就是这位神； | If the Pressure line is strong or moving, it is that god; |
| 如不上卦，再占他处之神可耳。 | 如果不上卦，再占别处的神就行。 | if it is not in the hexagram, divine the god of some other place. |
| 其鬼值木火水土者，皆同此法。 | 鬼临木、火、水、土的，都照这个办法。 | Pressure on Wood, Fire, Water or Earth is treated in the same way. |
| 如既得罪于正神者，以香花纸马祭之； | 如果是得罪了正神，用香花纸马来祭； | If he has offended an orthodox god, sacrifice with incense, flowers and paper horses; |
| 勾惹邪祟之鬼者，用浆纸钱，夜静之时，金鬼者向西送之，水鬼者向北送之。 | 如果是招惹了邪祟的鬼，用浆水和纸钱，夜深人静的时候送走：金鬼向西送，水鬼向北送。 | if he has provoked an evil haunting ghost, use rice-water and paper money, and send it off in the stillness of the night: a Metal ghost to the west, a Water ghost to the north. |
| 余仿此。 | 其余照这个类推。 | The rest follow this. |

> **按**:觉子上一条（LY.ILL.ZS.23）先按五行分了大类，这一条说不必再把大类落实到某位具体的神：古人把金官指为关公岳飞等，他不同意，主张问病人心里疑的是哪位神，再占一卦验证。「金官」按字面是金爻的官鬼。「浆纸钱」是米浆水和纸钱。「香花纸马」是香、花和纸扎的马，祭正神用。
>
> **Note:** In the previous entry (LY.ILL.ZS.23) Juezi first told the great kinds apart by the five phases; this entry says the great kinds need not be pinned down to a particular god: he disagrees with the ancients pointing a Metal Pressure to Guan Gong, Yue Fei and the like, and holds that one should ask which god the patient suspects in his heart and then divine to test it. A 'Metal official' is in the letter a Metal Pressure line. 'Rice-water and paper money' is rice-water and paper money. 'Incense, flowers and paper horses' are incense, flowers and paper-made horses, used to sacrifice to orthodox gods.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.25

---

## LY.ILL.ZS.25 祭送不是万灵药：时灾瘟癖照法祭送屡验，疯痨气蛊是自己失调、与鬼神无关；野鹤见滇黔蜀粤病不服药专信鬼神、杀牲救命反害命；李我平驳《易冒》《易林补遗》指定关岳、说不祭降灾是邪神，并说此书疾病章言鬼神是从俗

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:祭送鬼神，对时行灾病、瘟疫、癖积一类的病有用（野鹤多次验过）；疯、痨、气、蛊是自己没有调养好、酒色伤了身，和鬼神没有关系，祭送没有益处，该做的是节制饮食、远离色欲、平息怒气、养护精神。有病不吃药、专靠杀牲祭神的，反而害了性命。李我平批评有的书指定哪位神（关帝、岳王、关圣），又说祭了降福、不祭降灾，那是作威作福的邪神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Sacrificing and sending off the ghosts and spirits is of use for seasonal calamities, epidemics and hard lumps (the Old Man of Yehe verified it many times); madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly come from failing to look after oneself and from wine and lust damaging the body, have nothing to do with ghosts and spirits, and gain nothing from sacrifice, what is to be done being to moderate food, keep away from lust, calm anger and nourish the spirit. Those who take no medicine when ill and rely wholly on slaughtering animals to the gods only harm life. Li Woping criticises books that name a particular god (Emperor Guan, King Yue, the Sage Guan) and say that those who sacrifice are blessed and those who do not are sent calamity, which he calls evil gods who lord it and dispense favour.

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 13205 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13205*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：凡得时灾瘟癖，照此法而祭送者，余屡验之。 | 野鹤说：凡是得的是时行灾病、瘟疫、癖积一类的，照这个办法祭送，我多次验过。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: for illnesses that are seasonal calamities, epidemics or hard lumps, sacrifices and sending-off by this method have proved true many times. |
| 倘若是疯痨气蛊，既失调于前，酒色伤身，又失慎重于后，与鬼神何干？ | 如果是疯病、痨病、气病、蛊胀，先前没有调养好，酒色伤了身，后来又不谨慎，和鬼神有什么关系？ | But if it is madness, consumption, qi disorder or drum-belly, when he first failed to look after himself and wine and lust damaged his body, and then was again not careful, what has this to do with ghosts and spirits? |
| 祭之送之皆无益矣。 | 祭他、送他都没有用。 | Sacrificing to them and sending them off is of no use. |
| 惟节饱食、远色欲、息气养神，其病自减。 | 只有节制饮食、远离色欲、平息怒气、养护精神，病自然会减轻。 | Only by moderating food, keeping away from lust, calming anger and nourishing the spirit does the illness ease of itself. |
| 余游遍江湖，曾至滇黔蜀粤，见彼病不服药，专信鬼神。 | 我走遍江湖，到过云南、贵州、四川、广东，看到那里的人生病不吃药，专门相信鬼神。 | I have travelled all over the rivers and lakes, to Yunnan, Guizhou, Sichuan and Guangdong, and seen how there the sick take no medicine and put their whole trust in ghosts and spirits. |
| 先用三牲五牲，后至杀牛宰马，一次不愈，甚至二三十次。 | 先用三牲、五牲，后来杀牛宰马，一次不好，甚至做二三十次。 | First they use three or five kinds of sacrificial animals, then they slaughter oxen and horses, and if once does not cure, they go on even twenty or thirty times. |
| 因病致穷者多矣。 | 因为生病弄穷的人很多。 | Many are made poor by illness. |
| 殊不知害彼命而救命，其罪愈彰。 | 殊不知伤害别的生命来救自己的命，罪过更加明显。 | They do not realise that to harm other lives in order to save a life makes the sin all the plainer. |
| 所以屡见害性命之多者，终不能救而死，可不戒与？ | 所以屡次看到伤害的性命越多的人，最后还是救不了而死，难道不该引以为戒吗？ | So I have often seen that those who harmed the most lives could still not be saved and died in the end; should one not take warning? |
| 今下路读书人亦信此邪神者，余实不解。 | 现在下游一带的读书人也信这种邪神，我实在不明白。 | Now even scholars of the lower reaches believe in such evil gods, which I truly do not understand. |
| 神农尝百草，遗救生灵。 | 神农尝百草，是留给后世来救活生灵的。 | Shennong tasted the hundred herbs and left them to save living beings. |
| 未闻有何圣贤，教人宰牲命以救命也。 | 没听说有哪位圣贤，教人杀牲畜的命来救人的命。 | I have never heard of any sage who taught men to slaughter animals' lives in order to save a life. |

**李我平(楚江李坦,《增删卜易》的鉴定者)** — 《增删卜易》第 13207 行 · *Li Woping (Li Tan of Chujiang, who appraised the Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 13207*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 李我平曰：《易冒》以鬼临金者为关帝、岳王。 | 李我平说：《易冒》把鬼临金的当作关帝、岳王。 | Li Woping says: the Yi Mao takes Pressure on Metal as Emperor Guan and King Yue. |
| 《易林补遗》又以青龙为关圣，却不知关岳以前，金官与青龙是何神也？ | 《易林补遗》又把青龙当作关圣，却不知道在关羽、岳飞以前，金官和青龙是什么神？ | The Yilin Buyi in turn takes the Azure Dragon as the Sage Guan, but does not ask what gods the Metal Pressure and the Azure Dragon were before Guan and Yue. |
| 又曰：祭者降之以福，不祭者降之以灾。 | 又说：祭的就降福给他，不祭的就降灾给他。 | It also says: to those who sacrifice they send blessing, to those who do not they send calamity. |
| 若此，乃作威福之邪神也，而谓正直之神，何得有此？ | 如果是这样，那是作威作福的邪神，而说是正直的神，哪里会有这种事？ | If so, they are evil gods who lord it and dispense favour, and how could a god called upright do this? |
| 悖甚。 | 荒谬极了。 | Utterly absurd. |
| 此书通前彻后，无一不合于理，惟疾病章中，亦言鬼神之事，何也？ | 这部书从头到尾，没有一处不合道理，只有疾病章里也讲鬼神的事，为什么呢？ | This book, from beginning to end, is in accord with reason everywhere, and only its chapter on illness also speaks of ghosts and spirits; why? |
| 疑因今人问病，开口先问鬼神，不得不从俗耳，知者察之。 | 我怀疑是因为现在的人问病，开口先问鬼神，不得不随俗罢了，明白的人自己去体察。 | I suspect it is because people now, in asking about illness, first ask about ghosts and spirits as soon as they open their mouths, so that the book has had to follow custom; let those who understand judge. |

> **打架**:要不要指定是哪位神：《易冒》以鬼临金为关帝、岳王，《易林补遗》以青龙为关圣（李我平点了这两部书）；李我平说这些说法不通，还说「祭者降福、不祭者降灾」是作威作福的邪神。野鹤对祭送的态度是：时灾瘟癖照法祭送灵验，疯痨气蛊与鬼神无关。
>
> **Where they disagree:** Whether to name a particular god: the Yi Mao takes Pressure on Metal as Emperor Guan and King Yue, and the Yilin Buyi takes the Azure Dragon as the Sage Guan (Li Woping names the two books); Li Woping says this does not hold, and that 'blessing to those who sacrifice, calamity to those who do not' would make evil gods who lord it and dispense favour. The Old Man's attitude to sacrifice is that for seasonal calamities and epidemics sacrifice by the method proves true, and that madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly have nothing to do with ghosts and spirits.

> **按**:「时灾瘟癖」是当令的灾病、瘟疫和癖积（腹内结块的病）；「疯痨气蛊」是疯病、痨病（肺结核一类）、气病和蛊胀。「三牲」是牛、羊、猪，「五牲」是再加鸡、犬之类。「下路」在这里指长江下游一带。「乾按」是今人加的话，本库不引。
>
> **Note:** 'Seasonal calamity, epidemic and hard lump' are the calamities in season, pestilence and lumps forming in the belly; 'madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly' are madness, consumption (an illness of the lungs) and disorders of qi and of belly distension. 'Three sacrificial animals' are the ox, sheep and pig and 'five' add the fowl, dog and the like. 'Lower reaches' here means the lower Yangtze. The 'Qian note' is a modern addition and is not quoted here.

相关:LY.ILL.HJC.50、LY.ILL.ZS.23、LY.ILL.ZS.24

---

## LY.ILL.ZSZ.07 八卦断例·占病逢巽、旅、临：金神为祟、病者祷禳、病犯祟侵

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:周易尚占八卦断例里有三个卦的断辞把病和鬼神联在一起：巽卦「惟占病人，金神为崇」，占病人的时候是金神（白虎）在作祟；旅卦「病者祷禳」，病人要祷告、禳解；临卦「病犯崇侵」，病是撞上了鬼神作祟的侵扰。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan three hexagrams link illness with spirits: Xun, 'only in divining a sick person: a Metal spirit (the White Tiger) is the curse'; Lü (the Traveller), 'the sick should pray and perform exorcism'; Lin, 'illness meets the encroachment of a curse'.

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 1114 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of Judgments by Example for the Eight Palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 1114*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 巽者顺也 | 巽，就是顺。 | Xun (Wind) means compliance. |
| 顺时行事 | 顺着时势行事。 | Act in accord with the time. |
| 利财利婚 | 利于财、利于婚。 | Favourable to wealth and to marriage. |
| 利官利贵 | 利于官、利于贵人。 | Favourable to office and to nobles. |
| 身泰讼和 | 自身安泰，官司和解。 | The person is at ease and a lawsuit is settled. |
| 田收蚕遂 | 田有收成，蚕如愿。 | Fields give harvest and silkworms thrive. |
| 惟占病人 | 只有占病人， | Only in divining a sick person: |
| 金神为崇 | 是金神作祟。 | a Metal spirit is the curse. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 1174 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of Judgments by Example for the Eight Palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 1174*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旅者羁也 | 旅，就是羁旅在外。 | Lü (the Traveller) means sojourning. |
| 在旅之象 | 是在旅途的象。 | It is the image of being on a journey. |
| 不利守常 | 不利于守常。 | Keeping to the usual is unfavourable. |
| 运谋为上 | 运作谋划最好。 | Working and scheming is best. |
| 讼散婚成 | 官司散去，婚事成。 | The lawsuit disperses and marriage is made. |
| 孕生狱放 | 怀孕得生，囚犯得放。 | Pregnancy comes to birth and prisoners are freed. |
| 病者祷禳 | 病人要祷告、禳解。 | The sick should pray and perform exorcism. |
| 行人休望 | 在外的人不要指望（归来）。 | Do not look for the traveller. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 1234 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of Judgments by Example for the Eight Palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 1234*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 临者大也 | 临，就是大。 | Lin (Approach) means great. |
| 克己临人 | 克制自己，面对别人。 | Master oneself to face others. |
| 宜占家宅 | 适合占家宅。 | Suits divining the household. |
| 利问婚姻 | 利于问婚姻。 | Favourable to asking about marriage. |
| 财官并吉 | 财、官都吉。 | Wealth and office are both auspicious. |
| 谋望同伦 | 谋划指望，与同辈同步。 | Plans and hopes are in step with one's peers. |
| 孕生男子 | 怀孕，生男孩。 | Pregnancy: a boy is born. |
| 病犯崇侵 | 病，遇到鬼神作祟的侵扰。 | Illness meets the encroachment of a curse. |

> **按**:巽、临两处原文写的是「崇」（崇高的崇），按上下文是作祟的「祟」，译文按「祟」（鬼神降灾）译，书里没有标注。「金神」按这一部前面「十一、定六神」的「白虎金神」（第 461 行）是白虎，这里「金神为崇」是白虎作祟；别的书里金神常和七煞连称（LY.ILL.EL.34、EL.47），那是另一个意思，这里没有用。「祷禳」是祈祷、禳解（用仪式把灾禳除）。「病犯崇侵」读作病是触犯了、遇到了鬼神作祟的侵扰。三行里别的词组照录。
>
> **Note:** In Xun and Lin the original writes the character for 'lofty' where the context calls for the character for 'curse'; the translation reads it as 'curse' (harm sent by a spirit), and the book gives no mark. By 'Section 11: Fixing the Six Spirits' earlier in the same part ('the White Tiger is the Metal spirit', line 461) the 'Metal spirit' is the White Tiger, so 'a Metal spirit is the curse' here is the White Tiger afflicting; in other books the Golden Spirit is often named together with the Seven Killings (LY.ILL.EL.34, EL.47), which is another sense and is not used here. 'Pray and perform exorcism' is to pray and to dispel the calamity by rites. 'Illness meets the encroachment of a curse' reads the illness as having offended or run into the invasion of a curse sent by a spirit. The other phrases of the three lines are kept as they stand.

相关:LY.ILL.ZSZ.05、LY.ILL.EL.34、LY.ILL.EL.47、LY.ILL.TXF.11、LY.ILL.BSYG.05、LY.ILL.DYTJ.38
